     1	# Next release: 0.1.129
     2	
     3	**Released 2026-10-16:** 0.1.128 (build 132, tag `v0.1.128`) is the latest release; record `docs/validation/Bello-Agent-0.1.128-2026-10-16.md`.
     4	
     5	## 0.1.129
     6	
     7	- [x] Deleting a chat deletes its whole family (`dev/a129-family-delete`, owner decisions 2026-10-16): the chat, its saved sides at every depth (including unlisted ones) and their sub-agents go to Trash under one durable intent, listed by name on a Pi confirmation sheet. Deleting a side deletes its descendants and keeps its spend counted in main. A missing or removed project no longer blocks deletion; only the app's own chat data is removed and a skipped side-cost repair is named. Earlier orphan sides are untouched. [Validation](docs/validation/family-delete-0.1.129.md).
     8

   137	## 0.1.126 decisions made while the owner was away (2026-10-13 night)
   138	
   139	- RL-04 (capture lost at graceful shutdown): fixed with a bounded drain. The helper keeps delivering, and the app keeps acknowledging, queued capture packets until the outbox is empty or the existing shutdown deadline arrives. A drain cut short is marked partial. A durable spool-and-replay design was built and dropped: two review rounds found data-boundary bugs in it, including cleared chats' bodies surviving on disk.
   140	- SCROLL-11 (measuring every line of an expanded long diff before first paint): not fixed. Deferring the measurement produced reading-position edge cases in five review rounds, so it was reverted to the synchronous measurement. The cost is one-time per expansion; it is left as a known item.
   141	- UI-05 (send during image paste): Send waits while an image is importing. The button is disabled and a notice says "Adding image…". This replaces a deferred auto-send, which kept producing races in review (navigation, eviction, draft reload, deletion).

   172	## 0.1.123 (owner 2026-10-11)
   173	
   174	The Rust port is stopped (`rust` branch, `rust/docs/STATUS-2026-10-10.md`). Bring back into Swift what the port found (owner: "work on all of them"); no release without the owner's OK.
   175	
   176	- [x] Chats missing from the sidebar: `ChatRecord` rows without `toolMode`/`imported` fail to decode and are skipped (`Storage/MetadataStore.swift`). Done on `dev/chat-records-mcp`: defaulted keys decode as their defaults in `ChatRecord`, `TopicRecord` and `SessionReadState`; required keys stay required; encoding unchanged.
   177	- [x] MCP outcome marker cleared before the result is journaled (`PiAgentCore/MCP.swift`). Done on `dev/chat-records-mcp`: the marker is cleared only after the batch's result rows are appended and synced (one fsync per batch with an MCP call); a failed write or sync leaves the outcome unknown. Follow-up on `dev/mcp`: Inspector calls retain by command/host identity until the app receives the whole reply, saves and syncs it under the project's `MCPResults`, then acknowledges it to the same helper. Failed saves/reads keep the outcome unknown; a closed manager's late retention callback cannot remove the reopened workspace's marker. The 15 affected core checks pass; reverting the Inspector/closed-manager guards fails all three new regressions. 24 core and 38 app checks pass (`MCPInvocationRetentionTests`, `MCPRemovalTests`, `HostSupervisorTests`, `ConfigurationVaultTests`).
   178	- [x] HTTP MCP ignored `timeoutSeconds` (`dev/mcp`): HTTP now gets the same 1–300 s clamp/default as stdio and an overall deadline per exchange, including setup/notifications. Expiry cancels the transport and keeps a dispatched call's unknown-outcome marker. Heartbeat/keep-alive loopback regressions (`HTTPMCPDeadlineTests`) pass.
   179	- [x] Terminal: Quit leaves SIGHUP-ignoring shells running (`Terminal/PseudoTerminal.swift`); held keys may show the accent popup instead of repeating. Done on `dev/terminal-fixes`: at Quit each shell and its group get SIGHUP, one shared wait of at most 50 ms, then SIGKILL (closing/restarting a tab keeps the 2 s grace; `nohup` jobs in their own group survive, as in Terminal.app); a held printable key on a plain layout repeats straight into the shell (input methods, dead keys and marked text unchanged); a terminal taken out of the window now reports focus-out to programs that asked. The three then unconfirmed were reproduced and fixed on `dev/launch-perf` (merge 2c397f6f): only a key pressed inside the terminal repeats (held Return after Restart no longer reaches the new shell); in a narrow pane with a badge the tab row gives way so New stays clear; ⌃` waits behind sheets, model sheets and popover pickers.
   180	- [x] A crash mid-reply may lose the streamed text — done (dev/streaming, merge dc4539ab). The loss was only when the helper itself dies (killed, crashed, power loss); an app crash already saved the reply. Deltas now go every 100 ms / 16 KiB to `<journal>.stream` (`PartialReplyLog.swift`); on reopen the cut-off reply becomes the same paused, interrupted row a Stop leaves. Failing-first acceptance test SIGKILLs the real helper; 9 `PartialReplyLogTests`; 7 Codex rounds. Helper streaming CPU 8.6% → 8.9% of a core.
   181	- [ ] Streaming CPU (~37% app, ~10% helper): measured, no change. Cost is a fixed ~7 ms per delta (CA commit/draw 13%, layout 7%, transcript 3%), not re-layout of the whole reply. Partial redraw cut the redrawn area 6× but CPU only 37.7 → 36.3% (noise), reverted. **Owner decision:** cap streamed UI updates at ~30/s (about half the app's cost, slightly less smooth). Also seen: `StreamingCostTests.testDeltaAccumulationIsConstantTimeInTheReplySoFar` heap check flaky on base (2/4); deleting a chat leaves its `.meta` behind.
   182	- [x] **Smooth scrolling in long chats** (owner, 2026-10-11, top priority): scrolling a long chat keeps loading bounded areas and "doesn't really work"; the whole chat should scroll as one continuous document (honest scrollbar, no load stalls or jumps). Owner tests by hand and gives feedback. Done on `dev/smooth-scroll` (merge 56d2308b). Cause: the document was only the ≤500 loaded rows, so the scroller covered just them (knob to middle of 400 turns showed turn 390; knob held at top took 14.2 s / 119 page reads; 2,000 turns not reached in 60 s). Now history pages carry their position and the chat's total (helper `SessionReads.swift`, `HistoryReader.swift`); the document keeps room above/below the loaded rows sized by unloaded rows × this chat's measured row height (`Transcript/TranscriptExtent.swift`, cap 8 M pt); edge pages fill their room without moving what's on screen; landing in unreached room (scroller drag, fling) reads the page at that row by position (journal or helper), newest request wins, failures retry, a changed chat offers Reload. After: top reached at once, middle shows ~turn 193, rows on screen 0–48 ms after the hand stops, 0 scroller resizes over 5% (was 34), frame times unchanged, 0 jumps. During a fast drag the reader sees blank room until the hand stops; the knob can drift slightly as real heights replace the estimate. Codex: 10 agent rounds plus 5 lead rounds, each finding fixed test-first (landing on undrawable legacy rows, trim/edge room, focus-only pins blocking jumps, `history_changed` refusals, width-dependent heights, remeasure in leading/trailing room, side/edit positions). Residual: a failed recovery read on an idle chat retries only on the next update; reveals still skip the journal check position reads do. Record: `docs/perf/smooth-scrolling-0.1.123.md`.
   183	- [x] Find deep in history: 81% was case-insensitive `range(of:)`; pure-ASCII records that cannot hold an ASCII query are skipped by a byte scan (same results, `ContentSearchTests`), decoding is parallel within 16 MiB. Typed → marked: 400 turns 1,171 → ~450 ms, 2,000 turns 5,082 → 1,337 ms.
   184	- [x] Gate and soak fixes: the first gate (7a60c8a7) found two parallel failures that repeated alone (`TranscriptRowUpdateTests` held-page refresh, `StreamingDeliveryCostTests.testRefreshRoundTripOccupancy`), caused by the position-recovery read sent between a snapshot loop's requests; fixed on `dev/scroll-fix` (bc8b3d3e). The whole-release Codex review from `v0.1.122` found two failure paths, fixed test-first and re-reviewed: a reply log that cannot be set aside is kept and restored, with branch membership read from the whole timeline (`dev/stream-fix`, bdabaad5); a journal cut short or gone under a position read offers Reload (missing at once, unreadable after 3 reads in a row) instead of retrying forever (`dev/scroll-fix2`, af1ca062).
   185	- [x] **Folded sides stay folded** (owner, 2026-10-12): starting a new side from a chat whose sides are folded in the sidebar no longer unfolds them. Done on `dev/side-fold` (merge 0a1d54ce): `openSide` sets `quietSidebarReveal` to the parent's lineage plus the new side, as a sidebar click does (`WorkspaceSides.swift`); search, find hits, the menu bar, `selectSide`/`showSide` still unfold. While the folded new side is focused no row is highlighted, as for a side opened from the sides panel. `SidesPanelTests.testANewSideLeavesItsChatsFoldedSideListFolded` (failed first, mutation-checked); Codex no findings.
   186	- [ ] Release both Bello Agent and Bello Box once the fixes and smooth scrolling are done (owner, 2026-10-11). Box 0.0.78 is on Box `main` (bf38b1c), waiting for the owner to run the notarized `sparkle-release.sh`.
   187	- [x] Launch: menu-bar item made after the launch window, its panel on first open (was 42–52 ms ahead of the window). Release warm first window 264 → 235 ms; settled 573 → 555 ms (noise); cold 1078 → 1038 ms. Measured with `scripts/perf-launch.sh` over synthetic data (own bundle id; `PI_LAUNCH_FIXTURE` test-only vault, never in release builds). Record: `docs/perf/launch-0.1.123.md`.
   188	- [ ] Later: launch to settled window (sidebar ~75 ms, reopened transcript ~150 ms left), CI for the Swift apps, perf harness and Swift golden corpora from the Rust oracles.
   189	
   190	
   191	
   192	**Scope (owner, 2026-10-08):**
   193	- [x] **Mark as Unread**: a chat can be marked unread from the sidebar's context menu and the File menu; it shows the unread dot until opened, and survives a relaunch. Done on `dev/sidebar`: row, side-row and marked-rows menus plus File ▸ Mark as Unread; it looks and counts like one unread reply, **including the Dock badge** (decided with Codex; a later run failure does not take it off); opening the chat (the reader focusing it, not launch reopening it) clears it, seeing a reply does not; persisted in the read state; not for archived chats, connection tests, background requests, unkept sides or unsent New chats. VoiceOver: "<title>, unread"; the dot says "Unread". Gallery `27-sidebar-marked-unread-*`.
   194	- [x] **Paused after restart**: a chat that was paused (stopped mid-run, or holding queued work) still shows as paused in the sidebar and in the chat after the app restarts. Done on `dev/sidebar`. Cause: the app's journal reader ignored a stop with nothing queued (`queuePaused` alone), so such a chat opened "Ready"; and nothing at launch knew any chat was paused, so every row read idle until opened. Fix: the reader keeps that run as paused; a small `run-hold` record per waiting chat (written as runs change, flushed after the quit's helper stops) shows on the row, the sides panel and the menu bar at once, and launch checks those chats' journal ends off the main thread (every chat's once, on the first launch of this build). Nothing is resumed. VoiceOver: "<title>, paused". Real-helper relaunch test; gallery `27a`/`27b`.
   195	- [x] **Sidebar sorted by last updated, always** (owner chose this over a switch): newest activity first; pinned chats stay on top; manual drag ordering is removed. Done on `dev/sidebar`: every group newest activity first (a message sent or queued, an edit or rewrite, a run starting, stopping, being cut off or finishing; never per token), pinned on top (by activity too), a family ranked by its newest member; manual order, drag-to-reorder and the insertion line removed (a drop on a row goes to its group; old records with a manual rank still load, rank ignored); Next/Previous Chat step through the chats in the sidebar's order (as held). Rows do not move under the reader: while the pointer is over the list, a row's menu is open or a row is dragged, a chat with activity keeps its place until that ends (or the app goes to the background).
   196	- [x] **Search inside threads**, both ways (owner chose both):
   197	  - [x] the sidebar search matches message text as well as titles, shows a snippet under each matching chat, and opening it goes to the matching message (`dev/search`: trigram FTS5 index `search-index.sqlite` beside the desktop database, `Storage/ChatSearchIndex.swift`, `Workspaces/SidebarSearch.swift`; opening goes through the one adapter `SidebarSearchReveal`, which `dev/scroll` points at `revealInTranscript` with the hit's excerpt marked, its folded turn opened);
   198	  - [x] ⌘F in an open chat shows a find bar that highlights matches in the transcript, with next/previous (`dev/scroll`: Pi find bar over the transcript; matches across the whole chat, loaded or not, read in and brought into view; ⌘G / ⇧⌘G, Return / Shift-Return, Escape; "Search and Copy Conversation…" moves to ⌥⌘F; `docs/transcript-reveal-api.md`).
   199	- [x] **Recently opened chats tinted** (owner, 2026-10-08): the open chat's sidebar row has the strongest tint, earlier-opened chats progressively less, fading to the normal grey after a few steps. Ranked by order of opening, not time; remembered across relaunch. Done on `dev/sidebar`: the open chat's row has the accent wash, the four opened before it ×0.75/0.5/0.3/0.15 of it, older ones plain (selection, marks and hover keep their own fills); ranked by order of opening (a parent passed through on the way to its side does not count), kept with the selection across relaunch; rows redraw only when the order of opening changes. Gallery `28-sidebar-recently-opened-*`.
   200	- [x] **Draft indicator** (owner, 2026-10-08): a chat with an unsent composer draft shows a small draft marker on its sidebar row; clears when sent or emptied; survives relaunch. Done on `dev/sidebar`: a pencil after the title (VoiceOver "has a draft") for a chat whose saved draft holds text, an image, a skill, a draft an edit set aside, or a changed queued rewrite; it follows the store's committed draft writes (behind the typing, never per keystroke; the sidebar is told only when a chat gains or loses its draft), comes back after a relaunch, and never shows for unkept sides or unsent New chats. Gallery `29-sidebar-row-states-*` shows it beside unread, Paused and the recency wash.
   201	- [x] **Smooth scrolling in very long chats (owner's priority):** scrolling up and down a long chat must be smooth, with no jumps or lost position; scrolling all the way to the start of the thread must work. Study how DeepSeek Harness's web UI (`@deepseek-ai/dsh`, open source) loads and scrolls its transcript and follow the same approach; if it doesn't load partially, use whatever gives smooth scrolling, with the loaded window prefetched ahead of the reader. Prove it with end-to-end tests that measure smoothness (frame times, no position jumps) and that reaching the first message works.
   202	  - Done on `dev/scroll`. The reader's place is held through prepends and evictions: there were 18 jumps of 6,000–8,500 pt up and 28 down, now none. Pages are read three screenfuls ahead in the direction of travel: there were 32–60 frames stalled at an edge, now none. Home and ⌘↑ read the chat's first page: there were 34 presses, now one press, 0.6 s. Release frames improved: gesture up p95 37 → 30 ms, max 172–185 → 125–132 ms. No transcript measure got slower. `LongChatScrollTests` (serial) and `TranscriptRevealTests`/`TranscriptFindTests` are mutation-checked. Record: `docs/perf/long-chat-scrolling.md`. Remaining: 1–4% of Release frames still take 50–130 ms, where a page lands or a very long reply is first built (about 70 ms of TextKit layout for 51 KB). Measuring off the main thread is the next step. Release confirmation at `33f2dfd7`: the fling passed 6 of 6; the 200-turn probes had 0 jumps in 3 runs; the end-to-end class passed twice with its budgets.
   203

   221	# Completed release: 0.1.120
   222	
   223	**Released, 2026-10-06:** 0.1.120 (build 124, tag `v0.1.120`) is the latest release. The complete AppKit migration is shipped. Source/tag and website changes are pushed; the public page, identical update feeds and downloaded DMG hash/signature pass verification at 01:07:03 UTC. See `docs/validation/Bello-Agent-0.1.120-2026-10-06.md` for evidence and the explicit manual/measurement limits. `docs/HANDOVER-0.1.120.md` preserves the original implementation snapshot.
   224	
   225	**Scope (owner, 2026-10-04): no SwiftUI.** Every view, window and the app itself move to AppKit, for predictable layout and real performance across the board. The look stays pixel-identical (screenshot gallery before/after) apart from run-to-run data. Behaviour, keyboard, focus and accessibility stay the same or better. **One release at the end**, when no file imports SwiftUI, the gate passes and the hour soak is clean.
   226	
   227	**All work goes on `dev/next`.** Workstream branches (`dev/appkit-*`) are merged here by the integrator. `main` holds released versions only — never push to `main` outside a release.
   228	
   229	## How work is done
   230	- Parallel workstream agents, each in its own worktree, build folder and Codex session (gpt-6.1-sol, xhigh, read-only) that plans every step and reviews every diff before commit.
   231	- Port, don't redesign: same layout, spacing, colours, motion, copy and behaviour. Compare gallery captures before and after (`scripts/compare-captures.py`); differences must be run-to-run data only.
   232	- Performance discipline: reuse views, draw only what is visible, never relayout what didn't change, no work per token across the window. Measure against the baseline below.
   233	- Existing tests keep passing; tests that read SwiftUI structure are rewritten to read AppKit/accessibility instead, never deleted without an equivalent.
   234	- Checks: `scripts/check-next.sh` (build, `test <Class>…`, `helper`, `gate`). Serial-lane classes run alone.
   235	
   236	## Rules for code on this branch
   237	- AppKit only for new code. A file stops importing SwiftUI when its port is done.
   238	- Keep the app's Pi look; no stock-looking controls.
   239	- Keep `packages/bello-views` free of app types; macOS 13. App target macOS 14.
   240	- Motion policy unchanged. Accessibility (A1) must not regress: native AppKit accessibility roles, labels, values and selected states.
   241	- Tick items here in the commit that finishes them.
   242

     1	# Repository workflow
     2	
     3	- After completing requested repository changes, commit them and push to the
     4	  configured GitHub upstream. This is the owner's standing instruction as of
     5	  2026-09-26; a separate request to push is not needed.
     6	- For releases, also push the relevant release tag and any final validation-record
     7	  commit. Follow `docs/Release.md` for packaging and website publication.
     8	- Preserve unrelated work and existing release commit identities. Do not force-push
     9	  or rewrite published history unless explicitly requested.
    10	- A later request to keep work local or read-only takes precedence.
    11	
    12	# Next release
    13	
    14	- All work for the next release goes on the branch `dev/next`. Read
    15	  `NEXT-RELEASE.md` first: it lists the state, what is left, and the rules.
    16	- An agent that cannot run code commits and pushes to `dev/next` and says what
    17	  to check; the owner's Mac runs `scripts/check-next.sh` to pull, build and test.

- [x] Collapsed sub-agent work (`dev/a128-sub-display`): a finished compact turn hides typed parent/child messages, reports, control cards and child counts; the main agent's answer remains visible. Expanding restores the original rows and navigation. [Validation](docs/validation/sub-display-0.1.128.md).
- [x] Stable sub-agent numbers (`dev/a128-sub-display`): `Sub-agent N · description` across cards, search results, child headers, Back to Parent labels, native window/tab titles and family Inspector. Numbers derive from unique accepted starts in the parent journal, including legacy and missing-child starts, with an optional rebuildable desktop cache. Follow-ups retain their number; journals, model-facing text and tool contracts are unchanged. [Validation and limits](docs/validation/sub-display-0.1.128.md).
- [ ] Native validation follow-up (`dev/a128-sub-display`, review round 2): the one full parallel lane passed 2,469 tests with one intermediate-page retention failure in `AppShellPerformanceTests` and 37 skips. The unchanged entire class passed 11/11 alone, retaining eight pages for eight displays; the parallel failure's cause remains unconfirmed. Round 3’s one full lane passed 2,471 tests with zero failures and 37 skips; the earlier failure did not recur. [Exact evidence](docs/validation/sub-display-0.1.128.md#one-full-native-parallel-lane-and-isolated-follow-up).
- [x] Sub-agent prompt-cache audit (`dev/a128-subagent-cache`): child follow-ups keep their original instruction role through effort changes, reopen and summary requests; start-tool model enums have deterministic order. A generated 26-request loopback workflow improves from 8 broken comparisons to none across 22 parent/child pairs; estimated reusable input rises 60.82% → 86.80%. Effort changes can still affect provider-hidden prefixes; actual upstream cache hits remain gateway-dependent. No tool, capture or journal-format change. [Audit, before/after table, mutation and 291 helper tests](docs/validation/subagent-cache-0.1.128.md).
- [x] Save queued-message edits (`dev/a128-queue-edit`): the normal composer banner offers Save and Cancel; Return and ⌘Return save the same queued item. Queued rewrites keep literal slash text and original images/skills, publish acknowledged text immediately, and reject older queue snapshots. Removed or otherwise ended edits retain the rewrite and an explanation; Save/Cancel restore the parked draft, including after relaunch. Helper queue semantics and journal/wire formats are unchanged. [Root causes, validation and gallery](docs/validation/queue-edit-0.1.128.md).
- [x] Queued-edit review: historical Save acknowledgements refresh the current queue without replacing newer row text; confirmed recovery cancellation clears its retained failure warning, while unsaved rewrites keep their explanation. [Failing-first review validation](docs/validation/queue-edit-0.1.128.md#lead-review-round-1).
- [x] Queued-edit notice composition: queued-edit and preflight explanations are retained separately and recomposed whenever either changes or clears. Confirmed recovery settles the displayed warning immediately, even when status or repeated-cancel replies produce no new snapshot, while preserving the stopped-run explanation. [Review round 2 validation](docs/validation/queue-edit-0.1.128.md#lead-review-round-2).
- [x] Recover saved side chats and forks (`dev/a128-side-keep`): registration validates the chat header without rejecting history warnings; cut-off tails and damaged later records open in the existing recovery UI, with journal bytes untouched. Missing files retain their pending registration. Failed copies appear together by chat/parent name with read-only Open anyway, Show file in Finder and confirmed Dismiss; dismissing removes only the reminder and preserves attachment references. Registration writes require the matching pending copy; a matching completed receipt succeeds without overwriting newer drafts. Refreshed notice text does not undo dismissal, and overlapping failures stay combined. [Causes, decisions and validation](docs/validation/side-keep-0.1.128.md).
- [x] Sub-agent policy and effort (`dev/a128-subagent-policy`): one parent prompt rule and both start-tool descriptions require an explicit user request to delegate. Start and finished-child follow-ups accept model-supported `effort`; running-child steering keeps its effort. Accepted effort survives recovery, appears in summaries/status/receipts, and reaches ordinary request capture. Optional fields preserve older journals. [Validation, exact texts and decisions](docs/validation/subagent-policy-0.1.128.md).
- [x] Sub-agent policy review: mixed v3 recovery writes retain accepted effort, older family checkpoints replay their authoritative journals, and missing origin capabilities inherit only the owner's accepted list. Manual/custom model capabilities come from the saved connection independently of the parent's Model default choice. Effort schema wording clarified. [Failing-first review validation](docs/validation/subagent-policy-0.1.128.md#lead-review-round-1).
- [x] Sub-agent policy review 2: desktop reciprocal discovery accepts the same missing optional capabilities as helper recovery, preserving parent links through live follow-ups. The send schema has fixed size independent of retained family history, with no child-ID enums or saved-plan scan; the coordinator still rejects unsupported child efforts before acceptance. [Regression and mutation evidence](docs/validation/subagent-policy-0.1.128.md#lead-review-round-2).
- [x] Fork chats with sub-agents (`dev/a128-fork-subagents`): copy fallback now writes one marker at the source's required version; promoted APFS clones keep valid checkpoint hashes and replay parity. Forks retain sub-agent cards/results as history, their own budget and newly owned children. Round 9 above replaces the original disabled History controls with read-only Open. Forks never adopt or write the source's children. Existing duplicate-marker damage is rejected unchanged with instructions to fork again from the source; managed-child Fork remains deferred by design. [Root causes, tests, decisions and gallery](docs/validation/fork-subagents-0.1.128.md).
- [x] New-chat project choice (`dev/a128-new-chat-project`): an unsaved chat offers an accessible “in <Project>” chip above the composer in Flat and Topics. Its searchable Pi picker lists trusted, available projects; switching uses the target's ordinary New Chat defaults, keeps typed text and explicit image attachments, clears the topic and old resource/context state, and stops being available once the chat is saved or sent. Save/open races are guarded without changing helper tools, permissions, capture or journals. [Validation and gallery](docs/validation/new-chat-project-0.1.128.md).
- [x] New-chat review fixes: an already-open Session Inspector follows the new project's archive scope in the same window, retiring old reads and previews without reopening hidden windows or rebinding another owner. Checked and unchecked project-picker names and paths share a fixed-width icon column. Review validation: 49 affected tests, 22 inspected gallery captures, full parallel lane 2,468 passed / 37 skipped / zero failures. [Review validation](docs/validation/new-chat-project-0.1.128.md#lead-review-round-1).
- [x] New-chat navigation race: a project choice suspended on defaults or availability is rejected after any page, chat-selection or side-focus change, including leaving and returning to the same draft. Choices require the Chats page at entry and commit; newer navigation, the draft and the original project remain intact. Shared navigation behavior is unchanged; 34 affected tests pass after a navigation-guard mutation check. [Review validation](docs/validation/new-chat-project-0.1.128.md#lead-review-round-2).
- [x] New-chat choice ordering: one per-chat generation registers each menu choice synchronously, including reselecting the current project. Newer choices, navigation, New Chat and first-save attempts retire earlier work; failed/cancelled choices never revive it, and stale errors cannot overwrite the draft's notice. Eight new behavioral regressions, a real generation-guard mutation and 53 passing affected tests cover the ordering paths. Children remain view + Stop, without a human composer. ## 0.1.126 decisions made while the owner was away (2026-10-13 night)

- RL-04 (capture lost at graceful shutdown): fixed with a bounded drain. The helper keeps delivering, and the app keeps acknowledging, queued capture packets until the outbox is empty or the existing shutdown deadline arrives. A drain cut short is marked partial. A durable spool-and-replay design was built and dropped: two review rounds found data-boundary bugs in it, including cleared chats' bodies surviving on disk.
- SCROLL-11 (measuring every line of an expanded long diff before first paint): not fixed. Deferring the measurement produced reading-position edge cases in five review rounds, so it was reverted to the synchronous measurement. The cost is one-time per expansion; it is left as a known item.
- UI-05 (send during image paste): Send waits while an image is importing. The button is disabled and a notice says "Adding image…". This replaces a deferred auto-send, which kept producing races in review (navigation, eviction, draft reload, deletion).

+import AppKit
+
+/// A chat's question with a list, in the app's own sheet chrome: deleting a
+/// chat names every saved side that goes with it. The detail, the list and
+/// its notes scroll inside the body; Cancel and the action stay visible, and
+/// Return and Escape choose Cancel for a destructive action.
+@MainActor final class ChatQuestionSheet: NSView {
+    let sheet: PiKit.Sheet
+    let cancel = PiKit.Button("Cancel")
+    let confirm: PiKit.Button
+    let review: ChatQuestionScroll
+    private let answer: (Bool) -> Void
+
+    init(question: ChatQuestion, list: ChatQuestion.List, destructive: Bool, parentSize: NSSize, answer: @escaping (Bool) -> Void) {
+        self.answer = answer
+        let width = min(480, max(260, parentSize.width - 24))
+        confirm = PiKit.Button(question.action, style: destructive ? .danger : .primary)
+        var rows: [ShellItem] = [.view(Self.text(question.detail, id: "detail", font: PiKit.Font.body, color: .piInk), .fill)]
+        if !list.items.isEmpty {
+            var names: [ShellItem] = []
+            if !list.heading.isEmpty { names.append(.view(Self.text(list.heading, id: "list-heading", font: PiKit.Font.caption, color: .piInkSecondary), .fill)) }
+            for (index, item) in list.items.prefix(ChatQuestion.List.shownLimit).enumerated() {
+                let name = ShellText(String(item.title.prefix(300)), font: PiKit.Font.body, color: .piInk, maximumLines: 1)
+                name.setAccessibilityIdentifier("chat-question-item-\(index)")
+                name.setAccessibilityLabel(item.title)
+                let indent = CGFloat(min(max(0, item.depth - 1), 6)) * 14
+                names.append(.view(ShellStack(.horizontal, spacing: 6, padding: NSEdgeInsets(top: 0, left: indent, bottom: 0, right: 0),
+                    [.view(Self.text(item.depth > 1 ? "↳" : "•", id: "marker", font: PiKit.Font.caption, color: .piInkTertiary)), .view(name, .fill)]), .fill))
+            }
+            if list.items.count > ChatQuestion.List.shownLimit {
+                names.append(.view(Self.text("and \(list.items.count - ChatQuestion.List.shownLimit) more", id: "list-more", font: PiKit.Font.caption, color: .piInkSecondary), .fill))
+            }
+            rows.append(.view(ShellStack(.vertical, spacing: 4, names), .fill))
+        }
+        for (index, note) in list.notes.enumerated() {
+            rows.append(.view(Self.text(note, id: "note-\(index)", font: PiKit.Font.caption, color: .piWarning), .fill))
+        }
+        let body = ShellStack(.vertical, spacing: 14, padding: NSEdgeInsets(top: 20, left: 24, bottom: 20, right: 24), rows)
+        let maximumBody = max(140, min(420, parentSize.height - 170))
+        review = ChatQuestionScroll(body: body, width: width, maximumHeight: maximumBody)
+        sheet = PiKit.Sheet(question.title, content: review, footer: SheetFooterRow(trailing: [cancel, confirm]))
+        sheet.width = width
+        super.init(frame: .zero)
+        addSubview(sheet)
+        if destructive { cancel.keyEquivalent = "\r"; confirm.keyEquivalent = "" }
+        else { confirm.keyEquivalent = "\r"; cancel.keyEquivalent = "" }
+        cancel.onPress = { answer(false) }; confirm.onPress = { answer(true) }
+        cancel.nextKeyView = confirm; confirm.nextKeyView = cancel
+        cancel.setAccessibilityIdentifier("chat-question-cancel"); confirm.setAccessibilityIdentifier("chat-question-confirm")
+        sheet.onCancel = { answer(false) }
+        setAccessibilityElement(false)
+        setAccessibilityIdentifier("chat-question")
+    }
+    required init?(coder: NSCoder) { fatalError("Not used from a nib") }
+    override var isFlipped: Bool { true }
+    override var intrinsicContentSize: NSSize { sheet.intrinsicContentSize }
+    override var fittingSize: NSSize { intrinsicContentSize }
+    override func layout() { super.layout(); sheet.frame = bounds }
+    override func cancelOperation(_ sender: Any?) { answer(false) }
+    override func performKeyEquivalent(with event: NSEvent) -> Bool {
+        if event.modifierFlags.intersection([.command, .control, .option, .shift]).isEmpty {
+            if event.keyCode == 53 { answer(false); return true }
+            if [UInt16(36), 76].contains(event.keyCode) { (cancel.keyEquivalent == "\r" ? cancel : confirm).onPress?(); return true }
+            if review.scroll(key: event.keyCode) { return true }
+        }
+        return super.performKeyEquivalent(with: event)
+    }
+    override func pageDown(_ sender: Any?) { review.scroll(key: KeyCode.pageDown) }
+    override func pageUp(_ sender: Any?) { review.scroll(key: KeyCode.pageUp) }
+    override func scrollPageDown(_ sender: Any?) { review.scroll(key: KeyCode.pageDown) }
+    override func scrollPageUp(_ sender: Any?) { review.scroll(key: KeyCode.pageUp) }
+
+    private static func text(_ value: String, id: String, font: NSFont, color: NSColor) -> ShellText {
+        let text = ShellText(value, font: font, color: color)
+        text.setAccessibilityIdentifier("chat-question-" + id)
+        return text
+    }
+}
+
+/// The question's body, as tall as its content up to `maximumHeight`.
+@MainActor final class ChatQuestionScroll: NSScrollView {
+    private let body: ShellStack
+    private let size: NSSize
+    init(body: ShellStack, width: CGFloat, maximumHeight: CGFloat) {
+        self.body = body
+        size = NSSize(width: width, height: min(maximumHeight, body.height(forWidth: width)))
+        super.init(frame: .zero)
+        drawsBackground = false; borderType = .noBorder
+        hasVerticalScroller = true; autohidesScrollers = true; scrollerStyle = .overlay
+        documentView = body
+    }
+    required init?(coder: NSCoder) { fatalError("Not used from a nib") }
+    var scrolls: Bool { body.height(forWidth: size.width) > size.height }
+    override var intrinsicContentSize: NSSize { size }
+    override var fittingSize: NSSize { size }
+    override func layout() {
+        super.layout()
+        let width = max(1, contentSize.width)
+        body.setFrameSize(NSSize(width: width, height: max(contentSize.height, body.height(forWidth: width))))
+    }
+    @discardableResult func scroll(key: UInt16) -> Bool {
+        let clip = contentView
+        let last = max(0, body.bounds.height - clip.bounds.height)
+        let page = max(40, clip.bounds.height - 32)
+        let target: CGFloat
+        switch key {
+        case KeyCode.pageDown: target = clip.bounds.minY + page
+        case KeyCode.pageUp: target = clip.bounds.minY - page
+        case KeyCode.home: target = 0
+        case KeyCode.end: target = last
+        case KeyCode.downArrow: target = clip.bounds.minY + 20
+        case KeyCode.upArrow: target = clip.bounds.minY - 20
+        default: return false
+        }
+        clip.scroll(to: NSPoint(x: 0, y: min(last, max(0, target))))
+        reflectScrolledClipView(clip)
+        return true
+    }
+}

@@ -15,6 +15,29 @@ struct ChatQuestion: Equatable, Sendable {
     let detail: String
     /// The label on the button that goes ahead.
     let action: String
+    /// Names listed under the detail, with notes beneath them. A question
+    /// with a list is asked on the app's own sheet, where the list scrolls.
+    var list: List? = nil
+
+    struct List: Equatable, Sendable {
+        struct Item: Equatable, Sendable {
+            var title: String
+            /// 1 for a direct child; deeper items are indented under it.
+            var depth = 1
+        }
+        var heading: String
+        var items: [Item]
+        /// Plain sentences, such as what is skipped and why.
+        var notes: [String] = []
+        /// The rows shown; the remainder is summarized as a count.
+        static let shownLimit = 200
+        /// A plain-text form for an alert with no window to host the sheet.
+        var plainText: String {
+            var lines = items.prefix(20).map { String(repeating: "  ", count: max(0, $0.depth - 1)) + "• " + $0.title }
+            if items.count > 20 { lines.append("and \(items.count - 20) more") }
+            return ([heading].filter { !$0.isEmpty && !items.isEmpty } + lines + notes).joined(separator: "\n")
+        }
+    }
 }

+    /// Saved sides at every depth below `owner`, from the complete durable
+    /// graph, so sides outside the chat-list window go with their chat too.
+    /// Parents precede their children. Forks are provenance, never members;
+    /// a damaged intermediate record keeps its descendants out of the set.
+    func savedSideDescendants(of owner: String, workspaceID: String) throws -> [(chat: ChatRecord, depth: Int)] {
+        let records = try durableChatGraph().records
+        var children: [String: [ChatRecord]] = [:]
+        for chat in records.values where chat.workspaceID == workspaceID && !chat.isSubagent && !chat.imported {
+            if let parent = chat.persistedBudgetParentID { children[parent, default: []].append(chat) }
+        }
+        var result: [(chat: ChatRecord, depth: Int)] = [], queue = [(owner, 0)], seen: Set<String> = [owner], next = 0
+        while next < queue.count {
+            let (id, depth) = queue[next]; next += 1
+            guard depth < 128 else { continue }
+            for child in (children[id] ?? []).sorted(by: { $0.id < $1.id }) where seen.insert(child.id).inserted {
+                result.append((child, depth + 1)); queue.append((child.id, depth + 1))
+            }
+        }
+        return result
+    }
+
+    /// Every decodable, non-deleted chat record, and the ids present at all.
+    private func durableChatGraph() throws -> (records: [String: ChatRecord], present: Set<String>) {
+        let database = try ready()
+        var statement: OpaquePointer?
+        let sql = "SELECT c.id,c.value FROM records c WHERE c.kind='chat' AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM records d WHERE d.kind='session-deleted' AND d.id=c.id)"
+        guard sqlite3_prepare_v2(database,sql,-1,&statement,nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
+        defer { sqlite3_finalize(statement) }
+        var records: [String:ChatRecord] = [:], present = Set<String>()
+        var step = sqlite3_step(statement)
+        while step == SQLITE_ROW {
+            if let text = sqlite3_column_text(statement,0) {
+                let id = String(cString:text); present.insert(id)
+                let size = Int(sqlite3_column_bytes(statement,1))
+                if size <= Self.recordByteLimit("chat"), let bytes = sqlite3_column_blob(statement,1),
+                   let chat = try? JSONDecoder().decode(ChatRecord.self,from:Data(bytes:bytes,count:size)), chat.id == id { records[id] = chat }
+            }
+            step = sqlite3_step(statement)
+        }
+        guard step == SQLITE_DONE else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
+        return (records, present)
+    }

    22	    /// Saved sides below `root` from the complete durable graph, plus any
    23	    /// loaded record the store has not caught up with. Off the main actor.
    24	    func savedSideDescendants(of root: ChatRecord) async throws -> [(chat: ChatRecord, depth: Int)] {
    25	        guard let store, !root.isSubagent, !root.imported else { return [] }
    26	        var found = try await store.savedSideDescendants(of: root.id, workspaceID: root.workspaceID)
    27	        var known = Set(found.map(\.chat.id)).union([root.id])
    28	        func depth(_ item: ChatRecord) -> Int? {
    29	            var current = item, value = 0, seen: Set<String> = [item.id]
    30	            while let id = current.persistedBudgetParentID, seen.insert(id).inserted, value < 128 {
    31	                value += 1
    32	                if id == root.id { return value }
    33	                guard let parent = record(id), parent.workspaceID == root.workspaceID else { return nil }
    34	                current = parent
    35	            }
    36	            return nil
    37	        }
    38	        for item in chats where !item.isSubagent && !item.imported && item.workspaceID == root.workspaceID && !known.contains(item.id) {
    39	            if let value = depth(item) { found.append((item, value)); known.insert(item.id) }
    40	        }
    41	        // Each side directly under its parent, as the family picker reads.
    42	        var children: [String: [(chat: ChatRecord, depth: Int)]] = [:]
    43	        for side in found { children[side.chat.persistedBudgetParentID ?? "", default: []].append(side) }
    44	        var ordered: [(chat: ChatRecord, depth: Int)] = []
    45	        func visit(_ id: String) {
    46	            let next = (children.removeValue(forKey: id) ?? []).sorted {
    47	                let order = $0.chat.title.localizedStandardCompare($1.chat.title)
    48	                return order == .orderedSame ? $0.chat.id < $1.chat.id : order == .orderedAscending
    49	            }
    50	            for side in next { ordered.append(side); visit(side.chat.id) }
    51	        }
    52	        visit(root.id)
    53	        return ordered
    54	    }

   175	    /// Detaches the deleted root's runtimes. With `repairAncestors`, the
   176	    /// helper first repairs surviving ancestors' ledgers from the deleted
   177	    /// subtree. Returns a non-empty value when that repair was skipped
   178	    /// because the project is unavailable; no runtime can be loaded then.
   179	    /// Without `descendantsDeleted` (intents from older versions), surviving
   180	    /// saved sides also receive the owner's committed charges.
   181	    private func prepareFamilyDeletion(_ owner: SessionDeletionRecord, descendantsDeleted: Bool = false, repairAncestors: Bool = true) async throws -> [String] {
   182	        let journal = try familyDeletionJournal(owner)
   183	        var host = hosts[owner.workspaceID]
   184	        if host?.isReady != true {
   185	            try await host?.settleCaptureIfStopping()
   186	            // Nothing is loaded and no survivor needs this ledger: deleting a
   187	            // main chat with its whole family needs no helper at all.
   188	            if descendantsDeleted && !repairAncestors { return [] }
   189	            // A missing journal has no ledger left to repair. Inspection
   190	            // errors must not be mistaken for absence and permit cleanup.
   191	            guard let journal else { return [] }
   192	            do {
   193	                let values = try journal.resourceValues(forKeys: [.isRegularFileKey])
   194	                guard values.isRegularFile == true else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
   195	            } catch let error as NSError where error.domain == NSCocoaErrorDomain && [NSFileNoSuchFileError, NSFileReadNoSuchFileError].contains(error.code) { return [] }
   196	            try await ensureConfiguration()
   197	            // Owner decision (2026-10-16): a missing project no longer blocks
   198	            // deletion; the repair that needs it is skipped and named.
   199	            if descendantsDeleted, let reason = deletionProjectUnavailable(owner.workspaceID) { return [reason] }
   200	            guard let workspace = workspace(for: owner.workspaceID) else { throw HostError.failure("Restore this chat's project before deleting it so saved side costs can be repaired.") }
   201	            host = try await self.host(for: workspace)
   202	        }
   203	        guard let host else { return [] }
   204	        cancelIdle(workspaceID: owner.workspaceID)
   205	        defer { scheduleIdle(workspaceID: owner.workspaceID, host: host) }
   206	        // The helper refuses unsettled work and repairs surviving ledgers
   207	        // from the deleted subtree's committed charges, even with no loaded
   208	        // runtime or capture evidence. Await that durable repair before
   209	        // tombstones/Trash. Starting only the project helper needs no model
   210	        // profile/credential.
   211	        try await releaseIdleBudgetDependents(of:owner.id,host:host)
   212	        _ = try await host.request("session.forget", sessionID: owner.id, params: descendantsDeleted ? ["descendantsDeleted": .bool(true)] : [:])
   213	        return []
   214	    }
   215	
   216	    func markPendingFamilyDeletions() async {
   217	        guard let store else { return }
   218	        let intents = (try? await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()) ?? []
   219	        deletingFamilies.formUnion(intents.map(\.ownerID))
   220	        deletingFamilies.formUnion(intents.filter { $0.sideDepth != nil }.map(\.id))
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    func resumeFamilyDeletions() async {
   224	        guard let store else { return }
   225	        do {
   226	            let intents = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
   227	            for members in Dictionary(grouping: intents, by: \.ownerID).values { try await finishFamilyDeletion(members) }
   228	        } catch { self.error = "Family deletion is incomplete and will be retried. " + error.localizedDescription }
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    private func familyDeletionJournal(_ member: SessionDeletionRecord) throws -> URL? {
   232	        guard let path = member.path else { return nil }
   233	        let directory = try subagentDirectory(member.workspaceID), journal = URL(fileURLWithPath: path)
   234	        // Durable forks and kept sides use the helper's existing names. Only
   235	        // owners accept those aliases; managed children always use their UUID.
   236	        let names = member.id == member.ownerID ? [member.id + ".jsonl", "fork_" + member.id + ".jsonl", "side_" + member.id + ".jsonl"]
   237	            : member.sideDepth != nil ? (member.isSavedSide ? ["side_" + member.id + ".jsonl"] : []) : [member.id + ".jsonl"]
   238	        guard journal.resolvingSymlinksInPath().deletingLastPathComponent().path == directory.path,
   239	              names.contains(journal.lastPathComponent) else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
   240	        return journal
   241	    }
   242	
   243	    private func finishFamilyDeletion(_ members: [SessionDeletionRecord]) async throws {
   244	        guard let store, let owner = members.first?.ownerID else { return }
   245	        deletingFamilies.insert(owner)
   246	        guard let record = members.first(where: { $0.id == owner }),
   247	              try await store.get(String.self, kind: "session-deleted", id: owner) == owner else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
   248	        if record.repairCompleted != true {
   249	            // An older app's intent is not proof that it repaired side mirrors.
   250	            // Retry before any cleanup, using the persisted owner even when it
   251	            // is absent from the current chat-list window.
   252	            _ = try await prepareFamilyDeletion(record)
   253	            helperSessionDidUnload(owner)
   254	            try await store.completeFamilyDeletionRepair(owner)
   255	        }
   256	        try familyDeletionStep?("repaired")
   257	        // Validate the complete manifest before deleting any file. Remove the
   258	        // owner journal first, then saved sides parents-first: a cold family
   259	        // scan must never discover a side whose children have already been
   260	        // intentionally removed. The repair receipt and owner metadata remain
   261	        // until every file is cleaned up.
   262	        for member in members {
   263	            guard member.ownerID == owner, try await store.get(String.self, kind: "session-deleted", id: member.id) == owner else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
   264	            _ = try familyDeletionJournal(member)
   265	        }
   266	        func rank(_ member: SessionDeletionRecord) -> Int { member.id == owner ? 0 : member.sideDepth ?? 129 }
   267	        for member in members.sorted(by: { (rank($0), $0.id) < (rank($1), $1.id) }) {
   268	            guard member.ownerID == owner, try await store.get(String.self, kind: "session-deleted", id: member.id) == owner else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
   269	            let directory = try subagentDirectory(member.workspaceID)
   270	            let journal = try familyDeletionJournal(member)
   271	            try removeRetiredCaptureSpools(sessionID: member.id)
   272	            try await traces.clear(sessionID: member.id)
   273	            try await traces.forgetSubagentRelationships(sessionID: member.id, workspaceID: member.workspaceID)
   274	            if let journal {
   275	                let names: [String]
   276	                do { names = try FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: directory.path) }
   277	                catch let error as NSError where error.domain == NSCocoaErrorDomain && [NSFileNoSuchFileError, NSFileReadNoSuchFileError].contains(error.code) { names = [] }
   278	                var files = names.filter { $0 == journal.lastPathComponent || $0 == journal.lastPathComponent + ".meta" || $0 == journal.lastPathComponent + ".origin" || $0 == journal.lastPathComponent + ".promotion" || $0.hasPrefix(journal.lastPathComponent + ".stream") || JournalCheckpoint.isTemporary($0, for: journal) }
   279	                    .sorted().map { directory.appendingPathComponent($0) }
   280	                let outputs = try ToolOutputOwnership.directory(root: directory.appendingPathComponent("tool-output"), sessionID: member.id)
   281	                // Use throwing inspection: an I/O failure must retain the intent.
   282	                do {
   283	                    let values = try outputs.resourceValues(forKeys: [.isDirectoryKey, .isSymbolicLinkKey])
   284	                    guard values.isDirectory == true, values.isSymbolicLink != true else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
   285	                    files.append(outputs)
   286	                } catch let error as NSError where error.domain == NSCocoaErrorDomain && [NSFileNoSuchFileError, NSFileReadNoSuchFileError].contains(error.code) { }
   287	                if !files.isEmpty {
   288	                    if let familyFileRecycler { try await familyFileRecycler(files) }
   289	                    else {
   290	                        try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { (continuation: CheckedContinuation<Void, Error>) in
   291	                            NSWorkspace.shared.recycle(files) { _, error in if let error { continuation.resume(throwing: error) } else { continuation.resume() } }
   292	                        }
   293	                    }
   294	                }
   295	            }
   296	            try familyDeletionStep?("files:" + member.id)
   297	        }
   298	        // Keep the owner's retry entry/repair proof until child metadata and
   299	        // intents are gone too. Retrying never needs a deleted child journal.
   300	        for member in members.sorted(by: { (rank($0), $0.id) > (rank($1), $1.id) }) {
   301	            forgetReadState(member.id); forgetRunHold(member.id)
   302	            for kind in ["chat", "draft", "anchor", "capture-preference", "handoff", "side-keep", SideKeepRegistration.kind, "session-read", RunHoldRecord.kind, ContextReading.recordKind, JournalSlimMark.kind] { try await store.remove(kind: kind, id: member.id) }
   303	            try await store.removeAll(kind: "receipt:\(member.id)")
   304	            try await store.removeAll(kind: "pending:\(member.id)")
   305	            contextReadings.removeValue(forKey: member.id); subagentSummaries.removeValue(forKey: member.id); subagentEfforts.removeValue(forKey: member.id)
   306	            for (parentID, info) in sides where parentID == member.id || info.id == member.id {
   307	                sides.removeValue(forKey:parentID)
   308	                if info.pending { displays.removeValue(forKey:info.id); forgetOpened(info.id) }
   309	                else { displays[info.id]?.presentation.cancel() }
   310	            }
   311	            chats.removeAll { $0.id == member.id }; displays.removeValue(forKey: member.id); helperSessionDidUnload(member.id)
   312	            forgetOpened(member.id)
   313	            if selectedID == member.id { selectedID = nil; selected = nil }
   314	            if focusedSessionID == member.id { focusedSessionID = nil }
   315	            try await removeDeletedCapturePreference(sessionID: member.id)
   316	            try familyDeletionStep?("metadata:" + member.id)
   317	            try await store.remove(kind: SessionDeletionRecord.kind, id: member.id)
   318	            if member.id != owner { try familyDeletionStep?("intent:" + member.id) }
   319	        }
   320	        try await restoreSideBudgets()
   321	        for id in displays.keys { refreshCostReading(id) }
   322	        deletingFamilies.remove(owner)
   323	        deletingFamilies.subtract(members.filter { $0.sideDepth != nil }.map(\.id))
   324	    }
   325	}
   185	            // The preview is ready; the question about it is a sheet, and this
   186	            // task waits on the answer rather than the whole app.
   187	            switch await questions.confirm(Self.portableDraft, about: item.id) {
   188	            case .yes: break
   189	            case .no: return
   190	            case .busy: self.error = PiQuestion.busyNotice; return
   191	            }
   192	            var copy = ChatRecord(id: UUID().uuidString, workspaceID: item.workspaceID, title: item.title + " — portable handoff", path: nil, profileID: profileID, toolMode: item.toolMode)
   193	            copy.topicID = effectiveTopicID(for: item)
   194	            try await store.commitPortableHandoff(copy, draft: DraftRecord(id: copy.id, text: text), provenance: value["provenance"])
   195	            chats.insert(copy, at: 0); await select(copy.id)
   196	        } catch { self.error = error.localizedDescription } }
   197	    }
   198	    var canDeleteCommandChat: Bool {
   199	        conversationCommandsEnabled && conversationCommandID().flatMap(record).map { !$0.isSubagent } == true
   200	    }
   201	    func deleteChat() {
   202	        guard canDeleteCommandChat else { return }
   203	        deleteChat(conversationCommandID())
   204	    }
   205	    /// Deletes any chat by id, so archived chats can go straight from the sidebar.
   206	    func deleteChat(_ id: String?) {
   207	        guard let id, let item = record(id), !item.isSubagent else { return }
   208	        if pendingChatIDs.contains(id) { discardPendingChat(id); return }
   209	        guard let store else { error = StoreError.unavailable.localizedDescription; return }
   210	        if let info = side(id), info.pending { Task { await discardPendingSide(info) }; return }
   211	        if let busy = runningBudgetDependent(of:id) { error = budgetMemberBusy(busy); return }
   212	        guard displays[id]?.hasWork != true, displays[id]?.loading != true else { error = "Stop work and remove queued submissions before deleting this chat"; return }
   213	        Task {
   214	            do {
   215	                // Discovery reads metadata and member journals off the main
   216	                // actor; it never needs the project folder or its helper.
   217	                let preview = try await familyDeletionPreview(item)
   218	                guard record(item.id) != nil else { return }
   219	                let count = preview.managedCount, family = preview.sides
   220	                let isSide = deletionNeedsAncestorRepair(item)
   221	                let title = item.isArchived ? "Delete the archived chat “\(item.title)”?"
   222	                    : family.isEmpty ? (isSide ? "Delete this side?" : "Delete this chat?")
   223	                    : (isSide ? "Delete this side and its sides?" : "Delete this chat and its sides?")
   224	                var detail = item.imported ? "Remove its app index and draft. The imported original stays in place."
   225	                    : "Move its managed conversation files to Trash and remove its draft and retained trace bodies."
   226	                if !family.isEmpty { detail += family.count == 1 ? " Its saved side goes with it, with its sub-agents." : " All \(family.count) of its saved sides go with it, with their sub-agents." }
   227	                if count > 0 { detail += " Includes \(count) managed sub-agent\(count == 1 ? "" : "s")." }
   228	                if isSide { detail += " Its spend stays counted in “\(budgetOwner(for: item).title)”." }
   229	                if ([item.id] + family.map(\.chat.id)).contains(where: { self.sides[$0]?.pending == true }) { detail += " Its unsent side draft is also removed." }
   230	                var question = ChatQuestion(title: title, detail: detail, action: "Delete Chat")
   231	                question.list = .init(heading: family.isEmpty ? "" : "Saved sides deleted with it (\(family.count))",
   232	                                      items: family.map { .init(title: $0.chat.title, depth: $0.depth) }, notes: preview.skipped)
   233	                if !questions.ask(question, about: item.id, destructive: true, answered: { [weak self] confirmed in
   234	                    guard let self, confirmed, self.record(item.id) != nil else { return }
   235	                    self.performDelete(item, store: store, announced: preview.skipped)
   236	                }) { error = PiQuestion.busyNotice }
   237	            } catch { self.error = "Chat deletion could not be prepared. " + error.localizedDescription }
   238	        }
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    private func performDelete(_ item: ChatRecord, store: MetadataStore, announced: [String] = []) {
   242	        Task { do {
   243	            // The confirmation's count is only a preview: a running reply
   244	            // may accept its first delegation while the sheet is open.
   245	            // Every owned journal takes the same unload-and-rescan barrier.
   246	            if !item.imported {
   247	                let skipped = try await deleteSubagentFamily(item)
   248	                // Name anything the confirmation could not have announced.
   249	                if !Set(skipped).isSubset(of: announced) { error = "“\(item.title)” was deleted. " + skipped.joined(separator: " ") }
   250	                return
   251	            }
   252	            if let host = hosts[item.workspaceID] {
   253	                if host.isReady { _ = try await host.request("session.forget", sessionID: item.id) }
   254	                else { try await host.settleCaptureIfStopping() }
   255	            }
   256	            helperSessionDidUnload(item.id)
   257	            try removeRetiredCaptureSpools(sessionID: item.id)
   258	            try await traces.clear(sessionID: item.id)
   259	            forgetReadState(item.id); forgetRunHold(item.id)
   260	            for kind in ["chat", "draft", "anchor", "capture-preference", "handoff", "side-keep", SideKeepRegistration.kind, "session-read", RunHoldRecord.kind, ContextReading.recordKind, JournalSlimMark.kind] { try await store.remove(kind: kind, id: item.id) }
   261	            contextReadings.removeValue(forKey: item.id)
   262	            try await store.removeAll(kind: "receipt:\(item.id)")
   263	            for intent in try await store.list(CommandIntent.self, kind: "pending:\(item.id)") { try await store.remove(kind: "pending:\(item.id)", id: intent.id) }
   264	            pendingIntentsChanged(item.id)
   265	            chats.removeAll { $0.id == item.id }; displays.removeValue(forKey: item.id)
   266	            forgetOpened(item.id)
   267	            if selectedID == item.id { selectedID = nil; selected = nil }
   268	            if focusedSessionID == item.id { focusedSessionID = nil }
   269	            do { try await removeDeletedCapturePreference(sessionID: item.id) }
   270	            catch { self.error = "The chat was deleted, but its old capture preference could not be removed. \(error.localizedDescription)" }
   271	        } catch { self.error = "Chat deletion did not complete. \(error.localizedDescription)" } }
   272	    }
   273	}

 **Released 2026-10-16:** 0.1.128 (build 132, tag `v0.1.128`) is the latest release; record `docs/validation/Bello-Agent-0.1.128-2026-10-16.md`.
 
+## 0.1.129
+
+- [x] Deleting a chat deletes its whole family (`dev/a129-family-delete`, owner decisions 2026-10-16): the chat, its saved sides at every depth (including unlisted ones) and their sub-agents go to Trash under one durable intent, listed by name on a Pi confirmation sheet. Deleting a side deletes its descendants and keeps its spend counted in main. A missing or removed project no longer blocks deletion; only the app's own chat data is removed and a skipped side-cost repair is named. Earlier orphan sides are untouched. [Validation](docs/validation/family-delete-0.1.129.md).
+
 ## 0.1.128
 
 - [x] Release-gate repairs: side wire coverage asserts separate physical spend and shared main limits; sidebar press/cut-out fixtures use indented topic chats; rendered Find waits for expanding row geometry; child shortcuts retain command ownership; large agent results reuse completed Markdown segments and cached message-to-row lookups. [Validation](docs/validation/gate-fix-0.1.128.md).

+import AppKit
+import XCTest
+@testable import PiApp
+
+/// The Delete confirmation that names a chat's saved sides. Generated titles
+/// only; set PI_APP_CHAT_DELETION_GALLERY to a folder to write the scenes.
+final class ChatDeletionGalleryTests: XCTestCase, SerialTestLane {
+    @MainActor func testDeletionConfirmationScenes() throws {
+        guard let path = testEnvironment("PI_APP_CHAT_DELETION_GALLERY") else { throw XCTSkip("Set PI_APP_CHAT_DELETION_GALLERY for these scenes") }
+        let folder = URL(fileURLWithPath: path)
+        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
+        let few: [ChatQuestion.List.Item] = [.init(title: "Parser edge cases"), .init(title: "Escaped scalar spans", depth: 2),
+            .init(title: "A very long side title that keeps going well past the width of the sheet to show how it is cut", depth: 3), .init(title: "Release notes draft")]
+        let many = (1...240).map { ChatQuestion.List.Item(title: "Side conversation \($0)", depth: $0 % 4 == 0 ? 2 : 1) }
+        let scenes: [(String, String, String, [ChatQuestion.List.Item], [String])] = [
+            ("few", "Delete this chat and its sides?", "Move its managed conversation files to Trash and remove its draft and retained trace bodies. All 4 of its saved sides go with it, with their sub-agents. Includes 2 managed sub-agents.", few, []),
+            ("many", "Delete this chat and its sides?", "Move its managed conversation files to Trash and remove its draft and retained trace bodies. All 240 of its saved sides go with it, with their sub-agents.", many, []),
+            ("missing-project", "Delete this side and its sides?", "Move its managed conversation files to Trash and remove its draft and retained trace bodies. Its saved side goes with it, with its sub-agents. Its spend stays counted in “Parser review”.", [.init(title: "Escaped scalar spans")],
+             ["This chat's project folder is missing. Only Bello's own copy of the chat is removed; nothing in the project folder is touched.",
+              "Skipped: checking for costs from interrupted requests that were not yet copied into “Parser review”. That check needs the project folder. Spend already counted there stays counted."]),
+            ("no-sides", "Delete this chat?", "Move its managed conversation files to Trash and remove its draft and retained trace bodies.", [], ["This chat's project folder is missing. Only Bello's own copy of the chat is removed; nothing in the project folder is touched."]),
+        ]
+        for (name, title, detail, items, notes) in scenes {
+            var question = ChatQuestion(title: title, detail: detail, action: "Delete Chat")
+            question.list = .init(heading: items.isEmpty ? "" : "Saved sides deleted with it (\(items.count))", items: items, notes: notes)
+            for (theme, appearance) in [("light", NSAppearance.Name.aqua), ("dark", .darkAqua)] {
+                for size in [NSSize(width: 1240, height: 760), NSSize(width: 340, height: 520)] {
+                    let sheet = ChatQuestionSheet(question: question, list: try XCTUnwrap(question.list), destructive: true, parentSize: size, answer: { _ in })
+                    sheet.appearance = NSAppearance(named: appearance)
+                    sheet.frame = CGRect(origin: .zero, size: sheet.intrinsicContentSize)
+                    sheet.layoutSubtreeIfNeeded()
+                    XCTAssertLessThanOrEqual(sheet.bounds.width, size.width, name)
+                    XCTAssertLessThan(sheet.bounds.height, size.height, name)
+                    for button in [sheet.cancel, sheet.confirm] {
+                        XCTAssertTrue(sheet.bounds.contains(button.convert(button.bounds, to: sheet)), name)
+                    }
+                    try capture(sheet, to: folder.appendingPathComponent("\(name)-\(theme)-\(Int(size.width)).png"))
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    @MainActor private func capture(_ view: NSView, to path: URL) throws {
+        view.layoutSubtreeIfNeeded(); view.displayIfNeeded()
+        let image = try XCTUnwrap(view.bitmapImageRepForCachingDisplay(in: view.bounds))
+        view.cacheDisplay(in: view.bounds, to: image)
+        try XCTUnwrap(image.representation(using: .png, properties: [:])).write(to: path)
+    }
+}

+/// Owner decisions (2026-10-16): one Delete removes a chat with its saved
+/// sides at every depth and their sub-agents; a missing project no longer
+/// blocks deletion. Generated fixtures only; no provider request is sent.
+final class FamilyDeletionTests: XCTestCase {
+    @MainActor private func deleteAll(_ model: WorkspaceModel) { model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at:url) } } }
+    @MainActor private func assertGone(_ model: WorkspaceModel, _ ids: [String], owner: String, _ label: String) async throws {
+        let store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
+        for id in ids {
+            XCTAssertNil(model.record(id),label + ": " + id)
+            let saved = try await store.get(ChatRecord.self,kind:"chat",id:id)
+            XCTAssertNil(saved,label + ": " + id)
+            let tombstone = try await store.get(String.self,kind:"session-deleted",id:id)
+            XCTAssertEqual(tombstone,owner,label + ": " + id)
+            XCTAssertFalse(model.deletingFamilies.contains(id),label + ": " + id)
+        }
+        let intents = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
+        XCTAssertTrue(intents.isEmpty,label)
+    }
+
+    @MainActor func testDeletingMainDeletesEverySavedSideAtEveryDepthAndTheirSubagents() async throws {
+        for (warm, listed) in [(false,true),(true,true),(false,false)] {
+            let label = "warm=\(warm) listed=\(listed)"
+            let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()
+            let paths = try ["main","side","nested","sibling"].map { URL(fileURLWithPath:try XCTUnwrap(model.record($0)?.path)) }
+            try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()
+            let child = try appendManagedChild(model,owner:"nested")
+            if warm { _ = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("nested"))) }
+            if !listed { model.chats = model.chats.filter { $0.id == "main" } }
+            deleteAll(model)
+            try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main")))
+            try await assertGone(model,["main","side","nested","sibling",child.id],owner:"main",label)
+            for path in paths + [child.path] { XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath:path.path),label + ": " + path.lastPathComponent) }
+            XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.isEmpty,label)
+        }
+    }

+    @MainActor func testInterruptedFamilyDeletionResumesAndNeverReopensASide() async throws {
+        for interruption in ["files:main","files:side","files:nested","metadata:sibling","intent:nested","metadata:side"] {
+            let model = try await sideBudgetFixture(), store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
+            try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()
+            var interrupted = false
+            deleteAll(model)
+            model.familyDeletionStep = { step in if step == interruption { interrupted = true; throw StoreError.unavailable } }
+            do { try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main"))); XCTFail("No interruption at \(interruption)") }
+            catch { XCTAssertTrue(interrupted,"\(interruption): \(error)") }
+            let intents = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
+            for intent in intents where ["side","nested","sibling"].contains(intent.id) { XCTAssertNotNil(intent.sideDepth,interruption) }
+            // A relaunch blocks every member until the retry finishes.
+            model.deletingFamilies.removeAll(); await model.markPendingFamilyDeletions()
+            for id in ["side","nested","sibling"] where model.record(id) != nil {
+                do { _ = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record(id))); XCTFail("\(interruption): \(id) reopened") }
+                catch { XCTAssertEqual(error.localizedDescription,"This chat is being deleted.") }
+            }
+            model.familyDeletionStep = nil
+            await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
+            XCTAssertNil(model.error,interruption)
+            try await assertGone(model,["main","side","nested","sibling"],owner:"main",interruption)
+        }
+    }

+    @MainActor func testMissingOrRemovedProjectDeletesOnlyTheChatsOwnFiles() async throws {
+        for project in ["missing","removed"] {
+            for target in ["main","side"] {

+                try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()

+                let sentinel = folder.appendingPathComponent("project-file.txt")
+                try Data("not chat data".utf8).write(to:sentinel)
+                if project == "missing" {
+                    try await model.updateConfiguration { $0.workspaces[0].paths = [folder.appendingPathComponent("moved-away").path] }
+                } else {
+                    try await model.updateConfiguration { $0.workspaces = [] }
+                }
+                XCTAssertNotNil(model.deletionProjectUnavailable("w"),label)

+    /// 0.1.129: deleting a chat deletes every saved side below it. Damaged
+    /// evidence in a deleted descendant cannot block that deletion, and no
+    /// descendant journal is rewritten on its way to Trash.
+    func testFamilyDeletionSkipsMirrorsIntoDeletedDescendants() async throws {
+        for loaded in [false,true] {
+            let root = try temporaryDirectory(); defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at:root) }
+            _ = try fixture(root,shape:.current,crash:.afterAll)
+            let url = file("child",root)
+            let rows = try String(contentsOf:url,encoding:.utf8).split(separator:"\n").map { line -> String in
+                var record = try JSON.parse(Data(line.utf8))
+                if record["customType"].text == JournalRecordKind.cost { record["data"]["scope"]["sideParents"] = ["foreign"] }
+                return record.encoded()
+            }
+            try Data((rows.joined(separator:"\n") + "\n").utf8).write(to:url)
+            let before = try ["side","child"].map { try Data(contentsOf:file($0,root)) }, host = try await host(root)
+            if loaded { _ = try await open(host,"child",root) }
+            do { _ = try await host.command("session.forget",sessionID:"main",params:["descendantsDeleted":true]) }
+            catch { XCTFail("A deleted descendant's evidence must not block its family's deletion (loaded=\(loaded)): \(error)") }
+            for id in ["main","child"] { let value = await host.loadedSession(id); XCTAssertNil(value) }
+            await host.shutdown()
+            XCTAssertEqual(try ["side","child"].map { try Data(contentsOf:file($0,root)) },before,"loaded=\(loaded)")
+        }
+    }
+
+    func testMemberForgetAfterTheRootRepairLeavesAncestorsUntouched() async throws {
+        for repaired in [false,true] {
+            let root = try temporaryDirectory(); defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at:root) }
+            _ = try fixture(root,shape:.current,crash:.beforeMain)
+            let before = try Data(contentsOf:file("main",root)), host = try await host(root)
+            _ = try await host.command("session.forget",sessionID:"side",params:repaired ? ["descendantsDeleted":true,"ancestorsRepaired":true] : ["descendantsDeleted":true])
+            await host.shutdown()
+            let after = try Data(contentsOf:file("main",root))
+            if repaired { XCTAssertEqual(after,before,"An already-repaired member must not write its ancestors again") }
+            else { XCTAssertNotEqual(after,before,"The deleted root still repairs its surviving ancestor") }
+        }
+    }
