    @MainActor func testDeletingMainWithNoReadyHelperTrashesTheWholeFamilyWithoutStartingOne() async throws {
        for state in ["absent", "stopped", "stopping"] {
            let model = try await sideBudgetFixture(), parent = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main"))
            let original = try XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"])
            try await original.shutdownAndWait()
            let paths = try interruptedSideMirror(model)
            if state == "absent" { model.hosts.removeValue(forKey:"w") }
            if state == "stopping" {
                _ = try await model.host(for:try XCTUnwrap(model.workspace(for:"w")))
                original.shutdown()
            }
            XCTAssertFalse(model.hosts["w"]?.isReady == true)
            // Nothing survives that could need a repaired ledger, so neither
            // a connection nor the project helper is needed.
            try await model.updateConfiguration { $0.profiles = [] }
            var recycled: [URL] = []
            model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in
                recycled += urls
                XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.isEmpty,"Deletion must not open conversation runtimes")
                XCTAssertTrue(model.displays.isEmpty,"Deletion must not allocate hidden transcripts")
                for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at:url) }
            }
            let skipped = try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(parent)
            XCTAssertTrue(skipped.isEmpty,state)
            XCTAssertFalse(model.hosts["w"]?.isReady == true,state + ": no helper starts for a whole-family deletion")
            for path in paths + [URL(fileURLWithPath:try XCTUnwrap(parent.path))] {
                XCTAssertTrue(recycled.contains(path),state + ": " + path.lastPathComponent)
                XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath:path.path),state)
            }
            for id in ["main","side","nested","sibling"] { XCTAssertNil(model.record(id),state + ": " + id) }
        }
    }

    @MainActor func testDamagedSideEvidenceCannotBlockDeletingTheWholeFamily() async throws {
        for warm in [false,true] {
            let model = try await sideBudgetFixture(), parent = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main"))
            try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()
            let paths = try interruptedSideMirror(model,foreignSideIdentity:true)
            // A ready helper takes the runtime path; nothing is opened, since
            // opening any member already rejects the damaged family ledger.
            if warm { _ = try await model.host(for:try XCTUnwrap(model.workspace(for:"w"))) }
            model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at:url) } }
            do { try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(parent) }
            catch { XCTFail("warm=\(warm): a deleted side's damaged ledger blocked its family: \(error)") }
            XCTAssertNil(model.record(parent.id))
            for path in paths { XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath:path.path)) }
            let intents = try await XCTUnwrap(model.store).pendingFamilyDeletions()
            XCTAssertTrue(intents.isEmpty)
        }
    }

    @MainActor func testColdSideHelperStartFailureKeepsTheDeletionRetryable() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture(), parent = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("side"))
        try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()
        _ = try interruptedSideMirror(model)
        // A project change temporarily prevents helper startup. No tombstone
        // or file cleanup may precede the surviving main chat's repair.
        model.workspaceChangesInFlight.insert("w")
        var recycled = false
        model.familyFileRecycler = { _ in recycled = true }
        do { try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(parent); XCTFail("Unavailable helper must not skip the repair barrier") } catch { }
        let store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
        let intents = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
        let tombstone = try await store.get(String.self,kind:"session-deleted",id:parent.id)
        XCTAssertTrue(intents.isEmpty); XCTAssertNil(tombstone); XCTAssertFalse(recycled)
        XCTAssertNotNil(model.record(parent.id)); XCTAssertFalse(model.deletingFamilies.contains(parent.id))
        XCTAssertFalse(model.deletingFamilies.contains("nested"),"Release the blocked descendants too")
        XCTAssertTrue(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath:try XCTUnwrap(parent.path)))
        model.workspaceChangesInFlight.remove("w")
        model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at:url) } }
        try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(parent)
        XCTAssertNil(model.record(parent.id)); XCTAssertNil(model.record("nested"))
        XCTAssertNotNil(model.record("main")); XCTAssertNotNil(model.record("sibling"))
    }

    @MainActor func testPendingColdOwnerDeletionRepairsBeforeRetryingTrash() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture(), parent = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main"))
        try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()
        let paths = try interruptedSideMirror(model), store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
        // A previous app may have committed an intent before attempting the
        // repair. Recovery must not treat that intent as proof of mirroring.
        try await store.beginFamilyDeletion([SessionDeletionRecord(id:parent.id,ownerID:parent.id,workspaceID:"w",path:parent.path)])
        model.chats = try await store.loadChats().filter { $0.id != parent.id }
        XCTAssertNil(model.record(parent.id))
        var attempts = 0
        model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in
            attempts += 1
            for path in paths { XCTAssertEqual(try self.lastFixtureCost(path)["usd"]?.number,0.9,"Retry must repair before Trash too") }
            if attempts == 1 { throw StoreError.unavailable }
            for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at:url) }
        }
        await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertEqual(attempts,1)
        XCTAssertTrue(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath:try XCTUnwrap(parent.path)))
        try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()
        await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertEqual(attempts,2)
        let remaining = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertTrue(remaining.isEmpty)
        XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath:try XCTUnwrap(parent.path)))
        for path in paths {
            XCTAssertEqual(try fixtureJournalRows(path).filter { $0["customType"]?.string == "pi-app.cost-update.v1" }.count,1,"Retry is idempotent")
        }
    }

    @MainActor func testBudgetOnlyRefreshCoalescesAndRejectsRetiredRuntimeReplies() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()
        await model.stopHostsAndWait()
        var frames: [[String:WireValue]] = []
        let host = HostSupervisor(commandSender: { frames.append($0) })
        try await host.connect(cwd:model.root,state:model.root.appendingPathComponent("budget-host"))
        model.hosts["w"] = host; model.boundHostConnections["w"] = host.connectionID
        model.opened = ["main","side"]; model.budgetDependencySessions = ["main"]
        model.displays["side"] = SessionDisplay(id:"side")
        func reply(_ index: Int, _ spend: Double) throws {
            XCTAssertEqual(frames[index]["method"]?.string,"session.status")
            XCTAssertEqual(frames[index]["params"]?.object?["budgetOnly"],.bool(true))
            host.receive(.frame(["v":.number(1),"kind":.string("reply"),"hostEpoch":.string(try XCTUnwrap(host.epoch)),
                "commandId":try XCTUnwrap(frames[index]["commandId"]),"ok":.bool(true),
                "result":.object(["cost":.object(["spentUSD":.number(spend),"limitUSD":.number(0.75),"reportedRequests":.number(2)])])]),connectionID:try XCTUnwrap(host.connectionID))
        }
        model.refresh("main")
        try await eventually("First budget read") { frames.count == 1 }
        for _ in 0..<20 { model.refresh("main") }
        XCTAssertEqual(frames.count,1)
        try reply(0,0.4)
        try await eventually("One follow-up carries all invalidations") { frames.count == 2 }
        try reply(1,1.4)
        try await eventually("Authoritative root warns its side") {
            model.displays["side"]?.footer.cost.budgetSpentUSD == 1.4 && model.budgetStates["main"]?.readTask == nil
        }
        XCTAssertTrue(model.displays["side"]?.costLimitHeld == true)
        model.refresh("main")
        try await eventually("Held retired read") { frames.count == 3 }
        let retired = model.budgetStates["main"]?.readTask
        model.helperSessionDidUnload("main")
        model.opened.insert("main"); model.budgetDependencySessions.insert("main")
        model.refresh("main")
        try await eventually("Replacement runtime read") { frames.count == 4 }
        try reply(3,1.6)
        try await eventually("Replacement reading published") { model.budgetStates["main"]?.reading.spentUSD == 1.6 }
        try reply(2,0.1)
        await retired?.value
        XCTAssertEqual(model.budgetStates["main"]?.reading.spentUSD,1.6)
        XCTAssertEqual(model.displays["side"]?.footer.cost.budgetSpentUSD,1.6)
        XCTAssertNil(model.displays["main"])
    }

    @MainActor func testBudgetOnlyOwnerPublishesDurableSpendWithoutATranscript() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()
        model.costSeedRead = { item in item.id == "main" ? .object(["usd":.number(1.2),"reported":.number(2),"unreported":.number(0)]) : nil }
        let nested = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("nested"))

        XCTAssertTrue(view.footer.cost.reached)
        XCTAssertNil(model.displays["main"]); XCTAssertNil(model.displays["side"])
        try await model.setCostLimit(.usd(2),for:"main")
        try await eventually("Shared warning follows the raised owner limit") { view.footer.cost.limit == .usd(2) && !view.footer.cost.reached }
        XCTAssertNil(model.displays["main"])
    }

    @MainActor func testPersistedUnlistedSideAncestryRetiresAfterParentDeletionAndReload() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture(), parent = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main"))

        try await model.traces.recordSideBudgets(["side":["main"],"nested":["side","main"]],workspaceID:"w")
        model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at:url) } }
        try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(parent)
        for id in ["side","nested","sibling"] {
            let tombstone = try await store.get(String.self,kind:"session-deleted",id:id)
            XCTAssertEqual(tombstone,"main","Unlisted sides go with their chat")
            let saved = try await store.get(ChatRecord.self,kind:"chat",id:id)
            XCTAssertNil(saved)
        }
        for _ in 0..<2 {
            try await model.restoreSideBudgets()
            XCTAssertNil(model.record("side"),"Deleted sides must not reappear in the chat window")
            XCTAssertNil(model.record("nested"))
            model.chats = try await store.loadChats()
        }
    }

    }

    @MainActor func testLastDependentReleaseUnloadsImplicitAncestorsButKeepsDirectUses() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()
        _ = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("nested")))
        _ = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("sibling")))
        XCTAssertNil(model.displays["main"]); XCTAssertNil(model.displays["side"])
        model.releaseHelperSession("nested")
        try await eventually("Nested dependency is released", timeout: .seconds(5)) { !model.opened.contains("nested") && !model.opened.contains("side") }
        XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.contains("main"), "The sibling still pins the root")
        model.releaseHelperSession("sibling")
        try await eventually("Last side releases its budget writer", timeout: .seconds(5)) { model.opened.isEmpty }

        let host = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main")))
        _ = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("side")))
        model.releaseHelperSession("side")
        try await eventually("Side release settles") { !model.opened.contains("side") }
        XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.contains("main"), "A direct open has independent lifetime")
        _ = try await host.request("session.status", sessionID: "main")
    }
    @MainActor func testConcurrentSideOpensShareAndReleaseTheirImplicitWriter() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()
        let (held, release) = AsyncStream<Never>.makeStream()
        model.sessionClosings["main"] = (UUID(), Task { for await _ in held {} })
        let side = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("side")), sibling = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("sibling"))
        let first = Task { try await model.open(side) }, second = Task { try await model.open(sibling) }
        try await eventually("Both dependents wait for the previous owner close") { model.budgetOpenCallers["main"] == 2 }
        model.sessionClosings.removeValue(forKey: "main"); release.finish()
        _ = try await first.value; _ = try await second.value
        XCTAssertTrue(model.budgetDependencySessions.contains("main"), "Concurrent budget opens must not look like a direct user open")
        model.releaseHelperSession("side"); model.releaseHelperSession("sibling")
        try await eventually("The shared writer releases after both sides", timeout: .seconds(3)) { model.opened.isEmpty }
        let (nextHeld, nextRelease) = AsyncStream<Never>.makeStream()
        model.sessionClosings["main"] = (UUID(), Task { for await _ in nextHeld {} })
        let main = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main"))
        let direct = Task { try await model.open(main) }, dependent = Task { try await model.open(side) }
        try await eventually("A direct use shares the pending writer") { model.budgetOpenCallers["main"] == 2 }
        model.sessionClosings.removeValue(forKey: "main"); nextRelease.finish()
        _ = try await direct.value; _ = try await dependent.value
        model.releaseHelperSession("side")
        try await eventually("Dependent is released") { !model.opened.contains("side") }
        XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.contains("main")); XCTAssertFalse(model.budgetDependencySessions.contains("main"))
    }

    @MainActor func testFailedSideOpenReleasesPartiallyOpenedAncestors() async throws {

    }

    @MainActor func testOpeningDependentPinsAncestorsUntilItFailsOrReleases() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture(), side = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("nested"))
        var held: CheckedContinuation<WireValue?, Never>?
        model.costSeedRead = { item in
            if item.id == side.id { return await withCheckedContinuation { held = $0 } }
            return nil
        }
        let opening = Task { try await model.open(side) }
        try await eventually("The child is held before its runtime opens") { held != nil }
        model.releaseHelperSession("main"); model.releaseHelperSession("side")
        XCTAssertTrue(model.sessionClosings.isEmpty, "Credential/seed reads pin the budget before session.open is sent")
        XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.contains("main")); XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.contains("side"))
        held?.resume(returning: nil); held = nil
        _ = try await opening.value
        model.releaseHelperSession(side.id)
        try await eventually("Pending release runs after the last child unload") { model.opened.isEmpty && model.sessionClosings.isEmpty }
    }

    @MainActor func testConfigurationFallbackRetainsAnOwnerWhoseUnloadIsRejected() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()
        await model.stopHostsAndWait()
        var frames: [[String: WireValue]] = []
        let host = HostSupervisor(commandSender: { frames.append($0) })
        try await host.connect(cwd: model.root, state: model.root.appendingPathComponent("mock-host"))
        model.hosts["w"] = host; model.boundHostConnections["w"] = host.connectionID
        model.opened = ["main", "side"]
        let profile = try XCTUnwrap(model.profiles.first)
        var finished = false
        let saving = Task { defer { finished = true }; try await model.saveProfile(profile, key: "synthetic-unused-key") }
        var replied = 0
        while !finished {
            try await eventually("Configuration command or completion") { finished || frames.count > replied }
            while replied < frames.count {
                let frame = frames[replied]; replied += 1
                let rejected = frame["method"]?.string == "session.configure" || frame["sessionId"]?.string == "main"
                host.receive(.frame(["v": .number(1), "kind": .string("reply"), "hostEpoch": .string(try XCTUnwrap(host.epoch)),
                    "commandId": try XCTUnwrap(frame["commandId"]), "ok": .bool(!rejected), "result": .object([:]),
                    "error": .object(["code": .string("session_busy"), "message": .string("Synthetic pinned owner")])]), connectionID: try XCTUnwrap(host.connectionID))
            }
        }
        try await saving.value
        XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.contains("main"), "A busy acknowledgement cannot forget the loaded owner")
        XCTAssertFalse(model.opened.contains("side"))
    }

}

import Foundation

extension WorkspaceModel {
    /// Accounting ownership, never pane placement (a mounted fork is independent).
    func budgetDescends(_ item: ChatRecord, from owner: String) -> Bool {
        var current = item, seen: Set<String> = []
        while seen.insert(current.id).inserted {
            if current.id == owner { return true }
            guard let id = current.delegation?.budgetOwnerSessionID ?? sideBudgetParent(current),
                  let parent = record(id), parent.workspaceID == item.workspaceID else { return false }
            current = parent
        }
        return false
    }

    func budgetMemberBusy(_ id: String) -> String {
        "Wait for “\(record(id)?.title ?? id)” to finish its work and queued messages before changing this chat."
    }

    func runningBudgetDependent(of owner: String) -> String? {
        displays.keys.sorted().first { id in
            guard id != owner, let view = displays[id], view.hasWork || view.loading,
                  let item = record(id) else { return false }
            return budgetDescends(item,from:owner)
        }
    }

    /// Held through the metadata/configuration commit, not just session.close.
    /// Invalidate already-leased opens as well as rejecting new family work.
    func beginBudgetOwnerChange(_ id: String) throws {
        guard !budgetOwnerChanges.contains(id) else { throw HostError.failure("Wait for this chat’s changes to finish.") }
        if let busy = runningBudgetDependent(of:id) { throw HostError.failure(budgetMemberBusy(busy)) }
        budgetOwnerChanges.insert(id)
        for item in chats where budgetDescends(item,from:id) { connectionGenerations[item.id,default:0] &+= 1 }
    }

    func budgetOwnerIsChanging(_ item: ChatRecord) -> Bool {
        budgetOwnerChanges.contains { budgetDescends(item,from:$0) }
    }

    /// The helper preflights its authoritative runtime family, including sides
    /// outside the chat window and readers without displays, then releases idle
    /// dependents deepest first. A busy reply names the member and closes none.
    /// No side is kept or discarded here; unpublished drafts remain in memory.
    func releaseIdleBudgetDependents(of owner: String, host: HostSupervisor) async throws {
        if let busy = runningBudgetDependent(of:owner) { throw HostError.failure(budgetMemberBusy(busy)) }
        let opening = sessionOpenings.filter { record($0.key).map { budgetDescends($0,from:owner) } == true }
        for value in opening.values { _ = await value.task.result }
        let closing = sessionClosings.filter { record($0.key).map { budgetDescends($0,from:owner) } == true }
        for value in closing.values { await value.task.value }
        let names = Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues:opened.union(displays.keys).union([owner]).compactMap { id -> (String,WireValue)? in
            record(id).map { (id,.string($0.title)) }
        })
        let result = try await host.request("session.releaseDependents",sessionID:owner,params:["names":.object(names)])
        for id in result.object?["released"]?.array?.compactMap(\.string) ?? [] { helperSessionDidUnload(id) }
    }

    /// Folder changes hold the project barrier before this preflight and save
    /// the new roots only after every old runtime has actually unloaded.
    func releaseIdleWorkspaceSessions(_ workspaceID: String) async throws {
        guard let host = hosts[workspaceID], host.isReady else { return }
        let ids = opened.filter { record($0)?.workspaceID == workspaceID }.sorted()
        for id in ids where opened.contains(id) {
            try await releaseIdleBudgetDependents(of:id,host:host)
            if opened.contains(id) {
                _ = try await host.request("session.close",sessionID:id)
                helperSessionDidUnload(id)
            }
        }
    }
}
import AppKit
import Combine

/// Durable spend observed from the helper, with no transcript, context or
/// retained request rows. Owners can serve side budgets without a display.
@MainActor final class SessionBudgetState: ObservableObject {
    @Published var reading: SessionCostReading
    var limitSent: CostLimit?
    var readTask: Task<Void,Never>?
    var readID = UUID()
    var dirty = false
    var childCostCursor: SubagentCostCursor?
    /// The child's durable ledger revision, shared by live native events,
    /// status and cold list reads. Progress clocks never supersede it.
    var childLedgerRevision: Int?
    init(_ reading: SessionCostReading) { self.reading = reading }
}

/// Orders accepted progress without keeping its prose or making dollars
/// monotonic: a later accounting correction can legitimately reduce them.
struct SubagentCostCursor {
    var turn: Int, revision: Int
    var epoch: String?
    var elapsed: Double
    init?(_ summary: SubagentSummary) {
        guard let progress = summary.progress else { return nil }
        turn = summary.turn ?? 1; revision = summary.revision
        epoch = summary.progressEpoch; elapsed = progress.elapsedMs
    }
    func follows(_ previous: Self) -> Bool {
        if turn != previous.turn { return turn > previous.turn }
        guard revision >= previous.revision else { return false }
        return epoch != previous.epoch || elapsed > previous.elapsed
    }
}

/// What the cost-limit notice in a transcript asks for.
enum CostLimitNoticeAction: Sendable { case raise, continueRun }

// Each chat's cost limit: the Settings default, a chat's own choice, and the
// helper session that enforces it. The helper checks the chat's reported
// spend against the limit before every model request; the app decides which
// limit that is and tells the helper whenever it changes.
extension WorkspaceModel {
    /// The limit every chat without its own runs under.
    var defaultCostLimit: CostLimit { configuration.defaultChatCostLimit }
    /// Only actual sides inherit budgets; forks also have parentSessionID.
    func sideBudgetParent(_ item: ChatRecord) -> String? {
        guard !item.isSubagent else { return nil }
        if item.path != nil { return item.persistedBudgetParentID }
        // Only an unpublished side draft lacks a journal. A mounted saved
        // fork is pane placement, never an accounting relationship.
        return side(item.id).flatMap { !$0.kept ? $0.parentID : nil }
    }
    func budgetOwner(for item: ChatRecord) -> ChatRecord {
        var current = item, seen: Set<String> = [item.id]
        while let id = current.delegation?.budgetOwnerSessionID ?? sideBudgetParent(current),
              seen.insert(id).inserted, let parent = record(id), parent.workspaceID == item.workspaceID { current = parent }
        return current
    }
    func costLimit(for item: ChatRecord) -> CostLimit { budgetOwner(for: item).costLimit ?? defaultCostLimit }
    func costLimit(for id: String) -> CostLimit { record(id).map(costLimit(for:)) ?? defaultCostLimit }

    /// Metadata is read at restore/open boundaries, never during view layout.
    /// Recheck the chat generation after the actor hop so deletion cannot be
    /// undone by a stale ancestor read. Tombstones are checked by the store.
    func retainBudgetAncestors(for additional: ChatRecord? = nil) async throws {
        guard let store else { return }
        while true {
            let revision = chatsRevision
            var records = chats
            if let additional, record(additional.id) == nil { records.append(additional) }
            let ids = Set(records.map(\.id))
            guard records.contains(where: { $0.persistedBudgetParentID.map { !ids.contains($0) } == true }) else { return }
            let ancestors = try await store.loadBudgetAncestors(of: records, profiles: profiles)
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            guard !isShutDown else { throw CancellationError() }
            if revision != chatsRevision { continue }
            let added = ancestors.filter { record($0.id) == nil && !deletingFamilies.contains($0.id) }
            if !added.isEmpty { chats.append(contentsOf: added) }
            return
        }
    }

    func restoreSideBudgets() async throws {
        sideBudgetRestoreRevision &+= 1
        let revision = sideBudgetRestoreRevision
        try await retainBudgetAncestors()
        // Keep recovery precedes archive startup. Registration is already
        // durable; the projection is rebuilt when the archive opens below.
        guard await traces.isOpen else { return }
        guard let store else { return }
        let workspaces = try await store.sideBudgetAncestry()
        guard !isShutDown, !Task.isCancelled, revision == sideBudgetRestoreRevision else { return }
        for (workspace, sides) in workspaces {
            guard revision == sideBudgetRestoreRevision else { return }
            let affected = try await traces.recordSideBudgets(sides, workspaceID: workspace)
            // Orphans retain their display and row caches. Re-read the numeric
            // projection when ancestry changes, including managed children and
            // former ancestors; waiting for another request leaves stale help.
            guard !affected.isEmpty else { continue }
            for item in chats where item.workspaceID == workspace && (affected.contains(item.id) || item.delegation.map({ affected.contains($0.budgetOwnerSessionID) }) == true) {
                if displays[item.id] != nil || chatStats[item.id] != nil { scheduleAccounting(item.id,workspaceID:workspace) }
            }
        }
    }

    /// The chat's reading with its current limit and what its helper last
    /// said of its spend.
    func costReading(for id: String) -> SessionCostReading {
        var reading = budgetStates[id]?.reading ?? displays[id]?.footer.cost ?? SessionCostReading()
        if reading.budgetOwnerID != nil { reading.costAttribution = nil }
        reading.budgetOwnerID = nil; reading.budgetSpentUSD = nil
        reading.override = record(id).map { budgetOwner(for: $0).costLimit } ?? nil; reading.defaultLimit = defaultCostLimit; reading.limit = costLimit(for: id)
        if let item = record(id) {
            let owner = budgetOwner(for: item)
            if owner.id != id {
                reading.budgetOwnerID = owner.id
                reading.budgetSpentUSD = budgetStates[owner.id]?.reading.spentUSD ?? displays[owner.id]?.footer.cost.spentUSD
                reading.costAttribution = "Counts toward " + owner.title
            }
        }
        return reading
    }
    /// Puts a chat's limit on its display: the footer's figure and the stop notice.
        }
    }
}

/// The popover "Raise limit…" opens over its button: the chat's limit editor.
/// One app-owned popover at a time, like the stats pills' and the skills'.
@MainActor final class CostLimitPopover {
    static let shared = CostLimitPopover()
    let presenter = PiPopoverPresenter()
    /// The chat whose limit the open popover edits (a test seam).
    private(set) var sessionID: String?
    static let width: CGFloat = 380
    func toggle(model: WorkspaceModel, footer: SessionMetrics, sessionID: String, anchor: NSView) {
        if presenter.isShown, self.sessionID == sessionID { close(); return }
        presenter.close()
        self.sessionID = sessionID
        presenter.toggle(from: anchor, width: Self.width, maximumHeight: 460, animates: !PiKit.Motion.reduced) { [weak model] in
            let editor = CostLimitLiveEditor(footer: footer, title: "Raise this chat's limit") { limit in
                try await model?.setCostLimit(limit, for: sessionID)
                // Chosen: the popover has done its job.
                CostLimitPopover.shared.close()
            }
            return PaddedView(editor, padding: NSEdgeInsets(top: PiSpacing.lg, left: PiSpacing.lg, bottom: PiSpacing.lg, right: PiSpacing.lg))
        }
    }
    func close() { presenter.close(); sessionID = nil }
}

// A durable relationship, independent of which pane displays the chat.
extension ChatRecord {
    var persistedBudgetParentID: String? {
        if let delegation { return delegation.budgetOwnerSessionID }
        guard let path, URL(fileURLWithPath: path).lastPathComponent == "side_" + id + ".jsonl" else { return nil }
        return parentSessionID
    }
}
        if !budgetDependency { releasedHelperSessions.remove(item.id) }
        var accepted = false
        // Pin before the first await, including metadata/credential reads and
        // waiting for an earlier close. A failed open releases its ancestors.
        budgetOpenCallers[item.id, default: 0] += 1
        defer {
            // A successful direct open owns its runtime even if it shared an
            // ancestor open already in flight. Failed callers claim nothing.
            if accepted, !budgetDependency { budgetDependencySessions.remove(item.id) }
            if budgetOpenCallers[item.id, default: 0] <= 1 { budgetOpenCallers.removeValue(forKey: item.id) }
            else { budgetOpenCallers[item.id, default: 0] -= 1 }
            releaseUnusedBudgetSessions()
        }
        guard !deletingFamilies.contains(item.delegation?.budgetOwnerSessionID ?? item.id) else { throw HostError.failure("This chat is being deleted.") }
        if try await store?.get(String.self, kind: "session-deleted", id: item.delegation?.budgetOwnerSessionID ?? item.id) != nil { throw HostError.failure("This chat is being deleted.") }
        if let issue = item.delegationIssue { throw HostError.failure(issue) }
        displays[item.id]?.isManagedSubagent = item.isSubagent
        // A close sent when the chat's display was let go of lands first, so
        // this open is not answered by the session being unloaded; so does a
        // move to another connection, so it is not opened on the old one.
        while true {
            if let closing = sessionClosings[item.id] { await closing.task.value }
            else if let change = connectionSwitches[item.id] { await change.task.value }
            else { break }
        }
        if budgetDependency, !opened.contains(item.id), displays[item.id] == nil {
            budgetDependencySessions.insert(item.id)
        }
        // A caller that read the chat before it moved may already have
        // written something naming the old connection (a fork's or a side's
        // record): it is refused, and opens again from the chat as it is.
        if let current = record(item.id), current.profileID != item.profileID || current.workspaceID != item.workspaceID { throw connectionChanged }
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        guard !isShutDown else { throw CancellationError() }
        if automaticContext { try requireAutomaticContext(item.id) }
        guard let store else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        guard !workspaceChangesInFlight.contains(item.workspaceID) else { throw HostError.failure("Wait for this project's folder changes to finish before starting work.") }
        let lease = try connectionLease(for: item)
        guard let profile = profiles.first(where: { $0.id == item.profileID }) else { throw connectionUnavailable }
        try LiteLLMConfiguration.requireSupportedAPI(profile.api)
        try await retainBudgetAncestors(for: item)
        // Open authoritative budget writers before a kept side after relaunch.
        // Parent selection and model context are unchanged.
        if let parentID = sideBudgetParent(item), let parent = record(parentID), parent.id != item.id {
            let owner = budgetOwner(for: item)
            guard parent.workspaceID == item.workspaceID, owner.id != item.id,
                  sideBudgetParent(owner).flatMap({ record($0) }) == nil, !owner.isSubagent else { throw HostError.failure("The side’s budget ancestry is inconsistent.") }
            _ = try await open(parent, automaticContext: false, budgetDependency: true)
        }
        if sessionOpenings[item.id] == nil, opened.contains(item.id), let host = hosts[item.workspaceID], host.isReady { idleTasks[item.workspaceID]?.cancel(); accepted = true; return host }
        if isEphemeral(item.id) { throw HostError.failure("This unkept side lost its host. Open a new side from the parent; nothing was replayed.") }
        // Preserve the opening contract used by connection switching. A caller
        // waiting behind a switch pins its budget but has not begun this open.
        sessionOpenCallers[item.id, default: 0] += 1
        defer {
            if sessionOpenCallers[item.id, default: 0] <= 1 { sessionOpenCallers.removeValue(forKey: item.id) }
            else { sessionOpenCallers[item.id, default: 0] -= 1 }
        }
        try await materializeChat(item.id)
        guard !item.imported, let workspace = workspace(for: item.workspaceID), workspace.trusted else { throw HostError.failure("This chat needs its saved profile and trusted project. Imported originals cannot be written.") }
        let credential = try await credentials(for: profile)
        // A read already dispatched to Keychain may finish after shutdown.
        // It must not create a new helper once terminal teardown has begun.
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        guard !isShutDown else { throw CancellationError() }
        try requireConnection(lease)
        if automaticContext { try requireAutomaticContext(item.id) }
        let key = credential["apiKey"]?.string ?? ""
        if key.isEmpty { throw HostError.failure("Save an API key in Keychain for this profile") }
        let host = try await host(for: workspace)
        defer { if automaticContext { scheduleIdle(workspaceID: item.workspaceID, host: host) } }
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        guard !isShutDown else { throw CancellationError() }
        if automaticContext {
            do { try requireAutomaticContext(item.id) }
            catch { scheduleIdle(workspaceID: item.workspaceID, host: host); throw error }
        }
        if let pending = sessionOpenings[item.id] {
            try await pending.task.value
        } else if !opened.contains(item.id) {
            var wire = profile.wire.object ?? [:]
            if let headers = credential["headers"] { wire["headers"] = headers }
            if item.isSubagent, let model = item.model { wire["modelId"] = .string(model) }
            var params: [String: WireValue] = ["profile": .object(wire), "apiKey": .string(key), "toolMode": .string(item.toolMode)]
            if let grants = subagentModels(for: item) { params["subagentModels"] = grants }
            if let handoff = try await store.get(WireValue.self, kind: "handoff", id: item.id) { params["handoff"] = handoff }
            // The chat's cost limit, which the helper checks before every model request.
            params.merge(await costLimitParams(for: item)) { _, limit in limit }
            if automaticContext { try requireAutomaticContext(item.id) }
            if item.connectionTest == true || workspace.isScratch { params["connectionTest"] = .bool(true) }
            // A background request opens as the helper's utility request of its
            // kind: its own short instructions, no tools, no project context.
            if let task = item.backgroundTask, [BackgroundRequestKind.title, .suggestions, .webhook].contains(BackgroundRequestKind(task)) { params["backgroundTask"] = .string(task) }
            if let path = item.path { params["path"] = .string(path) }
            // Loading a retained handoff above yields too. Recheck immediately
            // before installing the shared operation, without another await.
            if let pending = sessionOpenings[item.id] {
                try await pending.task.value
            } else if !opened.contains(item.id) {
                let token = UUID()
                let task = Task { [self] in
                    try Task.checkCancellation()
                    try await withConnectionOpen(item, lease: lease, host: host) {
                        try await reconcileJournal(item.id, host: host, profile: profile)
                        let initial = try await host.request("session.open", sessionID: item.id, params: params)
                        budgetState(for:item.id).limitSent = costLimit(for:item.id)
                        observeCost(initial.object ?? [:],sessionID:item.id)

                        // its path before an idle unload, even when no message is sent.
                        if let path = initial.object?["path"]?.string, let index = chats.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == item.id }) {

                            // storage failure, even though the in-memory path is known.
                            try await store.put(chats[index], kind: "chat", id: item.id)
                        }

                        observeSessionCompletion(sessionID: item.id, snapshot: initial.object ?? [:], baseline: true)
                        displays[item.id]?.observeCompaction(initial.object ?? [:])
                        displays[item.id]?.observeContext(initial.object ?? [:], baseline: true)
                        let preference = try await capturePreference(sessionID: item.id)
                        // Managed readers inherit their owner's capture policy;
                        // opening one must never issue a child policy change.
                        if !item.isSubagent { _ = try await host.request("debug.mode", sessionID: item.id, params: ["mode": .string(preference.mode)]) }
                        // Recovery may have unloaded this reader after its open
                        // reply, while path/capture persistence yielded above.
                        try Task.checkCancellation()
                        opened.insert(item.id)
                        // Recovery may have corrected the cost after the open
                        // response. Read just the authoritative numeric state.
                        if budgetDependency { refreshBudgetState(item.id) }
            try put(chat, kind: "chat", id: chat.id)
            try put(ChatModelDefaults(chat: chat), kind: ChatModelDefaults.recordKind, id: chat.profileID)
            guard sqlite3_exec(database, "COMMIT", nil, nil, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        } catch { sqlite3_exec(database, "ROLLBACK", nil, nil, nil); throw error }
    }

    /// Several chat records in one transaction, each written as `put` writes
    /// it: launch settling every background request a quit left, at once.
    /// Rebase discovery on the owner's latest durable organization in one
    /// actor turn. A delayed scan cannot restore a deleted or moved family.
    func beginFamilyDeletion(_ members: [SessionDeletionRecord], repairCompleted: Bool = false) throws {
        try transaction {
            guard let owner = members.first?.ownerID, members.contains(where: { $0.id == owner }),
                  Set(members.map(\.id)).count == members.count, Set(members.map(\.workspaceID)).count == 1,
                  members.allSatisfy({ $0.ownerID == owner && ($0.id == owner || ($0.sideDepth == nil ? UUID(uuidString: $0.id) != nil : $0.isSavedSide)) }) else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
            for var member in members {
                if member.id == owner { member.repairCompleted = repairCompleted ? true : nil }
                try put(owner, kind: "session-deleted", id: member.id)
                try put(member, kind: SessionDeletionRecord.kind, id: member.id)
            }
        }
    }

    func completeFamilyDeletionRepair(_ owner: String) throws {
        guard var record = try get(SessionDeletionRecord.self,kind:SessionDeletionRecord.kind,id:owner),
              record.id == owner, record.ownerID == owner,
              try get(String.self,kind:"session-deleted",id:owner) == owner else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
        record.repairCompleted = true
        try put(record,kind:SessionDeletionRecord.kind,id:owner)
    }

    /// Recovery must see the complete durable manifest, including families
    /// larger than the generic metadata listing's desktop window. A damaged
    /// intent fails closed instead of silently leaving a member behind.
    func pendingFamilyDeletions() throws -> [SessionDeletionRecord] {
        let database = try ready()
        var statement: OpaquePointer?
        guard sqlite3_prepare_v2(database, "SELECT value FROM records WHERE kind=? ORDER BY id", -1, &statement, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        defer { sqlite3_finalize(statement) }
        sqlite3_bind_text(statement, 1, SessionDeletionRecord.kind, -1, unsafeBitCast(-1, to: sqlite3_destructor_type.self))
        var values: [SessionDeletionRecord] = [], result = sqlite3_step(statement)
        while result == SQLITE_ROW {
            let size = Int(sqlite3_column_bytes(statement, 0))
            guard size <= Self.recordByteLimit(SessionDeletionRecord.kind), let bytes = sqlite3_column_blob(statement, 0) else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
            values.append(try JSONDecoder().decode(SessionDeletionRecord.self, from: Data(bytes: bytes, count: size)))
            result = sqlite3_step(statement)
        }
        guard result == SQLITE_DONE else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        return values
    }

    func registerSubagent(_ found: DiscoveredSubagent, workspaceID: String) throws -> ChatRecord? {
        let header = found.header, link = header.link
        guard link.valid, try get(String.self, kind: "session-deleted", id: link.childSessionID) == nil,
              try get(String.self, kind: "session-deleted", id: link.budgetOwnerSessionID) == nil else { return nil }
        let parent = try get(ChatRecord.self, kind: "chat", id: link.budgetOwnerSessionID)
        let validParent = parent.flatMap { $0.workspaceID == workspaceID && !$0.isSubagent && !$0.imported ? $0 : nil }
        let issue = found.issue ?? (validParent == nil ? "The parent chat is unavailable. This saved sub-agent is view-only." : nil)
        let old = try get(ChatRecord.self, kind: "chat", id: link.childSessionID)
        // Directory discovery and live opening can spell the same validated
        // journal differently (/var and /private/var on macOS). Compare file
        // identity canonically without accepting a different journal or origin.
        let path = URL(fileURLWithPath: found.path).resolvingSymlinksInPath().path
        if let old { guard old.workspaceID == workspaceID, old.delegation == nil || old.delegation == link,
            old.path.map({ URL(fileURLWithPath: $0).resolvingSymlinksInPath().path }) == path else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord } }
        var child = old ?? ChatRecord(id: link.childSessionID, workspaceID: workspaceID, title: header.description, path: found.path,
            profileID: validParent?.profileID ?? "", toolMode: header.readOnly ? ChatRecord.readOnlyTools : ChatRecord.editingTools,
            model: header.model.model, thinkingLevel: header.model.thinkingLevel, contextWindow: header.model.contextWindow,
            maxOutputTokens: header.model.maxOutputTokens, modelOutputLimit: header.model.modelOutputLimit,
            parentSessionID: issue == nil ? link.budgetOwnerSessionID : nil, delegation: link)
        // Older desktop encoders drop unknown fields. The validated child
/// blocks deletion. Generated fixtures only; no provider request is sent.
final class FamilyDeletionTests: XCTestCase {
    @MainActor private func deleteAll(_ model: WorkspaceModel) { model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at:url) } } }
    @MainActor private func assertGone(_ model: WorkspaceModel, _ ids: [String], owner: String, _ label: String) async throws {
        let store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
        for id in ids {
            XCTAssertNil(model.record(id),label + ": " + id)
            let saved = try await store.get(ChatRecord.self,kind:"chat",id:id)
            XCTAssertNil(saved,label + ": " + id)
            let tombstone = try await store.get(String.self,kind:"session-deleted",id:id)
            XCTAssertEqual(tombstone,owner,label + ": " + id)
            XCTAssertFalse(model.deletingFamilies.contains(id),label + ": " + id)
        }
        let intents = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertTrue(intents.isEmpty,label)
    }

    @MainActor func testDeletingMainDeletesEverySavedSideAtEveryDepthAndTheirSubagents() async throws {
        for (warm, listed) in [(false,true),(true,true),(false,false)] {
            let label = "warm=\(warm) listed=\(listed)"
            let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()
            let paths = try ["main","side","nested","sibling"].map { URL(fileURLWithPath:try XCTUnwrap(model.record($0)?.path)) }
            try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()
            let child = try appendManagedChild(model,owner:"nested")
            if warm { _ = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("nested"))) }
            if !listed { model.chats = model.chats.filter { $0.id == "main" } }
            deleteAll(model)
            try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main")))
            try await assertGone(model,["main","side","nested","sibling",child.id],owner:"main",label)
            for path in paths + [child.path] { XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath:path.path),label + ": " + path.lastPathComponent) }
            XCTAssertTrue(model.opened.isEmpty,label)
        }
    }

    @MainActor func testConfirmationListsTheSidesThatGoWithTheChat() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()

        model.questions.answer = { asked.append($0); return false }
        model.deleteChat("main")
        try await eventually("The confirmation is asked") { !asked.isEmpty || model.error != nil }
        XCTAssertNil(model.error)
        let question = try XCTUnwrap(asked.first), list = try XCTUnwrap(question.list)
        XCTAssertEqual(question.title,"Delete this chat and its sides?")
        XCTAssertTrue(question.detail.contains("All 3 of its saved sides go with it"),question.detail)
        XCTAssertEqual(list.heading,"Saved sides deleted with it (3)")
        XCTAssertEqual(list.items.map(\.title),["sibling","side","nested"],"Each side under its parent, by name")
        XCTAssertEqual(list.items.map(\.depth),[1,1,2])
        XCTAssertTrue(list.notes.isEmpty)
        XCTAssertNotNil(model.record("side"),"Cancel deletes nothing")
        asked.removeAll()
        model.deleteChat("side")
        try await eventually("The side's confirmation is asked") { !asked.isEmpty || model.error != nil }
        let side = try XCTUnwrap(asked.first)
        XCTAssertEqual(side.title,"Delete this side and its sides?")
        XCTAssertEqual(side.list?.items.map(\.title),["nested"])
        XCTAssertTrue(side.detail.contains("Its spend stays counted in “Main”."),side.detail)
    }

    @MainActor func testConfirmationLimitsALongListAndTheSheetScrolls() async throws {

        let rows = ConversationPaneTests.views(ShellText.self,in:sheet).filter { $0.accessibilityIdentifier().hasPrefix("chat-question-item-") }

        XCTAssertTrue(ConversationPaneTests.views(ShellText.self,in:sheet).contains { $0.text == "and 60 more" })
        for button in [sheet.cancel,sheet.confirm] { XCTAssertTrue(sheet.bounds.contains(button.convert(button.bounds,to:sheet))) }
        XCTAssertEqual(sheet.cancel.keyEquivalent,"\r","Return chooses Cancel for a destructive question")
        XCTAssertTrue(question.list?.plainText.contains("and 240 more") == true,"The windowless alert form is limited too")
    }

    @MainActor func testDeletingASideDeletesItsDescendantsAndKeepsItsSpendCountedInMain() async throws {
        let model = try await sideBudgetFixture()
        try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()

        try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("side")))
        try await assertGone(model,["side","nested"],owner:"side","side")
        XCTAssertNotNil(model.record("main")); XCTAssertNotNil(model.record("sibling"))
        let host = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("sibling")))

        XCTAssertEqual(status.object?["cost"]?.object?["spentUSD"]?.number,0.9,"Owner decision: a deleted side's spend stays counted")
    }

    @MainActor func testInterruptedFamilyDeletionResumesAndNeverReopensASide() async throws {
        for interruption in ["files:main","files:side","files:nested","metadata:sibling","intent:nested","metadata:side"] {
            let model = try await sideBudgetFixture(), store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
            try await XCTUnwrap(model.hosts["w"]).shutdownAndWait()
            var interrupted = false
            deleteAll(model)
            model.familyDeletionStep = { step in if step == interruption { interrupted = true; throw StoreError.unavailable } }
            do { try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(try XCTUnwrap(model.record("main"))); XCTFail("No interruption at \(interruption)") }
            catch { XCTAssertTrue(interrupted,"\(interruption): \(error)") }
            let intents = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
            for intent in intents where ["side","nested","sibling"].contains(intent.id) { XCTAssertNotNil(intent.sideDepth,interruption) }
            // A relaunch blocks every member until the retry finishes.
            model.deletingFamilies.removeAll(); await model.markPendingFamilyDeletions()
            for id in ["side","nested","sibling"] where model.record(id) != nil {
                do { _ = try await model.open(try XCTUnwrap(model.record(id))); XCTFail("\(interruption): \(id) reopened") }
                catch { XCTAssertEqual(error.localizedDescription,"This chat is being deleted.") }
            }
            model.familyDeletionStep = nil
            await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
            XCTAssertNil(model.error,interruption)
            try await assertGone(model,["main","side","nested","sibling"],owner:"main",interruption)
        }
    }

import AppKit
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

/// What came back from a question asked in the middle of a task.
enum ChatAnswer: Equatable, Sendable {
    case yes
    case no
    /// Another question was already on screen, so this one was never asked.
    case busy
}

/// A question a chat has to ask before it acts.
struct ChatQuestion: Equatable, Sendable {
    let title: String
    let detail: String
    /// The label on the button that goes ahead.
    let action: String
    /// Names listed under the detail, with notes beneath them. A question
    /// with a list is asked on the app's own sheet, where the list scrolls.
    var list: List? = nil

    struct List: Equatable, Sendable {
        struct Item: Equatable, Sendable {
            var title: String
            /// 1 for a direct child; deeper items are indented under it.
            var depth = 1
        }
        var heading: String
        var items: [Item]
        /// Plain sentences, such as what is skipped and why.
        var notes: [String] = []
        /// The rows shown; the remainder is summarized as a count.
        static let shownLimit = 200
        /// A plain-text form for an alert with no window to host the sheet.
        var plainText: String {
            var lines = items.prefix(20).map { String(repeating: "  ", count: max(0, $0.depth - 1)) + "• " + $0.title }
            if items.count > 20 { lines.append("and \(items.count - 20) more") }
            return ([heading].filter { !$0.isEmpty && !items.isEmpty } + lines + notes).joined(separator: "\n")
        }
    }
}

/// The one way the app asks a question or offers a file chooser: on a sheet
/// over a window, never in a modal run loop.
///
/// A modal loop stops the main thread. Every other window freezes, git reads
/// and terminal output stop being delivered, and AppKit re-enters its own
/// window and application callbacks from inside the one that is running. The
/// answer arrives through a completion or a continuation instead, so a caller
/// reads exactly as it did before with an `await` in front of it.
///
/// One question at a time per asker: a second request while one is up is
/// refused rather than stacked behind it, so an error cannot bury the question
/// the reader is already answering. With no window to attach a sheet to — a
/// menu-bar-only launch, a closed main window — the alert runs
/// application-modal, which is all that is left and is safe there because
/// nothing is mid-decision.
///
/// `shared` is the app-wide asker, used by the screens that keep no state of
/// their own: the inspectors, the updater, the folder chooser. A chat holds
/// its own (`WorkspaceModel.questions`) and so does the Changes panel, so a
/// question about one is only ever refused by another question about the same
/// thing.
@MainActor final class PiQuestion: ObservableObject {
    static let shared = PiQuestion()

    /// True while a question of this asker's is on screen.
    @Published private(set) var asking = false
    /// The window the last sheet went onto: a panel takes its own question
    /// down with it, and a test can see where the sheet was attached.
    private(set) weak var askedIn: NSWindow?
    private var sheetQuestion: PiQuestionPresentation?
    deinit {
        guard let question = sheetQuestion else { return }
        Task { @MainActor in question.cancel() }
    }

    /// What to say when a caller's question was refused because one is up.
    static let busyNotice = "Answer the question already on screen first."

    // MARK: Test seams
    //
    // Each of these answers a question without putting one on the screen, and
    // each is nil (or the real presenter) in the app. Nothing else in this
    // type branches on whether a test is running.

    /// Answers a `ChatQuestion` — the form whose title and detail a test reads.
    var answer: ((ChatQuestion) -> Bool)?
    /// Answers a prepared `NSAlert`, for callers that build their own.
    var answerAlert: ((NSAlert) -> NSApplication.ModalResponse)?
    /// Answers a file chooser; an empty array is a cancellation.
    var chooseFiles: (() -> [URL])?
    /// Answers the single-file chooser, given the message it would have shown.
    var chooseFile: ((String) -> URL?)?
    /// Answers the one-line text question, given its title and current value.
    var enterText: ((String, String) -> String?)?
    /// How an alert reaches the screen, so a test can drive a real sheet's
    /// completion by hand without an event loop.
    typealias Present = (NSAlert, NSWindow, @escaping (NSApplication.ModalResponse) -> Void) -> Void
    var present: Present = { alert, window, answer in alert.beginSheetModal(for: window, completionHandler: answer) }
    /// Drives a custom Pi sheet without attaching a window in behavioral tests.
    var presentSheet: ((NSView, NSWindow, @escaping (Bool) -> Void) -> Void)?

    // MARK: Which window the question belongs to

    /// The caller's own window wins. Then, for a question about one chat, the
    /// window actually showing that chat's composer, so with two windows open
    /// the question appears over the conversation it is about. Then the key
    /// window, the main window, and finally any window that can take a sheet.
    ///
    /// A window already wearing a sheet cannot take another; a sheet itself
    /// can host one, which is how Settings asks from inside its own sheet.
    static func host(_ preferred: NSWindow? = nil, showing sessionID: String? = nil) -> NSWindow? {
        func free(_ window: NSWindow) -> Bool { window.isVisible && window.attachedSheet == nil }
        // A window the caller named is taken as given: the only thing that can
        // stop a sheet going onto it is a sheet already there.
        if let preferred, preferred.attachedSheet == nil { return preferred }
        if let sessionID {
            let owners = NSApp.windows.filter { free($0) && $0.canBecomeMain && $0.sheetParent == nil }
            if let owner = owners.first(where: { window in
                window.contentView.map { shows(sessionID, in: $0) } == true
            }) { return owner }
        }
        for candidate in [NSApp.keyWindow, NSApp.mainWindow] {
            if let candidate, free(candidate) { return candidate }
        }
        return NSApp.windows.first { free($0) && ($0.canBecomeMain || $0.isSheet) }
    }
    private static func shows(_ sessionID: String, in view: NSView) -> Bool {
        if let editor = view as? ComposerTextView { return editor.sessionID == sessionID && !editor.isHiddenOrHasHiddenAncestor }
        return view.subviews.contains { shows(sessionID, in: $0) }
    }

    /// The window went away under the question; nothing was chosen.
    func cancel() {
        if let sheetQuestion { sheetQuestion.cancel() }
        else { asking = false }
    }

    // MARK: Asking

    /// A custom Pi question belongs only to its caller's window. Refuse a
    /// busy window rather than queuing the question or moving it elsewhere.
    /// Keep the presentation until its closing animation has released it.
    @discardableResult
    func askSheet(over window: NSWindow, make: (@escaping (Bool) -> Void) -> NSView,
                  answered: @escaping (Bool) -> Void) -> Bool {
        guard !asking, window.attachedSheet == nil else { return false }
        let question = PiQuestionPresentation(make: make)
        sheetQuestion = question; asking = true; askedIn = window
        question.onFinished = { [weak self, weak question] accepted in
            guard let self, let question, self.sheetQuestion === question else { return }
            self.sheetQuestion = nil; self.asking = false
            answered(accepted)
        }
        question.present(on: window, using: presentSheet)
        return true
    }

    /// Puts `alert` on a sheet and calls back with what the reader pressed.
    /// False when a question of this asker's is already on screen, so the
    /// caller can say why nothing happened.
    @discardableResult
    func ask(_ alert: NSAlert, over window: NSWindow? = nil, about sessionID: String? = nil,
             answered: @escaping (NSApplication.ModalResponse) -> Void) -> Bool {
        if let answerAlert { answered(answerAlert(alert)); return true }
        guard !asking else { return false }
        guard let host = Self.host(window, showing: sessionID) else { answered(alert.runModal()); return true }
        asking = true; askedIn = host
        present(alert, host) { [weak self] response in
            MainActor.assumeIsolated {
                self?.asking = false
                answered(response)
            }
        }
        return true
    }

    /// The awaited form of the same question. `.cancel` when one is already up.
    func ask(_ alert: NSAlert, over window: NSWindow? = nil) async -> NSApplication.ModalResponse {
        await withCheckedContinuation { (continuation: CheckedContinuation<NSApplication.ModalResponse, Never>) in
            let asked = ask(alert, over: window) { continuation.resume(returning: $0) }
            if !asked { continuation.resume(returning: .cancel) }
        }
    }

    /// A two-button question. True when the reader chose to go ahead.
    /// `cancelIsDefault` makes Return choose Cancel, for an action that
    /// destroys something the reader can't get back.
    func confirm(_ title: String, _ detail: String, action: String = "Continue", cancel: String = "Cancel",
                 destructive: Bool = false, cancelIsDefault: Bool = false, over window: NSWindow? = nil) async -> Bool {
        await ask(Self.alert(title: title, detail: detail, action: action, cancel: cancel, destructive: destructive,
                             cancelIsDefault: cancelIsDefault),
                  over: window) == .alertFirstButtonReturn
    }

    /// A chat's own question, over the window showing that chat. False when
    /// another is already up, so the caller can say why nothing happened.
    @discardableResult
    func ask(_ question: ChatQuestion, about sessionID: String? = nil, destructive: Bool = false,
             answered: @escaping (Bool) -> Void) -> Bool {
        if let answer { answered(answer(question)); return true }
        if let list = question.list {
            guard !asking else { return false }
            if let window = Self.host(nil, showing: sessionID) {
                return askSheet(over: window, make: { answer in
                    ChatQuestionSheet(question: question, list: list, destructive: destructive,
                                      parentSize: window.contentView?.bounds.size ?? window.frame.size, answer: answer)
                }, answered: answered)
            }
        }
        let detail = [question.detail, question.list?.plainText ?? ""].filter { !$0.isEmpty }.joined(separator: "\n\n")
        let alert = Self.alert(title: question.title, detail: detail, action: question.action,
                               cancel: "Cancel", destructive: destructive, warn: false)
        return ask(alert, about: sessionID) { answered($0 == .alertFirstButtonReturn) }
    }

    /// The awaited form, for a question that comes up in the middle of a task:
    /// the task suspends on the sheet and resumes with the answer instead of
    /// stopping the run loop for every other chat while it is up.
    func confirm(_ question: ChatQuestion, about sessionID: String? = nil, destructive: Bool = false) async -> ChatAnswer {
        await withCheckedContinuation { continuation in
            let asked = ask(question, about: sessionID, destructive: destructive) { continuation.resume(returning: $0 ? .yes : .no) }
            if !asked { continuation.resume(returning: .busy) }
        }
    }

    /// Asks for one line of text, seeded with what is there now. Text longer
    /// than `limit` bytes is dropped rather than truncated.
    @discardableResult
    func askText(_ title: String, value: String, action: String, limit: Int, about sessionID: String? = nil,
                 entered: @escaping (String) -> Void) -> Bool {
        if let enterText { if let text = enterText(title, value), text.utf8.count <= limit { entered(text) }; return true }
        let alert = NSAlert()
        alert.messageText = title
        let field = NSTextField(string: value)
        field.frame = NSRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 480, height: 28)
        alert.accessoryView = field
        alert.addButton(withTitle: action)
        alert.addButton(withTitle: "Cancel")
        return ask(alert, about: sessionID) { response in
            guard response == .alertFirstButtonReturn, field.stringValue.utf8.count <= limit else { return }
            entered(field.stringValue)
        }
    }

    /// The awaited form of a one-line text question, on `window`'s sheet:
    /// what was entered, or nil for Cancel or when a question is already up.
    func enterText(_ title: String, detail: String = "", value: String, action: String, over window: NSWindow? = nil) async -> String? {
        if let enterText { return enterText(title, value) }
        let alert = NSAlert()
        alert.messageText = title
        alert.informativeText = detail
        let field = NSTextField(string: value)
        field.frame = NSRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 320, height: 24)
        alert.accessoryView = field
        alert.addButton(withTitle: action)
        alert.addButton(withTitle: "Cancel")
        alert.window.initialFirstResponder = field
        return await ask(alert, over: window) == .alertFirstButtonReturn ? field.stringValue : nil
    }

    // MARK: Choosing files

    /// Where to write a file, or nil when the reader cancelled or a question
    /// of this asker's is already on screen.
    func save(_ panel: NSSavePanel, over window: NSWindow? = nil) async -> URL? {
        if let chooseFiles { return chooseFiles().first }
        guard let response = await run(panel, over: window, about: nil) else { return nil }
        return response == .OK ? panel.url : nil
    }

    /// What the reader chose, or an empty array when they cancelled.
    func open(_ panel: NSOpenPanel, over window: NSWindow? = nil) async -> [URL] {
        if let chooseFiles { return chooseFiles() }
        guard let response = await run(panel, over: window, about: nil) else { return [] }
        return response == .OK ? panel.urls : []
    }

    /// Chooses one folder or file. `directories` picks a working directory;

    /// Commands on a chat by its id, whether it is open here or not.
    private func namedSessionCommand(_ method:String, id:String, params:JSON, directory:URL) async throws -> JSON? {
        if method.hasPrefix("debug.") { return try await traces.command(method,session:id,params:params) }
        switch method {
        case "session.releaseDependents":
            return try await withRuntimeGate {
                let family = try await loadedBudgetFamily(id,directory:directory)
                // Inspect every runtime before releasing any writer. This also
                // catches hidden owners/readers and work between HTTP requests.
                for (value, _) in family.sorted(by: { $0.1 > $1.1 }) {
                    guard await value.canUnload else { throw busyBudgetMember(value.id,names:params["names"]) }
                }
                var released: [JSON] = []
                for (value, _) in family.sorted(by: { $0.1 > $1.1 }) where value.id != id {
                    guard await value.unloadIfIdle() else { throw busyBudgetMember(value.id,names:params["names"]) }
                    await didUnloadBudgetMember(value)
                    released.append(JSON(value.id))
                }
                return ["accepted":true,"released":.array(released)]
            }
        case "session.forget":
            try await withRuntimeGate {
                let family = try await loadedBudgetFamily(id,directory:directory)
                // Preflight the whole family before detaching any writer.
                for (value, _) in family {
                    guard await value.canUnload else { throw AgentError("session_busy","Stop work and close/keep side chats before forgetting") }
                }
                // The app deletes a chat with every saved side below it. Its
                // descendants are not survivors, so nothing is mirrored down
                // into them; a member forgotten after its deleted root's
                // repair has no surviving ancestor left to repair either.
                let descendantsDeleted = params["descendantsDeleted"].flag == true
                let ancestorsRepaired = params["ancestorsRepaired"].flag == true
                // Deleting a side can remove the only priced/token evidence
                // after its ancestor's append failed. Repair surviving owners
                // before downward mirrors, detachment, capture clearing or Trash.
                let ancestors = ancestorsRepaired ? [] : try await Task.detached { try SideBudgetRecovery.ancestors(of:id,directory:directory) }.value
                for ancestor in ancestors.reversed() {
                    let records = try await Task.detached { try SideBudgetRecovery.deletionRecords(side:id,ancestor:ancestor,directory:directory) }.value
                    let committed: [JSON]
                    if let runtime = sessions[ancestor] {
                        try await runtime.importDeletionRecords(records)
                        committed = await runtime.committedSideCharges()
                    } else {
                        committed = try await Task.detached {
                            try SideBudgetRecovery.importDeletionRecords(records,owner:ancestor,directory:directory)
                            return try SideBudgetRecovery.committedSideCharges(owner:ancestor,directory:directory)
                        }.value
                    }
                    try await mirrorFamilyCharges(committed,owner:ancestor,directory:directory)
                }
                if !descendantsDeleted {
                    // Older callers: surviving saved sides reopen as independent chats.
                    let costs: [JSON]
                    if let owner = sessions[id] { costs = await owner.committedSideCharges() }
                    else { costs = try await Task.detached { try SideBudgetRecovery.committedSideCharges(owner:id,directory:directory) }.value }
                    try await mirrorFamilyCharges(costs,owner:id,directory:directory)
                }
                for (value, _) in family.sorted(by: { $0.1 > $1.1 }) {
                    guard await value.unloadIfIdle() else { throw AgentError("session_busy","Stop work and close/keep side chats before forgetting") }
                    await didUnloadBudgetMember(value)
                }
            }
            _=try await traces.command("debug.clear",session:id,params:[:]); return ["accepted":true]
        default: return nil
        }
    }
    /// Runtime ancestry governs loaded sides; persisted ancestry also finds a
    /// side-owned child reader opened without its direct owner's runtime.
    /// Call under the runtime gate. Pane placement never defines this family.
    private func loadedBudgetFamily(_ id: String, directory: URL) async throws -> [(AgentSession,Int)] {
        var family: [(AgentSession,Int)] = []
        var owners: Set<String> = [id]
        for value in sessions.values {
            let parents = await value.sideBudgetIDs
            if value.id == id || parents.contains(id) { family.append((value,parents.count)); owners.insert(value.id) }
        }
        var coldOwners: [String:Bool] = [:]
        for value in sessions.values where !owners.contains(value.id) {
            guard let owner = await value.subagents.origin?.link.budgetOwnerSessionID else { continue }
            if !owners.contains(owner), coldOwners[owner] == nil {
                // Only a loaded reader's direct ancestry is relevant to release.
                // Do not enumerate unrelated saved sides on the host actor.
                coldOwners[owner] = try await Task.detached {
                    try SideBudgetRecovery.ancestors(of:owner,directory:directory).contains(id)
                }.value
            }
            if owners.contains(owner) || coldOwners[owner] == true { family.append((value,129)) }
        }
        return family.sorted { $0.0.id < $1.0.id }
    }
    private func busyBudgetMember(_ id: String, names: JSON) -> AgentError {
        let name = String((names[id].text ?? id).prefix(120))
        return AgentError("side_budget_busy","Wait for “\(name)” to finish its work and queued messages before changing this chat.")
    }
    private func didUnloadBudgetMember(_ value: AgentSession) async {
        sessions.removeValue(forKey:value.id); profiles.removeValue(forKey:value.id); subagentReaders.remove(value.id)
        dirty.removeValue(forKey:value.id)
        emit(["v":1,"kind":"event","hostEpoch":JSON(epoch),"sessionId":JSON(value.id),"seq":JSON(await value.sequence),"type":"session.unloaded","payload":[:]])
    }
    /// Commands on an open chat that a quiesced workspace still answers:
    /// reads, a stop, and a side kept or closed. A fork and a new side
    /// refuse a quiesced workspace themselves.
    private func sessionCommand(_ method:String, id:String, session:AgentSession, params:JSON, in workspace:OpenWorkspace) async throws -> JSON? {
        let resources=workspace.resources, nativeTools=workspace.nativeTools
        try await session.requireSubagentCommand(method)
        switch method {
        case "budget.attempts": return try await session.budgetAttempts(after: params["after"].text)
        case "budget.reconcile":
            return try await withRuntimeGate {
                let result = try await session.reconcileCapturedAccounting(params["evidence"].list, mirrorChildren:false)
                let ids = params["evidence"].list.compactMap { $0["attemptId"].text }
                let costs = await session.sharedCharges(ids)
                try await mirrorFamilyCharges(costs,owner:id,directory:session.directory)
                return result
            }
        case "subagent.list":
            let cursor = try boundedInt(params["cursor"], maximum: Int.max), limit = try boundedInt(params["limit"], fallback: 100, maximum: 256)
            let coordinator = subagentCoordinator ?? SubagentCoordinator()
            var page = await coordinator.list(parent: session, cursor: cursor, limit: limit)
            // Opt-in desktop projection. The coordinator's model-facing
            // list/progress/result contract is unchanged.
            if params["includeCosts"].flag == true {
                let children = page["children"].list
                page["costs"] = try await withRuntimeGate {
                    guard sessions[id] === session else { throw AgentError(AgentErrorCode.sessionMissing,"Session runtime is not loaded") }
                    let plans = await session.subagents.plans
                    var result: JSON = [:]
                    for summary in children {
                        guard let delegation = summary["link"]["delegationID"].text, let plan = plans[delegation],
                              plan.origin.link.budgetOwnerSessionID == id else { continue }
                        let childID = plan.origin.link.childSessionID
                        if let child = sessions[childID] { result[childID] = await child.costSnapshot }
                        else { result[childID] = try await childBudgetReadings.read(plan:plan,directory:session.directory,allowMissing:summary["status"].text == "not_executed") }
                    }
                    return result
                }
            }
            return page
        case "subagent.stop":
            if let coordinator = await session.agentCoordinator, await session.subagents.family != nil {
                let target = AgentTurnTarget(childID:try required(params["childId"],"child id"),turn:try boundedInt(params["turn"],fallback:0,maximum:Int.max))
                try await coordinator.stop(parent:session,target:target,human:true); return ["accepted":true]
            }
            guard let subagentCoordinator else { throw AgentError("subagent_scope", "No managed delegation belongs to this parent") }
            try await subagentCoordinator.stop(owner: id, delegationID: required(params["delegationId"], "delegation id")); return ["accepted":true]
        case "turn.stop":
            if let origin = await session.subagents.origin, let owner = sessions[origin.link.budgetOwnerSessionID],
               let coordinator = await owner.agentCoordinator, let turn = await owner.agentState().latest(id) {
                try await coordinator.stop(parent:owner,target:turn.target,human:true)
            }
packages/swift-host/Sources/PiAgentCore/SideBudgetDeletion.swift:7:    static func ancestors(of id: String, directory: URL) throws -> [String] {
