    func keepSide(_ id: String) {
        guard let info = side(id), !info.kept, !info.keeping, !info.pending, let view = displays[id], !view.loading, hosts[info.workspaceID] != nil else { return }
        guard view.hasWork else { performKeepSide(id, whenFinished: false); return }
        // Asked on the window showing this side, so the run it is about keeps
        // streaming behind the question instead of freezing with it.
        let question = ChatQuestion(title: "Keep this side when it finishes?",
                                    detail: "Save it as a separate read-only chat when its current work and queue reach idle. Your parent chat stays unchanged.",
                                    action: "Keep When Finished")
        if !questions.ask(question, about: id, answered: { [weak self] keep in
            guard let self, keep else { return }
            self.performKeepSide(id, whenFinished: true)
        }) { view.notice = PiQuestion.busyNotice }
    }
    private func performKeepSide(_ id: String, whenFinished: Bool) {
        guard let info = side(id), !info.kept, !info.keeping, !info.pending, let view = displays[id], !view.loading, let host = hosts[info.workspaceID] else { return }
        view.loading = true
        Task { defer {
            view.loading = false
            updateHostActivity(workspaceID: info.workspaceID)
            if let host = hosts[info.workspaceID] { scheduleIdle(workspaceID: info.workspaceID, host: host) }
        }; do {
            guard let store else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
            var chat = info.chat
            chat.path = root.appendingPathComponent("Workspaces/\(info.workspaceID)/Sessions/side_\(id).jsonl").path
            try await store.put(SideKeepIntent(chat: chat), kind: "side-keep", id: id)
            let result = try await host.request("side.keep", sessionID: id, params: ["whenFinished": .bool(whenFinished)]).object ?? [:]
            if result["ephemeral"]?.bool == false { try await registerKeptSide(id: id, path: result["path"]?.string) }
            else { sides[info.parentID]?.keepRequested = true }
            refresh(id)
        } catch { view.notice = error.localizedDescription; self.error = error.localizedDescription } }
    }
    func applySideStatus(id: String, result: [String: WireValue]) async {
        guard let info = side(id) else { return }
        if let boundary = result["side"]?.object, boundary != info.boundary { sides[info.parentID]?.boundary = boundary }
        let keeping = result["keeping"]?.bool ?? false, requested = result["keepRequested"]?.bool ?? false
        if info.keeping != keeping { sides[info.parentID]?.keeping = keeping }
        if info.keepRequested != requested { sides[info.parentID]?.keepRequested = requested }
        if result["ephemeral"]?.bool == false && !info.kept {
            do { try await registerKeptSide(id: id, path: result["path"]?.string) }
            catch {
                // Registration can be retried from the preserved recovery
                // intent. It must not prevent this snapshot from updating the
                // transcript, run state, Stop control and queue afterwards.
                await reportSideKeepFailure(id: id, title: info.title, error: error)
            }
        }
    }
    /// Two spellings of one file are the same file. The app writes the path it
    /// intends into the recovery intent; the helper answers with the path it
    /// actually wrote, resolved. Under a symlinked root — `/tmp`, a home on a
    /// mounted volume — those two strings differ although nothing about them
    /// disagrees, and comparing them as text refused to register a side that
    /// had in fact been written exactly where it was asked for.
    private static func sameFile(_ lhs: String?, _ rhs: String?) -> Bool {
        guard let lhs, let rhs else { return false }
        if lhs == rhs { return true }
        func settled(_ value: String) -> String {
            URL(fileURLWithPath: value).standardizedFileURL.resolvingSymlinksInPath().path
        }
        return settled(lhs) == settled(rhs)
    }
    func registerKeptSide(id: String, path: String?) async throws {
        guard let store, let path else { throw HostError.failure("Keep could not match this chat to its saved file.") }
        let intent: SideKeepIntent
        switch try await store.sideKeepState(id: id) {
        case .pending(let pending): intent = pending
        case .registered(let chat, let expected):
            guard Self.sameFile(path, chat.path) else {
                throw HostError.failure("Keep could not match this chat to its saved file.")
            }
            try await history.validateIdentity(path: path, id: id)
            await finishSideKeepRegistration(chat, expected: expected)
            return
        case .missing:
            throw HostError.failure("Keep could not match this chat to its saved file.")
        }
        guard Self.sameFile(path, intent.chat.path) else {
            throw HostError.failure("Keep could not match this chat to its saved file.")
        }
        try await history.validateIdentity(path: path, id: id)
        let view = displays[id]
        let draft: DraftRecord
        if let view { draft = view.savedDraft }
        else { draft = try await store.get(DraftRecord.self, kind: "draft", id: id) ?? DraftRecord(id: id, text: "") }
        var retained = intent.chat
        // The file as the helper names it, which is how every snapshot will
        // name it. The app's own spelling of the same file (/private/tmp for
        // /tmp, a path through a symlink) had the first snapshot rename the
        // chat's file while it was being opened.
        retained.path = path
        if let existing = chats.first(where: { $0.id == id }) { retained.applyOrganization(from: existing) }
        else if let parentID = retained.parentSessionID, let parent = record(parentID) {
            // A parent may move while the helper publishes this saved side.
            retained.topicID = effectiveTopicID(for: parent)
        } else { retained.topicID = effectiveTopicID(for: retained) }
        if let profile = profiles.first(where: { $0.id == retained.profileID }) { retained.migrateOutputBudget(profile: profile) }
        // The transaction resolves a concurrent parent move and returns its
        // durable organization instead of the stale recovery intent's group.
        retained = try await store.commitKeptSide(retained, draft: draft, expected: intent.chat)
        await finishSideKeepRegistration(retained, expected: intent.chat)
    }
    private func finishSideKeepRegistration(_ saved: ChatRecord, expected: ChatRecord) async {
        var retained = saved
        let id = retained.id
        retained.topicID = effectiveTopicID(for: retained)
        if let index = chats.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
            if (retained.organizationRevision ?? 0) >= (chats[index].organizationRevision ?? 0) {
                chats[index].applyOrganization(from: retained)
            }
        } else { chats.insert(retained, at: 0) }
        if let info = side(id) { sides[info.parentID]?.kept = true; sides[info.parentID]?.keeping = false; sides[info.parentID]?.keepRequested = false; sides[info.parentID]?.topicID = chats.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.topicID }
        await retainSideReadState(id)
        sideKeepResolutionRevision &+= 1
        sideKeepIssues.removeAll { $0.chat == expected }
        do { try await restoreSideBudgets() }
        catch { self.error = "The chat was saved, but its family cost totals could not be refreshed: " + error.localizedDescription }
    }
    func reconcileSideKeeps() async {
        guard !reconcilingSideKeeps else { return }
        reconcilingSideKeeps = true; defer { reconcilingSideKeeps = false }
        do {
            while true {
                let revision = sideKeepResolutionRevision
                var issues: [SideKeepIssue] = []
                for intent in try await store?.list(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep") ?? [] {
                    do {
                        guard let path = intent.chat.path else { throw HostError.failure("No file location was saved for this chat.") }
                        try await registerKeptSide(id: intent.chat.id, path: path)
                    } catch {
                        // A missing file may still be awaiting publication. Keep
                        // the registration until it succeeds or is dismissed.
                        if let current = try await store?.get(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep", id: intent.chat.id), current.chat == intent.chat {
                            issues.append(sideKeepIssue(intent, error: error))
                        }
                    }
                }
                // An answer or live keep may settle an earlier item while a later
                // file is checked. Re-read rather than resurrecting its old notice.
                guard revision == sideKeepResolutionRevision else { continue }
                sideKeepIssues = issues.sorted { $0.id < $1.id }
                break
            }

extension SideBudgetRecovery {
    /// Follow only surviving, validated side origins. A missing parent ends
    /// ownership; fork provenance and recovered independent copies do not
    /// authorize accounting writes. Discovery of unrelated files stays isolated.
    static func ancestors(of id: String, directory: URL) throws -> [String] {
        var result: [String] = [], next = id, seen: Set<String> = [id]
        while let file = try journalURL(next,directory:directory) {
            // Unrelated/damaged history can still be explicitly deleted. As
            // with discovery, only an established side origin authorizes an
            // upward write; its ledger is read strictly by deletionRecords.
            guard file.lastPathComponent == "side_" + next + ".jsonl",
                  let origin = try? readOrigin(file) else { break }
            guard origin.id == next, seen.insert(origin.parent).inserted, result.count < 128 else { throw AttemptAccounting.damaged() }
            guard try parentExists(origin.parent,directory:directory) else { break }
            result.append(origin.parent); next = origin.parent
        }
        return result
    }

    /// Before a side's sole surviving evidence can be removed, adopt its own
    /// ledger and descendants through the same merge used by cold opening.
    /// Numeric records only; no conversation or capture content is copied.
    static func deletionRecords(side: String, ancestor: String, directory: URL) throws -> [AccountingRecord] {
        let family = try savedFamily(owner:ancestor,directory:directory).filter {
            $0.origin.id == side || $0.parents.contains(side)
        }
        guard family.contains(where: { $0.origin.id == side }) else { throw AttemptAccounting.damaged() }
        return try charges(owner:ancestor,directory:directory,family:family)
    }

    static func importDeletionRecords(_ records: [AccountingRecord], owner: String, directory: URL) throws {
        guard let file = try journalURL(owner,directory:directory) else { throw AttemptAccounting.damaged() }
        let writer = try SessionJournal(url:file,id:owner,cwd:directory,binding:nil,create:false)
        let reader = try JournalRecordReader(file)
        var accounting = AttemptAccounting(), spend = SessionSpend(), children = SubagentJournalState()
        while let row = try reader.next() {
            try accounting.consumeRecord(row,sessionID:owner,spend:&spend)
            try children.consume(row,sessionID:owner)
        }
        guard children.origin == nil else { throw AttemptAccounting.damaged() }
        for incoming in records {
            guard var update = try adoption(incoming,accounting:accounting) else { continue }
            update.data["accountingRevision"] = JSON(accounting.revision + 1)
            try accounting.consume(kind:update.kind,data:update.data,spend:&spend)
            try writer.append(["type":"custom","customType":JSON(update.kind),"data":AttemptAccounting.journalData(update.data)],flush:true)
        }
    }
}

extension AgentSession {
    /// The host's runtime gate validates persisted ancestry and owns discovery.
    /// A loaded ancestor keeps its writer and publishes repaired spend normally.
    func importDeletionRecords(_ records: [SideBudgetRecovery.AccountingRecord]) throws {
        guard !isSubagent, !closed else { throw AttemptAccounting.damaged() }
        for incoming in records {
            guard var update = try SideBudgetRecovery.adoption(incoming,accounting:accounting) else { continue }
            update.data["accountingRevision"] = JSON(accounting.revision + 1)
            try appendAccounting(kind:update.kind,data:update.data)
            familyBudgetEnabled = true
        }
        stopFamilyAtLimit()
    }

            // one switches now, a running one when its run ends.

                sessions.removeValue(forKey:id); profiles.removeValue(forKey:id); subagentReaders.remove(id)
                return ["accepted":true]
            }
        default: return nil
        }
    }
    /// Call only while holding the runtime gate, so disk and live mirrors can
    /// never acquire competing writers between discovery and persistence.
    private func mirrorFamilyCharges(_ costs: [JSON], owner id: String, directory: URL) async throws {
        guard !costs.isEmpty else { return }
        // Loaded readers need the same accepted-origin authority as disk
        // mirrors. Resolve each needed direct owner once, even without its
        // runtime. Discarded sides with no surviving reader need no authority.
        var plans: [String:[String:SubagentPlan]] = [:]
        for child in sessions.values where child.id != id {
            let managed = await child.isSubagent
            for cost in costs {
                var accepted: SubagentOrigin?
                if managed, cost["sourceSessionId"].text == child.id {
                    guard let delegation = cost["delegationId"].text, let owner = cost["budgetOwnerSessionId"].text else { throw AttemptAccounting.damaged() }
                    if plans[owner] == nil {
                        if let session = sessions[owner] { plans[owner] = await session.subagents.plans }
                        else { plans[owner] = try await Task.detached { try SideBudgetRecovery.childPlans(owner:owner,directory:directory) }.value }
                    }
                    guard let plan = plans[owner]?[delegation] else { throw AttemptAccounting.damaged() }
                    accepted = plan.origin
                }
                try await child.mirrorSharedCharge(cost,acceptedOrigin:accepted)
            }
        }
        let loaded = Set(sessions.keys)
        let sideReadings = try await Task.detached { try SideBudgetRecovery.mirrorCapturedCosts(costs,owner:id,directory:directory,loaded:loaded) }.value
        for (child,cost) in sideReadings { publishChildCost(cost,id:child) }
        var mirrors: [(SubagentPlan,URL,[JSON])] = []
        for owner in sessions.values {
            let plans = await owner.subagents.plans
            var batches: [String:[JSON]] = [:]
            for cost in costs {
                guard let delegation = cost["delegationId"].text,
                      let plan = plans[delegation], sessions[plan.origin.link.childSessionID] == nil else { continue }
                batches[delegation,default:[]].append(cost)
            }
            for (delegation,batch) in batches { mirrors.append((plans[delegation]!,owner.directory,batch)) }
        }
        let beforeMirror = beforeChildCostMirror
        let readings = try await Task.detached {
            try mirrors.map { plan, directory, costs in
                beforeMirror?()
                return (plan.origin.link.childSessionID,try SubagentRecovery.mirrorCapturedCosts(costs,plan:plan,directory:directory))
            }
        }.value
        for (child,cost) in readings { publishChildCost(cost,id:child) }
    }

    private func hasBudgetDependents(_ id: String) async -> Bool {
        for session in sessions.values where session.id != id {
            if await session.sideBudgetIDs.contains(id) { return true }
            if let owner = await session.budgetOwner, owner.id == id, !(await session.canUnload) { return true }
        }
        return false
    }
    /// `roots` (1…16 absolute directories, primary first) or the legacy single
    /// `cwd`. Duplicates collapse after canonicalization; order is preserved.
    static func workspaceRoots(_ params: JSON) throws -> [URL] {
        if params["roots"].isNull { return [canonical(try required(params["cwd"],"workspace"))] }
        let list=params["roots"].list
        guard !list.isEmpty, list.count <= 16 else { throw AgentError("invalid_params", "Workspace roots must list 1 to 16 directories") }
        var result: [URL]=[], seen=Set<String>()
        for item in list {
            let path=try required(item,"workspace root")
            guard path.hasPrefix("/") else { throw AgentError("invalid_params", "Workspace roots must be absolute paths") }
            let url=canonical(path); if seen.insert(url.path).inserted { result.append(url) }
        }
        if let cwd=params["cwd"].text, !cwd.isEmpty, canonical(cwd) != result.first { throw AgentError("invalid_params", "cwd must be the primary workspace root") }
        return result
    }
    /// Optional per-turn model, thinking, model-specific capacity and input overrides.
    static func turnOverrides(_ params: JSON) throws -> (model: String?, thinkingLevel: String?, contextWindow: Int?, maxOutputTokens: Int?, modelOutputLimit: Int?, input: [String]?) {
        var model: String?, level: String?
        if !params["model"].isNull {
            guard let text=params["model"].text, !text.isEmpty, text.utf8.count <= 200, !text.utf8.contains(where: { $0 < 32 || $0 == 127 }) else { throw AgentError("invalid_params", "Invalid model override") }
            model=text
        }
        if !params["thinkingLevel"].isNull {
            guard let text=params["thinkingLevel"].text, Profile.thinkingLevels.contains(text) else { throw AgentError("invalid_params", "Invalid thinking level override") }
            level=text
        }
        func limit(_ name: String, maximum: Int) throws -> Int? {
            if params[name].isNull { return nil }
            guard let value=params[name].int, value > 0, value <= maximum else { throw AgentError("invalid_params", "Invalid turn \(name) override") }
            return value
        }
        return (model,level,try limit("contextWindow",maximum:10_000_000),try limit("maxOutputTokens",maximum:1_000_000),try limit("modelOutputLimit",maximum:1_000_000),try turnInput(params["input"]))

struct DiscoveredSubagent: Sendable {
    var header: SubagentHeader
    var path: String
    var task: String
    var status: SubagentStatus
    var issue: String?
    var hasTerminalRecord = false
    var turn: Int? = nil
    var executionID: String? = nil
    /// Rebuildable UI ordinal in the owner's persisted acceptance order.
    var number: Int? = nil
    var effort: String? = nil
}

extension HistoryReader {
    /// Match helper recovery's SubagentOrigin.matchesAccepted rule without
    /// dropping fields absent from the desktop's smaller SubagentHeader type.
    /// Older helpers omit optional model capabilities; no other origin field
    /// or explicit capability difference is compatible.
    static func subagentOriginMatchesAccepted(_ mirror: WireValue, accepted: WireValue?) -> Bool {
        guard let accepted, let owner = accepted.object, var child = mirror.object,
              let ownerModel = owner["model"]?.object, var childModel = child["model"]?.object else { return false }
        if childModel["reasoning"] == nil || childModel["reasoning"] == .null { childModel["reasoning"] = ownerModel["reasoning"] }
        child["model"] = .object(childModel)
        return .object(child) == accepted
    }

    static func subagentPlan(_ row: [String: WireValue]) -> [String: WireValue]? {
        switch row["customType"]?.string {
        case "pi-app.subagent-call.v1": row["data"]?.object
        case "pi-app.agent-operation.v1": row["data"]?.object?["plan"]?.object
        default: nil
        }
    }

    func validateSubagentDeletion(path: URL, directory: URL, expected: SubagentTarget) throws {
        guard path.resolvingSymlinksInPath().deletingLastPathComponent().path == directory.resolvingSymlinksInPath().path,
              path.lastPathComponent == expected.sessionID + ".jsonl" else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
        var rows: [[String: WireValue]] = []
        try subagentRecords(path) { row, line in rows.append(row); return line < 3 }
        guard rows.count >= 2, rows[0]["type"]?.string == "session", rows[0]["version"]?.number == 3, rows[0]["id"]?.string == expected.sessionID,
              ["pi-app.native.v2", "pi-app.native.v3"].contains(rows[1]["customType"]?.string ?? ""), rows[1]["data"]?.object?["version"]?.number == 2 else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
        if rows.count > 2 { _ = try subagentHeader(path: path, directory: directory, expected: expected) }
    }
    /// The owning journal is the authority even before a child file exists.
    func subagentPlans(path: URL, ownerID: String, childID: String? = nil, includeInherited: Bool = false) throws -> [DiscoveredSubagent] {
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        let revision = Self.currentRevision(path: path.path)
        func matches(_ plan: DiscoveredSubagent) -> Bool {
            (includeInherited || plan.header.link.budgetOwnerSessionID == ownerID) &&
                (childID == nil || plan.header.link.childSessionID == childID)
        }
        if let revision, let cached = subagentPlanCache[path.path], cached.owner == ownerID, cached.revision == revision {
            subagentPlanRecency.removeAll { $0 == path.path }; subagentPlanRecency.append(path.path)
            return cached.plans.filter(matches)
        }
        var found: [DiscoveredSubagent] = [], starts: [String: SubagentLink] = [:]
        try subagentRecords(path) { row, line in
            if line == 1 { guard row["type"]?.string == "session", row["id"]?.string == ownerID, row["version"]?.number == 3 else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord } }
            if let data = Self.subagentPlan(row),
               let raw = data["origin"], let origin = try? JSONDecoder().decode(SubagentHeader.self, from: JSONEncoder().encode(raw)),
               origin.link.valid, UUID(uuidString: origin.link.childSessionID) != nil {
                let id = origin.link.childSessionID
                if let previous = starts[id] {
                    guard previous == origin.link else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
                    return true
                }
                starts[id] = origin.link
                // Count every unique start, even when filtering or when its
                // child file is missing. Follow-ups never contain a new plan.
                // Full scans retain every start, so inheritance/filter choices
                // share one revision cache. Targeted cold lookups still stop
                // at their child and never cache a partial set of plans.
                if let childID {
                    guard (includeInherited || origin.link.budgetOwnerSessionID == ownerID), childID == id else { return true }
                }
                let child = path.deletingLastPathComponent().appendingPathComponent(origin.link.childSessionID + ".jsonl")
                guard child.resolvingSymlinksInPath().deletingLastPathComponent() == path.resolvingSymlinksInPath().deletingLastPathComponent() else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
                found.append(DiscoveredSubagent(header: origin, path: child.path, task: data["task"]?.string ?? "", status: .interrupted, number: starts.count))
                if childID != nil { return false }
            }
            return true
        }
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        if childID == nil, let revision, Self.currentRevision(path: path.path) == revision {
            subagentPlanCache[path.path] = (ownerID, revision, found)
            subagentPlanRecency.removeAll { $0 == path.path }; subagentPlanRecency.append(path.path)
            while subagentPlanRecency.count > 8 {
                subagentPlanCache.removeValue(forKey: subagentPlanRecency.removeFirst())
            }
        }
        return found.filter(matches)
    }
    /// Streaming scan: retain only origins/plans/status, never transcript or
    /// capture bodies. A torn final line is left for the helper's recovery.
    func subagentRecords(_ path: URL, visit: ([String: WireValue], Int) throws -> Bool) throws {
        let file = try FileHandle(forReadingFrom: path); defer { try? file.close() }
        var pending = Data(), line = 0
        let needles = ["pi-app.subagent-", "pi-app.agent-", "pi-app.family-run"].map { Data($0.utf8) }
        while let bytes = try file.read(upToCount: 65_536), !bytes.isEmpty {
            subagentScannedBytes += bytes.count
            try Task.checkCancellation()
            var start = bytes.startIndex
            while let end = bytes[start...].firstIndex(of: 10) {
                pending.append(bytes[start..<end]); start = end + 1
                guard pending.count <= 33_554_432 else { throw StoreError.unreadableRecord }
                line += 1
                if line <= 3 || needles.contains(where: { pending.range(of: $0) != nil }) {
                    let row = try JSONDecoder().decode([String: WireValue].self, from: pending)
                    if try !visit(row, line) { return }
                }
                pending.removeAll(keepingCapacity: true)
            }
            pending.append(bytes[start...])
            guard pending.count <= 33_554_432 else { throw StoreError.unreadableRecord }
        }
    }

    /// Existing journals are readable even with rollout off. A missing or
    /// inconsistent reciprocal plan becomes an input-free recovery view.
    func discoverSubagents(directory: URL, parents: [ChatRecord]) throws -> [DiscoveredSubagent] {
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: directory.path) else { return [] }
        let byID = Dictionary(parents.filter { !$0.isSubagent && !$0.imported }.map { ($0.id, $0) }, uniquingKeysWith: { first, _ in first })
        var plans: [String: [String: WireValue]] = [:], checked: Set<String> = []
        var numbers: [String: [String: Int]] = [:]
        func plan(_ owner: String) throws -> [String: WireValue] {
            if checked.insert(owner).inserted {
                guard let parent = byID[owner], let saved = parent.path else { return [:] }
                let path = URL(fileURLWithPath: saved)
                guard path.resolvingSymlinksInPath().deletingLastPathComponent() == directory.resolvingSymlinksInPath() else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
                try subagentRecords(path) { row, line in
                    if line == 1 { guard row["type"]?.string == "session", row["id"]?.string == owner, row["version"]?.number == 3 else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord } }
                    if let data = Self.subagentPlan(row),
                       let raw = data["origin"], let origin = try? JSONDecoder().decode(SubagentHeader.self, from: JSONEncoder().encode(raw)),
                       origin.link.valid {
                        let id = origin.link.childSessionID
                        if numbers[owner]?[id] == nil { numbers[owner, default: [:]][id] = (numbers[owner]?.count ?? 0) + 1 }
                        guard origin.link.budgetOwnerSessionID == owner else { return true }
                        if let previous = plans[owner]?[id], previous != .object(data) { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
                        plans[owner, default: [:]][id] = .object(data)
                    }
                    return true
                }
            }
            return plans[owner] ?? [:]
        }
        var found: [DiscoveredSubagent] = []
        for path in try FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(at: directory, includingPropertiesForKeys: nil).sorted(by: { $0.lastPathComponent < $1.lastPathComponent }) where path.pathExtension == "jsonl" {
            guard UUID(uuidString: path.deletingPathExtension().lastPathComponent) != nil,
                  path.resolvingSymlinksInPath().deletingLastPathComponent() == directory.resolvingSymlinksInPath() else { continue }
            var prefix: [[String: WireValue]] = [], status: SubagentStatus = .interrupted, terminal = false
            var latestTurn: Int?, executionID: String?, effort: String?
            do {
                try subagentRecords(path) { row, line in
                    if line <= 3 { prefix.append(row) }
                    if line == 2, !["pi-app.native.v2", "pi-app.native.v3"].contains(row["customType"]?.string ?? "") { return false }
                    if row["customType"]?.string == "pi-app.subagent-terminal.v1", prefix.count == 3,
                       let data = row["data"]?.object,
                       data["delegationID"] == prefix[2]["data"]?.object?["link"]?.object?["delegationID"],
                       let value = data["status"]?.string.flatMap(SubagentStatus.init(rawValue:)), value.terminal { status = value; terminal = true }
                    let kind = row["customType"]?.string ?? ""
                    let turnData = kind == "pi-app.agent-operation.v1" ? row["data"]?.object?["turn"]?.object : row["data"]?.object
                    if ["pi-app.agent-operation.v1", "pi-app.subagent-turn.v1", "pi-app.subagent-turn-state.v1", "pi-app.subagent-turn-terminal.v1"].contains(kind),
                       let data = turnData, data["childID"]?.string == path.deletingPathExtension().lastPathComponent,
                       let number = data["turn"]?.nonnegativeInteger, number > 0, number >= (latestTurn ?? 0) {
                        if number != latestTurn { terminal = false; status = .interrupted; effort = nil }
                        latestTurn = number; executionID = data["executionID"]?.string
                        // State/terminal mirrors need not repeat the accepted
                        // turn's effort. Never replace it with an absent field.
                        if let accepted = data["effort"]?.string { effort = accepted }
                        if kind == "pi-app.subagent-turn-terminal.v1", let value = data["status"]?.string.flatMap(SubagentStatus.init(rawValue:)), value.terminal {
                            status = value; terminal = true
                        }
                    }
                    return true
                }
                guard prefix.count == 3, let raw = prefix[2]["data"], let header = try? JSONDecoder().decode(SubagentHeader.self, from: JSONEncoder().encode(raw)) else { continue }
                _ = try subagentHeader(path: path, directory: directory, expected: SubagentTarget(header.link))
                let accepted = (try? plan(header.link.budgetOwnerSessionID))?[header.link.childSessionID]?.object
                let issue = Self.subagentOriginMatchesAccepted(raw,accepted:accepted?["origin"]) ? nil : "The parent’s matching delegation is unavailable. This saved sub-agent is view-only."
                var effective = header
                if issue == nil, effective.model.reasoning == nil {
                    effective.model.reasoning = accepted?["origin"]?.object?["model"]?.object?["reasoning"]?.array?.compactMap(\.string)
                }
                found.append(DiscoveredSubagent(header: effective, path: path.path, task: accepted?["task"]?.string ?? "", status: status, issue: issue, hasTerminalRecord: terminal, turn: latestTurn, executionID: executionID, number: numbers[header.link.budgetOwnerSessionID]?[header.link.childSessionID], effort: effort))
            } catch is CancellationError { throw CancellationError() }
            catch { continue } // Invalid headers remain untouched; helper recovery reports their exact error.
        }
        return found
    }
}

# Family deletion and missing-project deletion (0.1.129)

Branch `dev/a129-family-delete`, based on `dev/next` 9d4123f6 (released 0.1.128).

## Owner decisions implemented (2026-10-16)

1. **Deleting a main chat deletes its whole family.** One Delete moves the chat, its saved sides at every depth and all of their managed sub-agents to Trash together. The confirmation lists the sides that go with it. Deleting a single side deletes that side and its descendants. Spend from a deleted side stays counted in the main chat ("Keep it counted").
2. **A chat whose project is missing can be deleted.** The restore-first requirement is gone. Deletion removes only the app's own chat records and files. Anything that needed the project folder is skipped and named.

## Behavior

- **Members.** `MetadataStore.savedSideDescendants(of:workspaceID:)` walks the complete durable chat graph, not just the 10,000-chat window. It is joined with any loaded record that the store has not caught up with yet. Membership follows `persistedBudgetParentID` (a `side_<id>.jsonl` journal plus `parentSessionID`). Forks are only provenance and are never included. Sub-agents are collected per member from the journal plans and the metadata, after the runtimes have detached, as before.
- **One durable intent.** Every member is written to `session-delete` / `session-deleted` under the root's owner ID in one transaction. Side members carry `sideDepth`, which is the only thing that lets a non-owner member use a `side_<id>.jsonl` name. A side ID must be one plain name. Managed children keep their strict UUID rule.
  - Files are removed root first, then sides parents-first, then sub-agents.
  - Metadata is removed in the reverse order, so the root's retry entry stays until everything else is gone.
  - Relaunch marks side members as deleting as well as the owner, and the tombstones block opens and discovery. M4 below shows that the tombstone alone already blocks reopening.
- **Running work.** As before, deletion refuses with the busy member's name. Nothing is stopped implicitly.
- **Ledger repair.**
  - A **main** chat has no surviving ancestor, so no repair runs. With no ready helper, none is started and no profile or credential is needed.
  - With a ready helper, idle dependents are released and `session.forget` is sent with `descendantsDeleted`. This means no owner charge is mirrored into the deleted subtree, so damaged evidence in a side that is going to Trash can no longer block the deletion.
  - A **side** root still takes the existing repair barrier: the helper repairs every surviving ancestor from the whole deleted subtree before tombstones and Trash. Members are then forgotten with `ancestorsRepaired`, so their ancestors are not written again.
- **Missing project.** `deletionProjectUnavailable` covers both a removed project and a missing root folder. The confirmation's notes say that only Bello's own copy is removed and nothing in the project folder is touched. For a side whose helper is not ready, the notes also include "Skipped: checking for costs from interrupted requests…". If the state changes after the confirmation, any skip it did not announce is reported once deletion finishes.
- **Earlier orphans.** Sides orphaned by 0.1.128 deletions are left alone: their parent no longer exists, so they belong to no family. Deleting an orphan deletes it and its own descendants, with no repair.
- **Older intents.** Intents written by 0.1.128 (sides survive) resume with their original manifest and the original downward-mirror repair. No user data is deleted retroactively.
- **Confirmation UI.** `ChatQuestion.list` sends the question to the new `ChatQuestionSheet`, which is built from Pi components: a scrolling body, at most 200 rows plus "and N more", depth indentation and warning-colored notes. Return and Escape choose Cancel for a destructive question. With no window available, it falls back to an alert whose list is limited to 20 rows.

## Changed files

- App
  - `Workspaces/WorkspaceSubagentDeletion.swift`: member discovery, preview, repair decision, ordering.
  - `Workspaces/WorkspaceChatLifecycle.swift`: confirmation and result.
  - `Storage/MetadataStore.swift`: graph query and the intent rule for side members.
  - `Storage/SessionDeletionRecord.swift`: `sideDepth`.
  - `Design/PiQuestion.swift`, `Design/ChatQuestionSheet.swift`.
- Helper
  - `HostService.swift`: the `session.forget` flags. No change to SessionCost, SessionRequestAdmission, AgentSteeringCoordinator, SessionSide or SessionRun.
- Docs
  - `docs/design/subagents.md` §5.6 and §9.5.

## Tests

New tests:

- Helper (`SideBudgetCrashMatrixTests`)
  - `testFamilyDeletionSkipsMirrorsIntoDeletedDescendants`, cold and loaded.
  - `testMemberForgetAfterTheRootRepairLeavesAncestorsUntouched`.
- App (`FamilyDeletionTests`)
  - Whole family, cold or warm, listed or unlisted, including a side-owned sub-agent.
  - The confirmation's list and wording.
  - A long list with a scrolling sheet.
  - Side deletion keeps 0.9 counted in main.
  - Interrupted deletion at six steps, relaunch marking and resume.
  - A missing or removed project, for both a main chat and a side. A sentinel file in the project folder stays untouched.
  - An earlier orphan survives until it is deleted itself.
- App (`SideBudgetIntegrationTests`)
  - Deleting main with no ready helper (absent, stopped or stopping) starts none.
  - Damaged side evidence cannot block a whole-family deletion, cold or with a ready helper.
  - A side's helper-start failure stays retryable.
- Gallery: `ChatDeletionGalleryTests` (`PI_APP_CHAT_DELETION_GALLERY`).

Tests updated because the old behavior they asserted is gone:

- Sides surviving owner deletion: `testDeleteActionAllowsAnIdleVisibleSide…`, `testDeletingAnOwnerDetachesAndDeletesItsLoadedSavedSides`, `testPersistedUnlistedSideAncestry…`, `testRestoreKeepsUnlistedAncestors…`.
- Main deletion repairing surviving sides with a cold helper: replaced by the tests above.

### Failing before the fix

- The helper tests were run against the HEAD `HostService.swift` with the new tests in place, and all 4 assertions failed:
  - The cold forget threw `subagent_recovery`.
  - The loaded forget threw `accounting_damaged`.
  - The member forget rewrote main's journal (2,353 vs 520 bytes).
- The pre-fix app does not have the new API the app tests call, so its old behavior was shown in two ways:
  - The first run of the updated old-semantics tests.
  - The mutations below.

### Mutation checks

Run against `FamilyDeletionTests` and `SideBudgetIntegrationTests`:

| Mutation | Result |
| --- | --- |
| M1: side members not added | 9 tests failed (whole family, side subtree, interruption, missing project, helper start, damaged evidence, no helper, unlisted, restore) |
| M2: missing project throws instead of skipping | `testMissingOrRemovedProjectDeletesOnlyTheChatsOwnFiles` failed |
| M3: main deletion always starts the helper | missing-project and no-ready-helper tests failed |
| M4: relaunch does not mark side members | survived: the per-side tombstone blocks opens on its own. The in-memory mark is kept as defense in depth (blocks discovery and ancestor retention before the first store read) |
| M5: `descendantsDeleted` not sent | `testDamagedSideEvidenceCannotBlockDeletingTheWholeFamily` (ready helper) failed |

### Focused runs

All with zero failures:

- `FamilyDeletionTests` + `SideBudgetIntegrationTests`: 25.
- `SubagentCostTests`: 11.
- 10 affected parallel classes plus `BudgetOwnerUnloadTests` and `PiQuestionSheetTests`, run with 4 workers: 142 passed. The one failure there was the obsolete survivor assertion, since updated.
- Serial-lane classes run alone: `ConversationPaneTests/testDeletingAChatAsksFirst`, `SubagentReaderTests`, `CaptureDrainTests`: 61.

### Gallery

The scenes are generated titles only: few sides, 240 sides, a missing project with a skip note, and no sides. Each is rendered light and dark, at 1240 and 340 wide. The images were checked by eye: buttons visible, long titles cut at the end, nested rows indented, notes legible in both themes.

### Full gate

See [Full gate result](#full-gate-result).

## Needs owner decision

- **Untrusted project.** A side in an untrusted project still cannot be deleted while its helper is not running, because the ancestor repair needs a trusted helper and the existing trust error is shown. Main chats are unaffected. The conservative choice keeps that behavior. The alternative is to skip the repair and name it, as for a missing folder.
- **Downgrade.** An older app that finds an interrupted 0.1.129 family intent cannot finish it, because its journal-name rule rejects `side_` members. It reports the incomplete deletion and keeps the intent until this version or a later one runs.
- **Damaged intermediate record.** A side below an unreadable intermediate side's metadata cannot be proven part of the family. It is left in place and becomes an independent chat after deletion, which is the 0.1.128 orphan behavior. It is never deleted on a guess.
- **Capture projection rows.** Capture-archive `side_budgets` projection rows for deleted sides stay, as they did for side deletion in 0.1.128. Their captures are cleared, so no total is affected.
- **Button label.** The confirmation's action stays "Delete Chat" for sides too.
import AppKit

// Side panels are durable child chats. A recovery intent precedes journal
// publication so an interrupted acknowledgement cannot orphan saved history.
struct SideRecord: Identifiable, Equatable {
    var id: String; var parentID: String; var workspaceID: String; var profileID: String; var title: String
    var kept = false; var keeping = false; var keepRequested = false
    /// Open on screen only; the helper session and journal appear with its first message.
    var pending = false
    var boundary: [String: WireValue] = [:]
    /// Model and reasoning overrides inherited from the parent when the side opens.
    var model: String? = nil
    var thinkingLevel: String? = nil
    var contextWindow: Int? = nil
    var maxOutputTokens: Int? = nil
    var modelOutputLimit: Int? = nil
    var outputBudgetVersion: Int? = 1
    var topicID: String?
    /// Legacy value retained for decoding only; sides follow the main budget.
    var costLimit: CostLimit? = nil
    /// What follows its chat's title in a new side's, until its first
    /// message names it.
    static let titleSuffix = " — side"
    var chat: ChatRecord { .init(id: id, workspaceID: workspaceID, title: title, path: nil, profileID: profileID, toolMode: ChatRecord.readOnlyTools, model: model, thinkingLevel: thinkingLevel, contextWindow: contextWindow, maxOutputTokens: maxOutputTokens, modelOutputLimit: modelOutputLimit, outputBudgetVersion: outputBudgetVersion, topicID: topicID, parentSessionID: parentID, costLimit: costLimit) }
}
struct SideKeepIntent: Codable, Sendable {
    /// Attachment discovery only: never listed for automatic registration.
    static let retainedKind = "side-keep-retained"
    var chat: ChatRecord
}

/// The exact pending copy consumed by a successful registration. A delayed
/// caller can acknowledge it without replaying chat/draft writes.
struct SideKeepRegistration: Codable, Sendable {
    static let kind = "side-keep-registration"
    var expected: ChatRecord
    var path: String?
}

enum SideKeepState: Sendable {
    case pending(SideKeepIntent)
    case registered(chat: ChatRecord, expected: ChatRecord)
    case missing
}

extension WorkspaceModel {
    /// Both are asked for once per sidebar row, and the sidebar redraws on every
    /// workspace change: a linear scan here is a scan of every chat per row.
    func side(_ id: String) -> SideRecord? { sidebarIndex.side(id, in: sides) }
    func record(_ id: String) -> ChatRecord? { chatRecord(id) ?? side(id)?.chat }
    func isEphemeral(_ id: String) -> Bool { side(id).map { !$0.kept } ?? false }
    var resourceTarget: SessionDisplay? { displays[resourceTargetSessionID ?? selectedID ?? ""] }
    /// The Session Inspector at the session's latest request: 🐞, ⌥⌘I and the chat's ⋯ menu.
    func inspect(_ id: String) { openInspector(session: id, focus: .latestRequest) }
    /// Shows or hides the integrated terminal under the selected chat.
    func toggleTerminal() {
        guard selectedID != nil else { return }
        terminalVisible.toggle()
        if !terminalVisible, let id = focusedSessionID ?? selectedID { displays[id]?.composerFocusRequest += 1 }
    }
    func inspectResources(_ id: String?) { resourceTargetSessionID = id; showResources = true }
    func updateHostActivity(workspaceID: String) {
        hosts[workspaceID]?.isBusy = sides.values.contains { $0.workspaceID == workspaceID && !$0.kept && !$0.pending } || displays.values.contains { ($0.hasWork || $0.loading) && record($0.id)?.workspaceID == workspaceID }
    }
    func canOpenSide(_ id: String) -> Bool {
        guard let parent = record(id), !parent.isSubagent, orphanedSideRefusal(id) == nil else { return false }
        return !parent.imported && parent.connectionTest != true && parent.workspaceID != WorkspaceRecord.scratchID
    }
    /// A saved side whose chat was deleted stays in the sidebar as a chat of
    /// its own, and opens no side of its own (the owner's choice, 0.1.116).
    /// A saved side is known by its journal, `side_‹id›.jsonl`: a chat forked
    /// from a reply also names the chat it came from, and keeps its sides.
    func orphanedSideRefusal(_ id: String) -> String? {
        guard let chat = record(id), let parentID = chat.parentSessionID, record(parentID) == nil,
              let path = chat.path, URL(fileURLWithPath: path).lastPathComponent.hasPrefix("side_") else { return nil }
        return "The chat this side was opened from was deleted, so it can't open sides of its own."
    }
    func canQuoteReply(_ id: String) -> Bool {
        guard !installPreparing, canOpenSide(id), side(id) == nil, let parent = record(id),
              !parent.isArchived, !parent.isBackgroundTask, workspace(for: parent.workspaceID) != nil else { return false }
        return true
    }
    /// Selection is quoted as unsent prose, never interpreted as a slash/skill
    /// command. Reuse an unfinished side draft without overwriting it; a saved
    /// side keeps its history while a new child opens beside the parent.
    func openQuotedSide(parentID: String, quote: TranscriptQuote) {
        guard selectedID == parentID, canQuoteReply(parentID), !quote.text.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty else { return }
        let existing = sides[parentID]
        if let existing, existing.keeping || (!existing.pending && !existing.kept) || displays[existing.id]?.loading == true {
            error = "Wait for the current side to finish opening before quoting another response."; return
        }
        let existingDraft = existing.flatMap { $0.pending ? displays[$0.id]?.draft : nil } ?? ""
        let quoted = quote.text.replacingOccurrences(of: "\r\n", with: "\n").replacingOccurrences(of: "\r", with: "\n")
            .components(separatedBy: "\n").map { "> " + $0 }.joined(separator: "\n")
        let draft = existingDraft + (existingDraft.isEmpty ? "" : "\n\n") + quoted + "\n\n"
        guard draft.utf8.count <= SubmissionLimits.messageBytes else { error = "The quoted draft exceeds 256 KiB. Select a shorter passage."; return }
        openSide(parentID: parentID)
        guard let info = sides[parentID], info.pending, let view = displays[info.id] else { return }
        view.draft = draft; view.directCommand = false; view.completionVisible = false; view.completionToken = nil
        draftChanged(view)
        focusedSessionID = info.id; view.composerFocusRequest += 1
    }
    func openSide(parentID: String? = nil, question: String = "") {
        guard !installPreparing, let parentID = parentID ?? selectedID, let parent = record(parentID), !parent.imported else { error = "Continue an imported original as a separate chat before opening a side."; return }
        // A new side opens beside its chat, which is on screen: the sidebar
        // unfolds nothing for it. A side list the reader folded stays folded,
        // the new side listed under the fold (owner, 2026-10-12).
        quietSidebarReveal = sidebarLineage(of: parentID)
        if let refusal = orphanedSideRefusal(parentID) { error = refusal; return }
        guard canOpenSide(parentID) else { error = "Connection-test chats keep tools disabled and cannot open side chats."; return }
        guard side(parentID) == nil else { error = "Close this side panel before opening a side from its saved chat."; return }
        // Another side can open while one is shown: the shown side stays a
        // saved child chat in the sidebar and comes back on click. Only a side
        // that is still being published must finish first.
        if let existing = sides[parentID], existing.pending {
            // The empty side is already waiting for its first message.
            quietSidebarReveal.insert(existing.id)
            focusedSessionID = existing.id; displays[existing.id]?.composerFocusRequest += 1
            if !question.isEmpty { displays[existing.id]?.draft = question; send(sessionID: existing.id) }
            return
        }
        if let existing = sides[parentID], !existing.kept || existing.keeping {
            quietSidebarReveal.insert(existing.id)
            focusedSessionID = existing.id
            error = "Wait for the current side to finish opening before starting another."; return
        }
        let id = UUID().uuidString, view = SessionDisplay(id: id)
        quietSidebarReveal.insert(id)
        view.historyState = .empty; view.selectionMetadataLoaded = true
        var info = SideRecord(id: id, parentID: parentID, workspaceID: parent.workspaceID, profileID: parent.profileID, title: parent.title + SideRecord.titleSuffix, model: parent.model, thinkingLevel: parent.thinkingLevel, contextWindow: parent.contextWindow, maxOutputTokens: parent.maxOutputTokens, modelOutputLimit: parent.modelOutputLimit, outputBudgetVersion: parent.outputBudgetVersion)
        info.topicID = effectiveTopicID(for: parent)
        if question.isEmpty {
            // Nothing is created until the first message: no intent, journal or helper session.
            info.pending = true; sides[parentID] = info
            displays[id] = view
            if selectedID == parentID { focusedSessionID = id }
            view.composerFocusRequest += 1
            return
        }
        sides[parentID] = info
        displays[id] = view; view.loading = true; view.draft = question
        Task {
            do {
                try await publishPendingSide(info, view: view)
                // This flag was set here, for the side's creation; the send
                // below sets its own.
                view.loading = false
                view.draft = question; send(sessionID: id)
            } catch {
                view.loading = false; view.notice = error.localizedDescription
                // Unwind the in-memory side for every failure, not only an
                // explicit rejection. A side left half-open can be neither
                // closed nor kept, and while it exists the app refuses to quit
                // and blocks every update. The saved side-keep intent still
                // lets a side the host did publish be recovered on restart.
                // Only this side: a lost host may already have put another
                // in its place holding what was typed here.
                let replaced = sides[parentID].map { $0.id != id } ?? false
                if !replaced { sides.removeValue(forKey: parentID) }
                displays.removeValue(forKey: id)
                if !replaced, let original = displays[parentID] {
                    if let recovery = draftWrites.recoveries[parentID] { await recovery.value }
                    original.draft += (original.draft.isEmpty ? "" : "\n\n") + question; original.directCommand = false; draftChanged(original)
                }
                self.error = error.localizedDescription; updateHostActivity(workspaceID: parent.workspaceID)
            }
        }
    }
    /// Creates the side on the helper: the recovery intent and draft, the
    /// parent's context snapshot as a durable child session, and its capture
    /// preference. Called when a side with a question opens, and when a
    /// pending side sends its first message.
    /// `draft` is what the kept side starts with on disk: the message being
    /// sent, whose composer has already emptied (`send`), else what the
    /// composer holds.
    func publishPendingSide(_ info: SideRecord, view: SessionDisplay, draft: DraftRecord? = nil) async throws {
        guard let store else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        guard let parent = record(info.parentID) else { throw HostError.failure("The parent chat is no longer available.") }
        let id = info.id, parentID = info.parentID
        // A send that publishes the side holds this flag for the whole
        // submission. Clearing it here, with the first message still on its
        // way, let Send submit that message a second time.
        let wasLoading = view.loading
        view.loading = true; defer { if !wasLoading { view.loading = false } }
        var saved = info.chat
        saved.topicID = effectiveTopicID(for: parent)
        saved.path = root.appendingPathComponent("Workspaces/\(info.workspaceID)/Sessions/side_\(id).jsonl").path
        try await store.put(SideKeepIntent(chat: saved), kind: "side-keep", id: id)
        try await store.put(draft ?? DraftRecord(id: id, text: view.draft), kind: "draft", id: id)
        let host = try await open(parent); host.isBusy = true
        sides[parentID]?.pending = false
        let result: [String: WireValue]
        var written = false
        do {
            // Sides share the main chat’s budget.
            result = try await host.request("side.open", sessionID: parentID, params: ["sideSessionId": .string(id)]).object ?? [:]
            written = true
            guard result["sessionId"]?.string == id, sides[parentID]?.id == id else { throw HostError.failure("Side identity changed; reopen this project before creating another side.") }
            try await registerKeptSide(id: id, path: result["path"]?.string)
        } catch {
            // Nothing was kept, so the side is still the draft it was: it can
            // be sent again or closed, and its text is where it was. Left
            // half-open, nothing could close, keep or replace it, and while it
            // existed the app refused to quit and blocked every update. A side
            // the helper did write is still recovered from its intent; one it
            // refused leaves nothing behind, since a draft side's text is
            // never stored.
            if sides[parentID]?.id == id, sides[parentID]?.kept != true { sides[parentID]?.pending = true }
            if !written {
                try? await store.remove(kind: "side-keep", id: id)
                try? await store.remove(kind: "draft", id: id)
            }
            throw error
        }
        let preference = try await capturePreference(sessionID: id)
        // Read inside the actor: a review marker must never overwrite newer
        // typing with the draft a journal read started from.
        guard var draft = try get(DraftRecord.self, kind: "draft", id: sessionID) else { return }
        draft.requireSubmissionReview(commands)
        try put(draft, kind: "draft", id: sessionID)
    }
    @discardableResult func commitKeptSide(_ proposed: ChatRecord, draft: DraftRecord, expected: ChatRecord) throws -> ChatRecord {
        try transaction {
            guard proposed.id == expected.id else { throw StoreError.staleRevision }
            let pending = try get(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep", id: expected.id)
            if pending == nil {
                // Another registration may have won while this caller read the
                // header. A receipt proves which copy won; an ID alone cannot.
                // Return its current organization without touching newer typing.
                guard let completed = try sideKeepRegistration(id: expected.id),
                      completed.expected == expected else { throw StoreError.staleRevision }
                return completed.chat
            }
            // Identity reads suspend outside this transaction. Dismissal, a
            // replacement or another receipt may have consumed their intent.
            // Compare the captured intent before path/organization migration;
            // no chat or draft write is allowed without that exact pending copy.
            guard pending?.chat == expected else { throw StoreError.staleRevision }
            var chat = proposed
            if let existing = try get(ChatRecord.self, kind: "chat", id: chat.id) {
                // Replaying a keep receipt must preserve an independent rename
                // or topic move made after this child was first retained.
                chat.applyOrganization(from: existing)
            } else if let parentID = chat.parentSessionID {
                // Whichever commits first, moving a parent and publishing a new
                // side agree on the parent's current durable group. A fork has
                // no parentSessionID and keeps its captured topic instead.
                let parent = try get(ChatRecord.self, kind: "chat", id: parentID)
                chat.topicID = parent?.workspaceID == chat.workspaceID ? parent?.topicID : nil
            }
            try put(chat, kind: "chat", id: chat.id); try put(draft, kind: "draft", id: chat.id); try remove(kind: "side-keep", id: chat.id)
            try put(SideKeepRegistration(expected: expected, path: chat.path), kind: SideKeepRegistration.kind, id: chat.id)
            // The registered chat now owns this reference. Other dismissed
            // paths with the same ID remain independently protected.
            try remove(kind: SideKeepIntent.retainedKind, id: sideKeepRetentionID(expected))
            guard let saved = try get(ChatRecord.self, kind: "chat", id: chat.id) else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
            return saved
        }
    }
    /// A commit may precede its caller's sidebar publication. Read its receipt
    /// and current chat atomically, without treating a replacement as settled.
    func sideKeepState(id: String) throws -> SideKeepState {
        try transaction {
            if let pending = try get(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep", id: id) { return .pending(pending) }
            if let completed = try sideKeepRegistration(id: id) { return .registered(chat: completed.chat, expected: completed.expected) }
            return .missing
        }
    }
    private func sideKeepRegistration(id: String) throws -> (chat: ChatRecord, expected: ChatRecord)? {
        guard let receipt = try get(SideKeepRegistration.self, kind: SideKeepRegistration.kind, id: id),
              let saved = try get(ChatRecord.self, kind: "chat", id: id),
              receipt.expected.id == id, saved.id == id,
              saved.workspaceID == receipt.expected.workspaceID, saved.path == receipt.path else { return nil }
        return (saved, receipt.expected)
    }
    private func sideKeepRetentionID(_ chat: ChatRecord) throws -> String {
        // Preserve each file, including replacements that reuse a chat ID.
        let identity = try JSONEncoder().encode([chat.workspaceID, chat.id, chat.path] as [String?])
        return SHA256.hash(data: identity).map { String(format: "%02x", $0) }.joined()
    }
    /// Only the exact pending copy reviewed by the confirmation can be
    /// dismissed. Keep its journal discoverable for attachment retention,
    /// without leaving an automatic-registration intent or changing its bytes.
    func dismissSideKeep(_ expected: ChatRecord) throws {
        try transaction {
            guard let current = try get(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep", id: expected.id) else { return }
            guard current.chat == expected else { throw StoreError.staleRevision }
            try put(current, kind: SideKeepIntent.retainedKind, id: sideKeepRetentionID(current.chat))
            try remove(kind: "side-keep", id: expected.id)
        }
    }
    /// A handoff must never reappear as an empty chat after a partial write.
    func commitPortableHandoff(_ chat: ChatRecord, draft: DraftRecord, provenance: WireValue?) throws {
        let database = try ready()
        guard sqlite3_exec(database, "BEGIN IMMEDIATE", nil, nil, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        transactionDrafts = []  // told once committed (`transaction`)
        do {
            try put(chat, kind: "chat", id: chat.id)
            try put(draft, kind: "draft", id: chat.id)
            if let provenance { try put(provenance, kind: "handoff", id: chat.id) }
            guard sqlite3_exec(database, "COMMIT", nil, nil, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
            flushTransactionDrafts()
        } catch { transactionDrafts = nil; sqlite3_exec(database, "ROLLBACK", nil, nil, nil); throw error }
    }

        guard sqlite3_exec(database, "BEGIN IMMEDIATE", nil, nil, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        do {
            try put(chat, kind: "chat", id: chat.id)

    /// Loaded sides and managed children retain their actual budget ancestry
    /// outside the ordinary chat window. A fork source is only provenance.
    func loadBudgetAncestors(of chats: [ChatRecord], profiles: [ProfileRecord] = []) throws -> [ChatRecord] {
        try migrateLoadedChats(budgetAncestorRecords(of: chats), profiles: profiles)
    }
    private func budgetAncestorRecords(of chats: [ChatRecord]) throws -> [(ChatRecord, Int64)] {
        let database = try ready()
        var seen = Set(chats.map(\.id)), checked = Set<String>(), pending = chats, values: [(ChatRecord, Int64)] = []
        while let child = pending.popLast() {
            guard let id = child.persistedBudgetParentID, !seen.contains(id), checked.insert(child.workspaceID + "\u{0}" + id).inserted,
                  try get(String.self, kind: "session-deleted", id: id) == nil else { continue }
            var statement: OpaquePointer?
            guard sqlite3_prepare_v2(database, "SELECT value,revision FROM records WHERE kind='chat' AND id=?", -1, &statement, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
            defer { sqlite3_finalize(statement) }
            sqlite3_bind_text(statement, 1, id, -1, unsafeBitCast(-1, to: sqlite3_destructor_type.self))
            let result = sqlite3_step(statement)
            if result == SQLITE_DONE { continue }
            guard result == SQLITE_ROW else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
            let size = Int(sqlite3_column_bytes(statement, 0))
            guard size <= Self.recordByteLimit("chat"), let bytes = sqlite3_column_blob(statement, 0) else { continue }
            decodedChats += 1
            guard let parent = try? JSONDecoder().decode(ChatRecord.self, from: Data(bytes: bytes, count: size)),
                  parent.id == id, !parent.isSubagent, parent.workspaceID == child.workspaceID else { continue }
            seen.insert(id); values.append((parent, sqlite3_column_int64(statement, 1))); pending.append(parent)
        }
        return values
    }
    private func migrateLoadedChats(_ records: [(ChatRecord, Int64)], profiles: [ProfileRecord]) throws -> [ChatRecord] {
        var values = records
        var upgraded: [(ChatRecord, Int64)] = []
        for index in values.indices {
            let previous = values[index].0
            if values[index].0.sidebarOrder == nil { values[index].0.sidebarOrder = values[index].1 }
            if let profile = profiles.first(where: { $0.id == values[index].0.profileID }) {
                values[index].0.migrateOutputBudget(profile: profile)
            }
            if values[index].0 != previous { upgraded.append((values[index].0, values[index].1)) }
        }
        // The upgrade commits once. Per-chat commits cost one fsync each
        // (synchronous=FULL), which the first launch after an upgrade pays
        // before a single row of the sidebar can render.
        if !upgraded.isEmpty {
            try transaction { for (chat, revision) in upgraded { try put(chat, kind: "chat", id: chat.id, revision: revision) } }
        }
        return values.map(\.0).sorted(by: ChatRecord.sidebarPrecedes)
    }

        let database = try ready()
        var statement: OpaquePointer?

import Foundation

// What the workspace reads back at launch and which folder it works in.
// `restore()` runs once: it opens the store off the main actor, pulls the
// projects, topics, chats and read states back, leaves the sidebar paintable
// before the rest of the model has settled, and reopens the chat the reader
// had open (`WorkspaceLaunchSelection.swift`).

extension WorkspaceModel {
    func restore() async {
        guard !restoring else { return }
        guard store != nil else { launching = false; return }
        restoring = true; launching = true
        guard await prepareStore(), let store else { restoring = false; launching = false; return }
        // The rows are live from the moment the chats are listed. A chat the
        // reader opens from them, or from the menu bar, before this finishes
        // is the one they want: launch must not open another over it.
        let launchSelection = selectionRevision
        // The chat list and the vault are independent reads, and the sidebar's
        // first frame needs both: read serially, every chat was listed under a
        // "Retained chats" placeholder until the Keychain answered. What the
        // reader had open last time rides along with the chat list.
        async let savedConfiguration = vault.load()
        async let savedSelection = store.get(RememberedSelection.self, kind: RememberedSelection.recordKind, id: RememberedSelection.recordID)
        do {
            let restored = try await store.loadChats()
            var loaded: VaultConfiguration?
            do { let saved = try await savedConfiguration; applyConfiguration(saved); loaded = saved }
            catch { configurationLoaded = false; self.error = error.localizedDescription; retryConfigurationWhenActive() }
            // A record this build cannot read is no record: the launch opens
            // what it always did.
            let remembered = (try? await savedSelection)?.sanitized
            // In the same turn as the rows: a chat opened from the first
            // painted row brings its side back, and a pane opened or closed
            // from then on is kept track of on top of what was read.
            chats = restored
            adoptRememberedSelection(remembered)
            await settleBackgroundRequests()
            await nameUnnamedJournals()
            await markPendingFamilyDeletions()
            await reconcileSubagents()
            try await restoreTopics()
            try await restoreReadStates()
            await restoreRunHolds()
            await restoreDraftMarks()
            await restoreContextReadings()
            await reconcileSideKeeps()
            try await restoreProjectSidebarStates()
            if selectedID == nil, selectionRevision == launchSelection {
                // The reopened chat's project is the chosen one from here on.
                selectedWorkspaceID = launchProjectID(remembered); profileChoice = requestProfiles.first?.id ?? ""
            }
            // The request archive's retention sweep and retained billing for
            // every listed chat are the two longest reads of a launch, and the
            // sidebar is already on screen: they run after it, not before it.
            // The archive opens (with its sweep) before the chat does, since
            // the chat's accounting reads it and its helper writes into it;
            // the billing of every listed chat waits until the chat is open.
            var archiveOpen = false
            if let loaded { archiveOpen = await openConfiguredArchive(loaded) }
            if archiveOpen {
                await resumeFamilyDeletions()
                for summary in subagentSummaries.values where record(summary.link.childSessionID)?.delegationIssue == nil {
                    if let workspace = record(summary.link.childSessionID)?.workspaceID { try await traces.recordSubagent(summary, workspaceID: workspace) }
                }
            }
            // Restore history without opening every runtime: one chat, the one
            // the reader had open. Only the focused, safe native chat may
            // prepare its context in the background.
            let launchOpened = await reopenRememberedSelection(remembered, unlessSelectedSince: launchSelection)
            // The pane shows the chat now, or the welcome if there is none.
            launching = false
            // A chat launch opened reads its own accounting once its page is
            // on screen (`historyViewportReady`), as every opened chat does. A
            // chat the reader opened while launch was still reading may have
            // read it before the archive was open: read those again.
            if archiveOpen { if launchOpened { await refreshChatStats() } else { await refreshRetainedAccounting() } }
            // A chat this build cannot list is not a chat that is gone. Say so
            // once, instead of leaving a row silently missing from the sidebar.
            let unlisted = await store.unlistedChats
            if unlisted > 0, self.error == nil {
                self.error = "\(unlisted) saved chat\(unlisted == 1 ? "" : "s") could not be listed by this version. Their conversation files are untouched."
            }
            // Journals written before 0.1.111, slimmed once while nothing else is going on.
            scheduleJournalSlimming()
            schedulePastedImageCleanup()
        } catch { restoring = false; launching = false; self.error = "Chats could not be restored. \(error.localizedDescription)" }
    }

    /// it. A crash or a failed write in between left a record with no path
