    /// the database an operation has to materialise; grouping used to
    /// materialise all of it. One addition per decode when nothing reads it.
    private(set) var decodedChats = 0
    private(set) var organizationCommits = 0
    /// Cheap and non-throwing. Opening SQLite runs WAL recovery, which used to
    /// happen on whichever thread built the model — for the app, the main actor
    /// during its first `body`. The database opens inside the actor instead, on
    /// first use or when `open()` asks for it.
    init(url: URL) { self.url = url }
    /// Opens the database now and reports why it could not be opened, for the
    /// caller that needs to know there is no storage at all.
    func open() throws { _ = try ready() }
    private func ready() throws -> OpaquePointer {
        if !attempted {
            attempted = true
            try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true, attributes: [.posixPermissions: 0o700])
            connection = try? MetadataDatabase(url: url)
        }
        guard let database = connection?.handle else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        return database
    }
    /// Reserve before an asynchronous debounce. Persisted revisions, rather
    /// than the wall clock alone, keep edits ordered across clock corrections.
    func reserveRevision(kind: String, id: String) throws -> Int64 {
        let database = try ready()
        var statement: OpaquePointer?
        guard sqlite3_prepare_v2(database, "SELECT revision FROM records WHERE kind=? AND id=?", -1, &statement, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        defer { sqlite3_finalize(statement) }
        let transient = unsafeBitCast(-1, to: sqlite3_destructor_type.self)
        sqlite3_bind_text(statement, 1, kind, -1, transient); sqlite3_bind_text(statement, 2, id, -1, transient)
        let result = sqlite3_step(statement)
        guard result == SQLITE_ROW || result == SQLITE_DONE else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        let previous = result == SQLITE_ROW ? sqlite3_column_int64(statement, 0) : 0
        let latest = max(lastReservedRevision, previous)
        guard latest < Int64.max else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
        lastReservedRevision = max(latest + 1, Int64(Date().timeIntervalSince1970 * 1_000_000))
        return lastReservedRevision
    }
    func put<T: Encodable & Sendable>(_ value: T, kind: String, id: String, revision: Int64? = nil, releasingTitleClaim: Bool = false) throws {
        let database = try ready()
        let savedRevision: Int64
        if kind == TopicRecord.recordKind {
            // Topic callers carry their own persisted revision. Reserving a new
            // one here would let a delayed snapshot overwrite a newer rename.
            guard let topic = value as? TopicRecord, topic.id == id, topic.isValid, topic.revision > 0,
                  revision == nil || revision == topic.revision,
                  try get(Int64.self, kind: TopicRecord.deletedRecordKind, id: id) == nil else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
            if let previous = try get(TopicRecord.self, kind: kind, id: id) {
                guard previous.workspaceID == topic.workspaceID, previous.createdAt == topic.createdAt else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
                guard topic.revision > previous.revision || topic == previous else { throw StoreError.staleRevision }
            }
            savedRevision = topic.revision
        } else { savedRevision = try revision ?? reserveRevision(kind: kind, id: id) }
        let data: Data
        if kind == "chat", var chat = value as? ChatRecord {
            guard try get(String.self, kind: "session-deleted", id: id) == nil else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
            // A chat's record is written from many places at once; what an
            // older copy may not take back is in `ChatRecordMerge.swift`.
            if let previous = try get(ChatRecord.self, kind: kind, id: id) { chat = chat.merged(over: previous, releasingTitleClaim: releasingTitleClaim) }
            if let topicID = chat.topicID { chat = chat.fitting(topic: try get(TopicRecord.self, kind: TopicRecord.recordKind, id: topicID)) }
            data = try JSONEncoder().encode(chat)
        } else { data = try JSONEncoder().encode(value) }
        guard data.count <= Self.recordByteLimit(kind) else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
        var statement: OpaquePointer?
        guard sqlite3_prepare_v2(database, "INSERT INTO records VALUES(?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(kind,id) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value,revision=excluded.revision WHERE excluded.revision>=records.revision", -1, &statement, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        defer { sqlite3_finalize(statement) }
        let transient = unsafeBitCast(-1, to: sqlite3_destructor_type.self)
        sqlite3_bind_text(statement, 1, kind, -1, transient); sqlite3_bind_text(statement, 2, id, -1, transient)
        _ = data.withUnsafeBytes { sqlite3_bind_blob(statement, 3, $0.baseAddress, Int32(data.count), transient) }
        sqlite3_bind_int64(statement, 4, savedRevision)
        guard sqlite3_step(statement) == SQLITE_DONE else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        guard sqlite3_changes(database) == 1 else { throw StoreError.staleRevision }
        if kind == "draft", let draft = value as? DraftRecord { noteDraftWrite(id, draft.holdsUnsentDraft) }
        if kind.hasPrefix("receipt:") { try prune(kind: kind, keeping: 128) }
    }
    /// Update only the cost choice from the latest durable chat. The revision
    /// also protects it from unrelated writes holding an older chat copy.
    func updateChatCostLimit(id: String, limit: CostLimit?) throws -> ChatRecord {
        guard var chat = try get(ChatRecord.self, kind: "chat", id: id), limit?.isValid != false else { throw StoreError.invalidRecord }
        let revision = try reserveRevision(kind: "chat", id: id)
        chat.costLimit = limit; chat.costLimitRevision = revision
        try put(chat, kind: "chat", id: id, revision: revision)
        return chat
    }
    /// Freeze the previous sidebar order once when upgrading older records.
    /// Later metadata writes (renaming, opening or changing a model) cannot move
    /// a chat unexpectedly. This only migrates desktop metadata, never journals.
    func loadChats(profiles: [ProfileRecord] = []) throws -> [ChatRecord] {
        let database = try ready()
        var statement: OpaquePointer?
        // Preserve the ordinary-chat window. Managed children are durable
        // members of a family, not competing roots: they must neither evict
        // their owner nor disappear behind this unrelated desktop limit.
        let query = """
            WITH chats AS (
                SELECT value,revision,id,CASE WHEN length(value)>524288 THEN NULL WHEN json_valid(CAST(value AS TEXT))
                    THEN json_type(CAST(value AS TEXT),'$.delegation') ELSE NULL END AS delegation
                FROM records WHERE kind='chat'
            ), roots AS (
                SELECT value,revision,id FROM chats WHERE delegation IS NOT 'object'
                ORDER BY revision DESC,id ASC LIMIT 10000
            )
            SELECT value,revision FROM (
                SELECT value,revision,id FROM roots UNION ALL
                SELECT value,revision,id FROM chats WHERE delegation='object'
            ) ORDER BY revision DESC,id ASC
            """
        guard sqlite3_prepare_v2(database, query, -1, &statement, nil) == SQLITE_OK else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        var values: [(ChatRecord, Int64)] = []
        do {
            defer { sqlite3_finalize(statement) }
            var result = sqlite3_step(statement)
            while result == SQLITE_ROW {
                let size = Int(sqlite3_column_bytes(statement, 0))
                // One oversized or undecodable row (a record shape written by
                // another version) must not hide every other chat.
                if size <= 524_288, let bytes = sqlite3_column_blob(statement, 0) {
                    decodedChats += 1
                    if let chat = try? JSONDecoder().decode(ChatRecord.self, from: Data(bytes: bytes, count: size)) {
                        values.append((chat, sqlite3_column_int64(statement, 1)))
                    }
                }
                result = sqlite3_step(statement)
            }
            guard result == SQLITE_DONE else { throw StoreError.unavailable }
        }
        values += try budgetAncestorRecords(of: values.map(\.0))
        unlistedChats = max(0, try count(kind: "chat") - values.count)
        return try migrateLoadedChats(values, profiles: profiles)
    }

    /// The complete durable graph, independent of the presentation window.
    /// Reads metadata only; discarded draft content is never materialized.
    func sideBudgetAncestry() throws -> [String: [String: [String]]] {
        let (records, present) = try durableChatGraph()
        var result: [String:[String:[String]]] = [:]
        for chat in records.values where !chat.isSubagent && chat.parentSessionID != nil {
            var current = chat, parents: [String] = [], seen: Set<String> = [chat.id], valid = true
            while let id = current.persistedBudgetParentID {
                guard seen.insert(id).inserted, parents.count < 128 else { valid = false; break }
                guard let parent = records[id] else {
                    // Undecodable metadata is not proof of deletion. Preserve
                    // its prior projection until the damaged record is repaired.
                    if present.contains(id) { valid = false }
                    break
                }
                guard parent.workspaceID == chat.workspaceID, !parent.isSubagent else { break }
                parents.append(id); current = parent
            }
            if valid { result[chat.workspaceID,default:[:]][chat.id] = parents }
        }
import AppKit

// Multi-folder workspaces. The primary `path` stays the host working directory
// and display name; `paths` adds trusted folders. Folder changes are durable in
// the configuration vault first and take effect when the workspace host is
// reopened, so they are refused while that workspace still has active work.
extension WorkspaceModel {
    /// Total roots (primary + extras) the host accepts for one workspace.
    static let maximumRoots = 16

    /// Directory picker; symlinks are resolved so duplicates compare by real path.
    static func chooseFolders(message: String, multiple: Bool) async -> [String] {
        let panel = NSOpenPanel(); panel.canChooseFiles = false; panel.canChooseDirectories = true; panel.canCreateDirectories = false
        panel.allowsMultipleSelection = multiple; panel.message = message
        return await PiQuestion.shared.open(panel).map { $0.resolvingSymlinksInPath().path }
    }

    /// Rejects relative, missing, duplicate or overlong root lists before they reach the vault.
    static func validateRoots(_ roots: [String]) throws {
        guard !roots.isEmpty else { throw HostError.failure("A project needs a primary folder.") }
        guard roots.count <= maximumRoots else { throw HostError.failure("A project can contain up to \(maximumRoots) folders.") }
        guard Set(roots).count == roots.count else { throw HostError.failure("Each folder can be part of a project only once.") }
        for root in roots {
            var directory: ObjCBool = false
            guard root.hasPrefix("/"), root.utf8.count <= 4096, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: root, isDirectory: &directory), directory.boolValue else {
                throw HostError.failure("\(root) is not an existing folder.")
            }
        }
    }

    func chatCount(workspaceID: String) -> Int { chats.filter { $0.workspaceID == workspaceID }.count }

    /// A project folder moved or renamed while the app was closed used to
    /// reach the helper launch, which failed as "The bundled host could not
    /// start. Reinstall this build." Checked first, it is named instead, and
    /// the project is marked so its chats offer Locate Folder….
    func requireProjectFolders(_ workspace: WorkspaceRecord) throws {
        guard let missing = workspace.roots.first(where: { root in
            var directory: ObjCBool = false
            return !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: root, isDirectory: &directory) || !directory.boolValue
        }) else {
            if missingProjectFolders[workspace.id] != nil { missingProjectFolders.removeValue(forKey: workspace.id) }
            return
        }
        missingProjectFolders[workspace.id] = missing
        throw HostError.failure("The project folder \(missing) is missing or was moved. Choose Locate Folder… to point the project at where it is now; its chats stay as they are.")
    }

    /// Points a project at the new place of a folder that was moved, keeping
    /// the project, its chats and their journals (they live in app storage
    /// under the project's id, not in the folder).
    func relocateProjectFolder(_ workspaceID: String, from missing: String, to folder: String) async throws {
        try await ensureConfiguration()
        guard let workspace = workspaces.first(where: { $0.id == workspaceID }) else { throw HostError.failure("This project is no longer configured.") }
        guard workspace.roots.contains(missing) else { throw HostError.failure("That folder is not part of this project.") }
        try requireIdle(workspaceID)
        let replacement = URL(fileURLWithPath: folder).resolvingSymlinksInPath().path
        // Only the new place has to exist now: another folder of the project
        // may be missing too, and is located on its own.
        try Self.validateRoots([replacement])
        let roots = workspace.roots.map { $0 == missing ? replacement : $0 }
        guard Set(roots).count == roots.count else { throw HostError.failure("That folder is already part of this project.") }
        if let other = workspaces.first(where: { $0.id != workspaceID && $0.path == replacement }) {
            throw HostError.failure("\(other.path) is already another project. Choose the folder this project was moved to.")
        }
        workspaceChangesInFlight.insert(workspaceID)
        defer { workspaceChangesInFlight.remove(workspaceID) }
        try await releaseIdleWorkspaceSessions(workspaceID)
        try await updateConfiguration { config in
            guard let index = config.workspaces.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == workspaceID }) else { throw HostError.failure("This project is no longer configured.") }
            if config.workspaces[index].path == missing { config.workspaces[index].path = replacement }
            else { config.workspaces[index].paths = config.workspaces[index].paths.map { $0 == missing ? replacement : $0 } }
        }
        missingProjectFolders.removeValue(forKey: workspaceID)
        try await restartWorkspaceHost(workspaceID)
        for chat in chats where chat.workspaceID == workspaceID { displays[chat.id]?.sendFailure = nil }
    }

    /// Asks where a moved project folder is now, then points the project there.
    func locateMissingFolder(_ workspaceID: String) async {
        guard let missing = missingProjectFolders[workspaceID] else { return }
        let name = URL(fileURLWithPath: missing).lastPathComponent
        guard let folder = await Self.chooseFolders(message: "Where is the folder “\(name)” now? It was at \(missing).", multiple: false).first else { return }
        do { try await relocateProjectFolder(workspaceID, from: missing, to: folder) }
        catch { self.error = error.localizedDescription }
    func retryConfiguration() {
        guard !configurationLoaded else { stopConfigurationRetry(); return }
        guard !configurationRetrying else { return }
        configurationRetrying = true
        let failure = error
        Task {
            defer { configurationRetrying = false }
            do {
                try await reloadConfiguration()
                stopConfigurationRetry()
                if error == failure { error = nil }
                // Launch chose these while there was nothing to choose from;
                // without them New Chat had no project or connection.
                if selectedWorkspaceID == nil { selectedWorkspaceID = workspaces.first?.id }
                if !profiles.contains(where: { $0.id == profileChoice }) { profileChoice = requestProfiles.first?.id ?? "" }
            } catch { }
        }
    }
    func stopConfigurationRetry() {
        if let configurationRetry { NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver(configurationRetry) }
        configurationRetry = nil
    }
    /// Trusted, tool-free home for chats that belong to no project. It lives in
    /// the app's own state directory, so no user folder is exposed.
    var scratchWorkspace: WorkspaceRecord {
        WorkspaceRecord(id: WorkspaceRecord.scratchID, path: root.appendingPathComponent("Scratch", isDirectory: true).path, trusted: true)
    }
    /// Configured projects plus the scratch workspace.
    func workspace(for id: String) -> WorkspaceRecord? {
        workspaces.first { $0.id == id } ?? (id == WorkspaceRecord.scratchID ? scratchWorkspace : nil)
    }
    func updateConfiguration(expectedRevision: Int64? = nil, _ change: @escaping @Sendable (inout VaultConfiguration) throws -> Void) async throws {
        try await ensureConfiguration()
        let saved = try await vault.update(expectedRevision: expectedRevision ?? configuration.revision, change)
        applyConfiguration(saved)
        // The vault write already committed. A failure migrating local chat
        // limits must not be reported as a save that did not happen: retrying
        // a route change would fork a second, duplicate connection.
        await finishConfiguration(saved)
    }
    /// Vault access can succeed after an initial startup failure. Migrate once
    /// when the matching connection is available, without replacing live state.
    private func migrateLegacyOutputBudgets() async throws {
        guard chats.contains(where: { $0.outputBudgetVersion == nil }), let store else { return }
        let saved = try await store.loadChats(profiles: profiles)
        for migrated in saved where migrated.outputBudgetVersion == 1 {
            guard let index = chats.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == migrated.id }), chats[index].outputBudgetVersion == nil else { continue }
            chats[index].maxOutputTokens = migrated.maxOutputTokens
            chats[index].modelOutputLimit = migrated.modelOutputLimit
            chats[index].outputBudgetVersion = migrated.outputBudgetVersion
        }
    }
    func credentials(for profile: ProfileRecord) async throws -> [String: WireValue] {
        // Recheck Keychain access when opening a connection. No per-profile,
        // shell, environment, Pi auth.json or plaintext fallback is consulted.
        let saved = try await vault.load()
import Foundation

/// Cold-open migration/repair reads only origin and accounting records. The
/// owner's journal receives numeric usage, never side conversation content.
enum SideBudgetRecovery {
    struct Origin {
        var id: String
        var parent: String
        var file: URL
    }
    static func parentExists(_ id: String, directory: URL) throws -> Bool {
        try journalURL(id,directory:directory) != nil
    }
    static func journalURL(_ id: String, directory: URL) throws -> URL? {
        let id = try identity(JSON(id))
        for name in [id, "side_" + id, "fork_" + id] {
            let file = canonical(directory.appendingPathComponent(name + ".jsonl").path)
            guard within(file,canonical(directory.path)) else { throw AttemptAccounting.damaged() }
            do {
                _ = try FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath:file.path)
                return file
            } catch let error as NSError where error.domain == NSCocoaErrorDomain && error.code == NSFileReadNoSuchFileError { }
        }
        return nil
    }

    static func unkeyedCharge(spend: SessionSpend, accounting: AttemptAccounting, side: String, parents: [String]) -> JSON? {
        var remainder = spend
        for entry in accounting.attempts.values {
            var counted = SessionSpend(); counted.add(record: entry.effective)
            remainder.usd -= counted.usd; remainder.reported -= counted.reported; remainder.unreported -= counted.unreported
        }
        if let amount = accounting.unkeyedUSD { remainder.usd = amount }
        guard remainder.reported > 0 || remainder.unreported > 0 || remainder.usd > 0.000000000001 else { return nil }
        var seed = remainder.record
        seed["usd"] = JSON(max(0,remainder.usd)); seed["attemptId"] = JSON("side-history:" + side)
        seed["source"] = "side-history"; seed["outcome"] = "completed"; seed["purpose"] = "legacy"
        return charge(seed,side:side,parents:parents)
    }
    static func charges(owner: String, directory: URL, family: [(origin: Origin, parents: [String])]? = nil) throws -> [AccountingRecord] {
        var result: [AccountingRecord] = []
        for (origin, parents) in try family ?? savedFamily(owner:owner,directory:directory) {
            // Only this family's ledger can affect opening its owner. An
            // unrelated side's damaged tail must not prevent other chats
            // opening; damage in a related ledger must still fail closed.
            let reader = try JournalRecordReader(origin.file)
            var accounting = AttemptAccounting(), remainder = SessionSpend(), children = SubagentJournalState()
            while let line = try reader.nextLine() {
                if line.isEmpty { continue }
                let record = try JSON.parse(line)
                try accounting.consumeRecord(record,sessionID:origin.id,spend:&remainder)
                try children.consume(record,sessionID:origin.id)
            }
            try recoverChildren(children,owner:origin.id,directory:directory,accounting:&accounting,spend:&remainder)
            for key in accounting.attempts.keys.sorted() {
                let entry = accounting.attempts[key]!
                if let record = record(entry,side:origin.id,parents:parents) { result.append(record) }
            }
            if let seed = unkeyedCharge(spend:remainder,accounting:accounting,side:origin.id,parents:parents) {
                result.append(AccountingRecord(kind:JournalRecordKind.cost,data:seed))
            }
        }
        return result
    }
    static func savedFamily(owner: String, directory: URL) throws -> [(origin: Origin, parents: [String])] {
        #if DEBUG
        SideOriginReadProbe.scan(directory)
        #endif
        let files = try FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(at: directory, includingPropertiesForKeys: nil)
        var origins: [String: Origin] = [:]
        for file in files where file.lastPathComponent.hasPrefix("side_") && file.pathExtension == "jsonl" {
            guard within(canonical(file.path), canonical(directory.path)) else { continue }
            // Membership is not established yet. An unreadable/incomplete
            // unrelated file must not block the workspace; opening that file
            // still reports its ordinary damaged-history/recovery error.
            // Once an origin links it to this owner, charges reads strictly.
            if let origin = try? readOrigin(file) { origins[origin.id] = origin }
        }
        var result: [(origin: Origin, parents: [String])] = []
        for origin in origins.values.sorted(by: { $0.id < $1.id }) {
            var parents: [String] = [], next = origin.parent, seen: Set<String> = [origin.id]
            while seen.insert(next).inserted, parents.count < 128 {
                parents.append(next)
                if next == owner { break }
                guard let parent = origins[next]?.parent else { break }
                next = parent
            }
            guard parents.last == owner else { continue }
            result.append((origin, parents))
        }
        return result
    }
    static func readOrigin(_ file: URL) throws -> Origin? {
        try SideOriginIndex.read(file)
    }
    static func charge(_ original: JSON, side: String, parents: [String]) -> JSON {
        // An allowlist is deliberate: even a malformed side cost record can
        // never copy arbitrary side content into the parent journal.
        var data = original.map.filter { ["attemptId","sourceSessionId","budgetOwnerSessionId","delegationId","purpose","outcome","usd","reported","unreported","usage","usageStatus","status","source","scope","sideParents","wallTimestamp"].contains($0.key) }
        if data["sourceSessionId"] == nil { data["sourceSessionId"] = JSON(side) }
        if data["budgetOwnerSessionId"] == nil { data["budgetOwnerSessionId"] = JSON(side) }
        if data["sideParents"] == nil { data["sideParents"] = .array(parents.map { JSON($0) }) }
        return .object(data)
    }
}

final class SideKeepRecoveryTests: XCTestCase {

    /// The helper's envelope and parent chain, including a promoted marker.
    static func journal(id: String, marker: Int = 3, tool: Bool = false, coordination: Bool = false) throws -> Data {
        var bytes = Data(), parent: String?
        func append(_ id: String, _ fields: [String: WireValue]) throws {
            var row = fields; row["id"] = .string(id); row["parentId"] = parent.map(WireValue.string) ?? .null
            let encoder = JSONEncoder(); encoder.outputFormatting = [.sortedKeys]
            bytes.append(try encoder.encode(row)); bytes.append(10); parent = id
        }
        try append(id, ["type": .string("session"), "version": .number(3)]); parent = nil
        try append("marker", ["type": .string("custom"), "customType": .string("pi-app.native.v\(marker)"), "data": .object(["version": .number(Double(marker))])])
        try append("question", ["type": .string("message"), "message": .object(["role": .string("user"), "content": .string("Generated question")])])
        if tool {
            try append("call", ["type": .string("message"), "message": .object(["role": .string("assistant"), "content": .array([.object(["type": .string("toolCall"), "id": .string("read-1"), "name": .string("read"), "arguments": .object(["path": .string("fixture.txt")])])])])])
        }
        if coordination {
            for kind in ["agent-operation", "agent-message", "agent-delivery", "subagent-turn-state", "subagent-turn-terminal", "family-run", "subagent-call"] {
                try append(kind, ["type": .string("custom"), "customType": .string("pi-app.\(kind).v1"), "data": .object([:])])
            }
            for author in ["parent", "subagent"] {
                try append(author, ["type": .string("message"), "message": .object([
                    "role": .string("user"), "content": .array([.object(["type": .string("text"), "text": .string("{\"kind\":\"agent_events\",\"version\":3,\"events\":[]}")])]),
                    "nativeKind": .string("agent_message"), "nativeAgentDelivery": .object(["version": .number(1), "author": .string(author), "childId": .string("child"), "turn": .number(1), "eventIds": .array([])])])])
            }
            try append("fork-context", ["type": .string("custom"), "customType": .string("pi-app.native.context.v1"), "data": .object(["ids": .array(["question", "parent", "subagent"].map(WireValue.string))])])
        }
        try append("state", ["type": .string("custom"), "customType": .string("pi-app.native.state.v1"), "data": .object(["active": .bool(tool), "queue": .array([]), "steering": .array([])])])
        return bytes
    }

    @MainActor @discardableResult private func save(_ bytes: Data?, id: String, in model: WorkspaceModel) async throws -> ChatRecord {
        let path = model.root.appendingPathComponent("\(id).jsonl")
        if let bytes { try bytes.write(to: path) }
        let chat = ChatRecord(id: id, workspaceID: "w", title: "Side \(id)", path: path.path, profileID: "p", toolMode: "read-only", parentSessionID: "parent")
        try await model.store?.put(SideKeepIntent(chat: chat), kind: "side-keep", id: id)
        return chat
    }

    @MainActor func testCutOffStreamingAndToolTailsRegisterAndOpenWithExistingRecoveryNotice() async throws {
        let model = try fixture()
        for tool in [false, true] {
            let id = tool ? "tool" : "streaming"
            var bytes = try Self.journal(id: id, tool: tool)
            bytes.append(Data("{\"type\":\"message\",\"id\":\"unfinished".utf8))
            let chat = try await save(bytes, id: id, in: model)
            await model.reconcileSideKeeps()
            XCTAssertNotNil(model.record(id), "A history notice is not an identity failure")
            let pending = try await model.store?.get(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep", id: id)
            XCTAssertNil(pending)
            await model.select(id)
            XCTAssertEqual(model.selected?.notice, "The last record of this chat was cut off while it was written. Everything before it is shown, read-only.")
            XCTAssertEqual(model.selected?.messages.first?.text, "Generated question")
            XCTAssertTrue(model.hosts.isEmpty)
            XCTAssertEqual(try Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: chat.path!)), bytes)
        }
    }

    }

    @MainActor func testInvalidIdentityIsRejectedWithoutChangingBytes() async throws {
        let model = try fixture()
        for (id, text) in [("wrong", "{\"type\":\"session\",\"version\":3,\"id\":\"other\"}\n"),
                           ("version", "{\"type\":\"session\",\"version\":4,\"id\":\"version\"}\n"),
                           ("missing", "{\"type\":\"message\"}\n"), ("cut-header", "{\"type\":\"session\""), ("unreadable", "not-json\n")] {
            let bytes = Data(text.utf8), chat = try await save(bytes, id: id, in: model)
            do { try await model.history.validateIdentity(path: chat.path!, id: chat.id); XCTFail("Invalid identity: \(id)") } catch {}
            XCTAssertEqual(try Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: chat.path!)), bytes)
        }
        await model.reconcileSideKeeps(); XCTAssertTrue(model.chats.isEmpty)
        let pending = try await model.store?.list(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep")
        XCTAssertEqual(pending?.count, 5)
        XCTAssertEqual(model.sideKeepIssues.count, 5)
        XCTAssertEqual(model.sideKeepIssues.first { $0.id == "wrong" }?.reason, "The file belongs to a different chat.")
        XCTAssertEqual(model.sideKeepIssues.first { $0.id == "version" }?.reason, "The file uses a conversation version this app cannot register.")
        XCTAssertEqual(model.sideKeepIssues.first { $0.id == "missing" }?.reason, "The file has no readable conversation header.")
    }

    @MainActor func testDismissRequiresPiConfirmationPreservesFilesAndDoesNotReturnAfterRelaunch() async throws {
        let model = try fixture(), bytes = try Self.journal(id: "other")

        window.isReleasedWhenClosed = false; defer { window.close() }
        var answer: ((Bool) -> Void)?, sheet: SideKeepDismissSheet?

        XCTAssertTrue(text.contains("file stays on disk")); XCTAssertTrue(text.contains(chat.title))
        answer?(false)
        var pending = try await model.store?.get(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep", id: chat.id)
        XCTAssertNotNil(pending); XCTAssertEqual(model.sideKeepIssues.count, 1)
        model.dismissSideKeep(issue, over: window); answer?(true)
        try await eventually("dismiss the reviewed copy") { model.sideKeepIssues.isEmpty }
        pending = try await model.store?.get(SideKeepIntent.self, kind: "side-keep", id: chat.id)
        XCTAssertNil(pending)
        let draft = try await model.store?.get(DraftRecord.self, kind: "draft", id: chat.id)
        XCTAssertEqual(draft?.text, "Unsent draft"); XCTAssertTrue(model.chats.isEmpty)
        XCTAssertEqual(try Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: chat.path!)), bytes)
        model.shutdown(); try await model.traces.close(); await model.store?.close()
        let reopened = makeWorkspaceModel(stateRoot: model.root)
        await reopened.restore()
        XCTAssertTrue(reopened.sideKeepIssues.isEmpty); XCTAssertNil(reopened.error)
        XCTAssertEqual(try Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: chat.path!)), bytes)
    }

    @MainActor func testArchivedCopyKeepsItsOrganizationAndMissingOrMovedFilesRemainActionable() async throws {
import Foundation

extension WorkspaceModel {
    /// Side panes and copied transcript views also need the cached child names.
    /// Only seed newly inserted displays; progress updates keep their identity.
    func seedSubagentNumbers(replacing previous: [String: SessionDisplay]) {
        let added = displays.filter { previous[$0.key] !== $0.value }
        guard !added.isEmpty else { return }
        let numbers = Dictionary(chats.compactMap { chat in chat.subagentNumber.map { (chat.id, $0) } }, uniquingKeysWith: { first, _ in first })
        for display in added.values {
            display.subagentNumbers.merge(numbers, uniquingKeysWith: { _, saved in saved })
            display.subagentEfforts = subagentEfforts
            display.subagentCosts = budgetStates.reduce(into: [:]) { result, entry in
                if record(entry.key)?.delegation?.budgetOwnerSessionID == display.id, entry.value.reading.spentUSD != nil {
                    result[entry.key] = SubagentCostPresentation(costReading(for:entry.key))
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Recover child metadata without starting a helper or resuming any run.
    /// The scan is actor-isolated; only validated small identities reach UI.
    func reconcileSubagents(parentID: String? = nil) async {
        guard store != nil else { return }
        let projects = parentID.flatMap { record($0)?.workspaceID }.map { Set([$0]) } ?? Set(workspaces.map(\.id) + chats.map(\.workspaceID))
        for project in projects where project != WorkspaceRecord.scratchID {
            do {
                let directory = try subagentDirectory(project)
                let found = try await history.discoverSubagents(directory: directory, parents: chats.filter { $0.workspaceID == project })
                guard !Task.isCancelled, !isShutDown else { return }
                for child in found where parentID == nil || child.header.link.budgetOwnerSessionID == parentID {
                    try await registerDiscoveredSubagent(child, workspaceID: project)
                }
            } catch is CancellationError { return }
            catch { self.error = "Sub-agent history could not be reconciled. " + error.localizedDescription }
        }
    }

    func registerDiscoveredSubagent(_ found: DiscoveredSubagent, workspaceID: String, live: SubagentSummary? = nil, connection: UUID? = nil) async throws {
        guard let store, !deletingFamilies.contains(found.header.link.budgetOwnerSessionID),
              let child = try await store.registerSubagent(found, workspaceID: workspaceID) else { return }
        guard !isShutDown, !deletingFamilies.contains(found.header.link.budgetOwnerSessionID) else { return }
        try Task.checkCancellation()
        if let connection, hosts[workspaceID]?.connectionID != connection { throw CancellationError() }
        publishSubagentMetadata(child)
        publishSubagentEffort(found.header.model, child: child)
        // No terminal row means only "interrupted if restored", not evidence
        // that an attached child has stopped. Do not discard live progress on
        // a topic move, archive/restore or repeated discovery.
        let previous = subagentSummaries[child.id]
        let retained = previous.flatMap { value in
            value.link == found.header.link && value.revision > 0 &&
                ((value.turn ?? 1) > (found.turn ?? 1) || ((value.turn ?? 1) == (found.turn ?? 1) && (!found.hasTerminalRecord || value.status == found.status))) ? value : nil
        }
        let summary = live ?? retained ?? SubagentSummary(link: found.header.link, description: found.header.description, model: found.header.model.model, status: found.status, revision: 0, turn: found.turn, executionID: found.executionID, effort: found.effort ?? found.header.model.thinkingLevel)
        if let live, let previous, !live.supersedes(previous) { return }
        if subagentSummaries[child.id] != summary { subagentSummaries[child.id] = summary }
        if live != nil { observeSubagentCost(summary) }
        if let owner = displays[summary.link.budgetOwnerSessionID], owner.childSummaries[child.id] != summary { owner.childSummaries[child.id] = summary }
        displays[child.id]?.isManagedSubagent = true
        displays[child.id]?.subagentStatus = summary.status
        displays[child.id]?.subagentTurn = summary.turn
        if let issue = child.delegationIssue { displays[child.id]?.notice = issue }
        // Terminal or newly created content is indexed by the normal search
        // scheduler. Streamed progress never reaches this registration path.
    }

    func publishSubagentMetadata(_ child: ChatRecord) {
        if let at = chats.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == child.id }) {
            let current = child.merged(over: chats[at])
            if chats[at] != current { chats[at] = current }
        }
        else { chats.append(child) }
        if let number = child.subagentNumber {
            for display in displays.values where display.subagentNumbers[child.id] != number {
                display.subagentNumbers[child.id] = number
            }
        }
    }

    func interruptSubagents(workspaceID: String) {
        for child in chats where child.workspaceID == workspaceID && child.isSubagent {
            guard var summary = subagentSummaries[child.id], !summary.status.terminal else { continue }
            summary.status = .interrupted; summary.revision = 0; summary.progress = nil; summary.progressEpoch = nil
            subagentSummaries[child.id] = summary; displays[child.id]?.subagentStatus = .interrupted
            displays[summary.link.budgetOwnerSessionID]?.childSummaries[child.id] = summary
        }
    }
}

import Foundation

@testable import PiApp

final class SubagentDeletionTests: XCTestCase {
    @MainActor func testSavedForkAndKeptSideFamiliesDeleteTheirJournalsAndSidecars() async throws {
        for prefix in ["fork_", "side_"] {
            let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
            let model = try SubagentOrganizationTests.fixture(root); registerWorkspaceFixtureTeardown(model, root: root)
            try await model.traces.configure(quota: nil, bodyRetention: 86_400, metricRetention: 86_400)
            model.chats.remove(at: 1)
            let family = try SubagentDiscoveryTests.family(model)
            let old = URL(fileURLWithPath: try XCTUnwrap(model.chats[0].path))
            let journal = old.deletingLastPathComponent().appendingPathComponent(prefix + old.lastPathComponent)
            try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: old, to: journal); model.chats[0].path = journal.path
            let parent = model.chats[0], store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
            try await store.putChats(model.chats)
            try await store.put(SideKeepRegistration(expected: parent, path: parent.path), kind: SideKeepRegistration.kind, id: parent.id)
            let files = [journal, family.path] + [".meta", ".stream"].map { URL(fileURLWithPath: journal.path + $0) }
            for file in files.dropFirst(2) { try Data("fixture".utf8).write(to: file) }
            model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) } }
            do { try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(parent) } catch { XCTFail("\(prefix) deletion failed: \(error)"); continue }
            XCTAssertTrue(files.allSatisfy { !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: $0.path) })
            XCTAssertNil(model.record(parent.id))
            let pending = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
            XCTAssertTrue(pending.isEmpty)
            let receipt = try await store.get(SideKeepRegistration.self, kind: SideKeepRegistration.kind, id: parent.id)
            XCTAssertNil(receipt)
        }
    }

    @MainActor func testPendingOwnerDeletionAcceptsLegacyNamesButChildDeletionKeepsUUIDNames() async throws {
        for child in [false, true] {
            for prefix in ["fork_", "side_"] {
                let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
                let model = try SubagentOrganizationTests.fixture(root); registerWorkspaceFixtureTeardown(model, root: root)
                try await model.traces.configure(quota: nil, bodyRetention: 86_400, metricRetention: 86_400)
                let directory = try model.subagentDirectory("pane-project")
                try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: directory, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
                let id = UUID().uuidString, path = directory.appendingPathComponent(prefix + id + ".jsonl")
                try Data("fixture".utf8).write(to: path)
                let store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
                let record = SessionDeletionRecord(id: id, ownerID: child ? "chat-parent" : id, workspaceID: "pane-project", path: path.path)
                let owner = SessionDeletionRecord(id: record.ownerID, ownerID: record.ownerID, workspaceID: record.workspaceID, path: nil)
                try await store.beginFamilyDeletion(child ? [owner, record] : [record])
                model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) } }
                await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
                let pending = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
                XCTAssertEqual(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: path.path), child)
                XCTAssertEqual(pending.isEmpty, !child)
                XCTAssertEqual(model.error != nil, child)
            }
        }
    }

    @MainActor func testDeleteFindsAnUnregisteredChildAndPreventsRediscoveryAndStaleWrites() async throws {
        let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
        let model = try SubagentOrganizationTests.fixture(root); registerWorkspaceFixtureTeardown(model, root: root)
        try await model.traces.configure(quota: nil, bodyRetention: 86_400, metricRetention: 86_400)
        model.chats.remove(at: 1)
        let family = try SubagentDiscoveryTests.family(model)
        let store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store), parent = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("chat-parent"))
        for chat in model.chats { try await store.put(chat, kind: "chat", id: chat.id) }
        for suffix in [".meta", ".origin", ".stream", ".stream-unrestored-fixture", ".promotion"] { try Data("fixture".utf8).write(to: URL(fileURLWithPath: family.path.path + suffix)) }
        let parentPromotion = URL(fileURLWithPath: try XCTUnwrap(parent.path) + ".promotion")
        try Data("private parent promotion fixture".utf8).write(to: parentPromotion)
        let temporary = family.path.deletingLastPathComponent().appendingPathComponent("." + family.path.lastPathComponent + ".meta." + UUID().uuidString)
        let unrelated = family.path.deletingLastPathComponent().appendingPathComponent(".another.jsonl.meta." + UUID().uuidString)
        for file in [temporary, unrelated] { try Data("private checkpoint fixture".utf8).write(to: file) }
        let outputs = family.path.deletingLastPathComponent().appendingPathComponent("tool-output")
        let ownedOutputs = [parent.id, family.child].map { outputs.appendingPathComponent($0) }
        let foreignOutputs = outputs.appendingPathComponent("foreign-chat")
        for folder in ownedOutputs + [foreignOutputs] {
            try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
            try Data("short private shell output".utf8).write(to: folder.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString + ".log"))
        }
        var recycled: [URL] = []
        model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in recycled += urls; for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) } }
        try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(parent)
        XCTAssertNil(model.record(parent.id)); XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: family.path.path))
        XCTAssertTrue(recycled.contains { $0.path == family.path.path + ".meta" })
        XCTAssertTrue(recycled.contains { $0.path == family.path.path + ".origin" })
        XCTAssertTrue(recycled.contains { $0.path == family.path.path + ".promotion" })
        XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: parentPromotion.path))
        XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: temporary.path))
        XCTAssertTrue(ownedOutputs.allSatisfy { !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: $0.path) })
        XCTAssertTrue(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: foreignOutputs.path))
        XCTAssertTrue(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: unrelated.path))
        XCTAssertEqual(model.record("chat-side")?.parentSessionID, parent.id, "Ordinary sides retain their existing independent deletion policy")
        do { try await store.put(parent, kind: "chat", id: parent.id); XCTFail("Stale parent recreated after deletion") } catch { }
        await model.reconcileSubagents()
        XCTAssertNil(model.record(family.child))
    }
    @MainActor func testPartialDeletionIsDurableAndRetriesWithoutNewAuthorization() async throws {
        let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
        let model = try SubagentOrganizationTests.fixture(root); registerWorkspaceFixtureTeardown(model, root: root)
        try await model.traces.configure(quota: nil, bodyRetention: 86_400, metricRetention: 86_400)
        model.chats.remove(at: 1)
        let family = try SubagentDiscoveryTests.family(model)
        let store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store), parent = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("chat-parent"))
        for chat in model.chats { try await store.put(chat, kind: "chat", id: chat.id) }
        model.familyFileRecycler = { _ in throw StoreError.unavailable }
        do { try await model.deleteSubagentFamily(parent); XCTFail("Injected filesystem failure ignored") } catch { }
        XCTAssertTrue(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: family.path.path))
        let intents = try await store.list(SessionDeletionRecord.self, kind: SessionDeletionRecord.kind)
        XCTAssertEqual(Set(intents.map(\.id)), Set([parent.id, family.child]))
        await model.stopHostsAndWait(); model.shutdown()
        try await model.traces.close(); await store.close()
        // A real relaunch retains the project's configuration. The recovery
        // barrier can start its helper without opening any conversation.
        let reopened = WorkspaceModel(stateRoot: model.root, vault: model.vault)
        defer { reopened.shutdown() }
        addTeardownBlock { @MainActor in await reopened.stopHostsAndWait() }
        let nextStore = try XCTUnwrap(reopened.store)
        reopened.chats = try await nextStore.loadChats()
        try await reopened.traces.configure(quota: nil, bodyRetention: 86_400, metricRetention: 86_400)
        reopened.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) } }
        await reopened.resumeFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: family.path.path))
        let saved = try await nextStore.get(ChatRecord.self, kind: "chat", id: parent.id)
        XCTAssertNil(saved); XCTAssertNil(reopened.record(parent.id))
    }
    @MainActor func testUnreadableDirectoryListingKeepsTheIntentEvenWhenKnownPathsCanBeInspected() async throws {
        let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
        let model = try SubagentOrganizationTests.fixture(root); registerWorkspaceFixtureTeardown(model, root: root)
        try await model.traces.configure(quota: nil, bodyRetention: 86_400, metricRetention: 86_400)
        let directory = try model.subagentDirectory("pane-project")
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: directory, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let parent = model.chats[0], path = directory.appendingPathComponent(parent.id + ".jsonl"), store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
        try Data("private fixture".utf8).write(to: path)
        try await store.putChats(model.chats)
        try await store.beginFamilyDeletion([SessionDeletionRecord(id: parent.id, ownerID: parent.id, workspaceID: parent.workspaceID, path: path.path)])
        model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) } }
        // Search permission permits stat of known paths; read permission is
        // needed only to enumerate. This isolates the listing failure guard.
        try FileManager.default.setAttributes([.posixPermissions:0o100], ofItemAtPath: directory.path)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.setAttributes([.posixPermissions:0o700], ofItemAtPath: directory.path) }
        await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
        let pending = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertEqual(pending.count, 1); XCTAssertNotNil(model.record(parent.id)); XCTAssertNotNil(model.error)
        try FileManager.default.setAttributes([.posixPermissions:0o700], ofItemAtPath: directory.path)
        await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
        let remaining = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertTrue(remaining.isEmpty); XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: path.path))
    }

    @MainActor func testEnumerationFailureKeepsDeletionIntentAndRetriesAfterRepair() async throws {
        let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
        let model = try SubagentOrganizationTests.fixture(root); registerWorkspaceFixtureTeardown(model, root: root)
        try await model.traces.configure(quota: nil, bodyRetention: 86_400, metricRetention: 86_400)
        model.chats.remove(at: 1)
        let family = try SubagentDiscoveryTests.family(model), store = try XCTUnwrap(model.store)
        let parent = try XCTUnwrap(model.record("chat-parent")), directory = family.path.deletingLastPathComponent()
        try await store.putChats(model.chats)
        let members = [SessionDeletionRecord(id: parent.id, ownerID: parent.id, workspaceID: parent.workspaceID, path: parent.path),
                       SessionDeletionRecord(id: family.child, ownerID: parent.id, workspaceID: parent.workspaceID, path: family.path.path)]
        try await store.beginFamilyDeletion(members)
        let saved = root.appendingPathComponent("saved-sessions")
        try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: directory, to: saved)
        try Data("enumeration must fail with ENOTDIR".utf8).write(to: directory)
        model.familyFileRecycler = { urls in for url in urls { try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) } }
        await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
        let pending = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertEqual(Set(pending.map(\.id)), Set(members.map(\.id)))
        XCTAssertNotNil(model.record(parent.id)); XCTAssertNotNil(model.error)
        try FileManager.default.removeItem(at: directory)
        try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: saved, to: directory)
        await model.resumeFamilyDeletions()
        let finished = try await store.pendingFamilyDeletions()
        XCTAssertTrue(finished.isEmpty); XCTAssertFalse(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: family.path.path))
    }

    func testLateCaptureCannotRecreateDeletedFamilyGroupsOrDeleteAnotherWorkspace() async throws {
        let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: root) }
        let archive = PayloadArchive(root: root)
        try await archive.configure(quota: nil, bodyRetention: 86_400, metricRetention: 86_400)
        let summary = SubagentSummary(link: SubagentTranscriptTests.link, description: "Review", model: "model", status: .running, revision: 1)
        for workspace in ["one", "two"] { try await archive.recordSubagent(summary, workspaceID: workspace) }
        try await archive.forgetSubagentRelationships(sessionID: "chat-parent", workspaceID: "one")
        try await archive.recordSubagent(summary, workspaceID: "one")
        let deleted = try await archive.inspectorSubagents(owner: "chat-parent", workspaceID: "one")
        let retained = try await archive.inspectorSubagents(owner: "chat-parent", workspaceID: "two")
        XCTAssertTrue(deleted.isEmpty); XCTAssertEqual(retained.count, 1)
        try await archive.close()
    }

import AppKit

/// One custom question's lifetime, including AppKit's closing animation.
/// Late or repeated answers cannot act again. Closing the parent cancels.
@MainActor final class PiQuestionPresentation {
    private var content: NSView?
    private var sheet: PiSheetWindow?
    private var result: Bool?
    private var finished = false
    private var parentObserver: NSObjectProtocol?
    var onFinished: ((Bool) -> Void)?

    init(make: (@escaping (Bool) -> Void) -> NSView) {
        content = make { [weak self] in self?.respond($0) }
    }

    func present(on parent: NSWindow, using presenter: ((NSView, NSWindow, @escaping (Bool) -> Void) -> Void)?) {
        guard let content else { return }
        if let presenter {
            presenter(content, parent) { [weak self] in self?.respond($0) }
            return
        }
        let sheet = PiSheetWindow(content: content,
            inherited: PiSheetWindowInherited(reduceMotion: parent.contentView?.piReducesMotion ?? false, enabled: true),
            close: { [weak self] in self?.respond(false) })
        self.sheet = sheet
        // An owner can go away during the closing animation. The sheet
        // retains its presentation until release; finish breaks this cycle.
        sheet.onEnded = { [self] _ in finish() }
        parentObserver = NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(forName: NSWindow.willCloseNotification, object: parent, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            MainActor.assumeIsolated { self?.cancel() }
        }
        sheet.present(on: parent)
    }

    func respond(_ accepted: Bool) {
        guard result == nil, !finished else { return }
        result = accepted
        if let sheet { sheet.end(animated: true, requested: true) }
        else { finish() }
    }

    /// The owner/window going away revokes an answer even while the sheet is
    /// sliding closed. No action has been dispatched until finish calls back.
    func cancel() {
        guard !finished else { return }
        result = false
        if let sheet { sheet.end(animated: false, requested: true) }
        else { finish() }
    }

    private func finish() {
        guard !finished else { return }
        if let parentObserver { NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver(parentObserver) }
        parentObserver = nil
        finished = true; content = nil; sheet = nil
        let completed = onFinished; onFinished = nil
        completed?(result ?? false)
    }
}
import AppKit
import QuartzCore

extension PiKit {
    /// A sheet's chrome: a header (a badge, the title, a quieter subtitle
    /// and the actions) on the window's cream, a hairline, the content on the
    /// content canvas, and an optional footer under another hairline.
    ///
    /// Escape leaves the sheet — `onCancel` when it has unsaved work to ask
    /// about, else `dismiss` — unless `cancelDisabled` holds it back during a
    /// write. In a window of its own (`windowChrome`) the header is the title
    /// bar: it leaves room for the window buttons, and Escape is the window's.
    @MainActor final class Sheet: NSView {
        static let trafficLightInset: CGFloat = 78
        let title: String
        let subtitle: String?
        let symbol: String?
        let windowChrome: Bool
        let content: NSView
        let actions: [NSView]
        let footer: NSView?
        var cancelDisabled = false
        var onCancel: (@MainActor () -> Void)?
        var dismiss: (@MainActor () -> Void)?
        /// Fixed or least sizes, as the SwiftUI sheet's frames.
        var width: CGFloat?, height: CGFloat?, minWidth: CGFloat?, minHeight: CGFloat?

        private let header = Box.ClipView(), body = Box.ClipView(), foot = Box.ClipView()
        private let titleView: TextLine
        private let subtitleView: SelectableText?
        private let badge: IconBadge?
        private let topLine = CALayer(), bottomLine = CALayer()
        private let windowBar = PiWindowBarView(frame: .zero)

        init(_ title: String, subtitle: String? = nil, symbol: String? = nil, windowChrome: Bool = false, content: NSView,
             actions: [NSView] = [], footer: NSView? = nil) {
            self.title = title; self.subtitle = subtitle; self.symbol = symbol; self.windowChrome = windowChrome
            self.content = content; self.actions = actions; self.footer = footer
            titleView = TextLine(Line(title, font: PiKit.Font.title(17), color: .piInk))
            subtitleView = subtitle.map { text in
                // Selectable, at most two lines, cut at the end (`lineLimit(2)`).
                let field = SelectableText(text, font: PiKit.Font.caption, color: .piInkSecondary)
                field.maximumNumberOfLines = 2
                field.cell?.truncatesLastVisibleLine = true
                return field
            }
            badge = symbol.map { IconBadge(symbol: $0, size: 30) }
            super.init(frame: .zero)
            wantsLayer = true
            for view in [header, body, foot] { view.wantsLayer = true; addSubview(view) }
            // In a window of its own the header is the title bar: the window's
            // drag area and chrome lie behind it, as `PiWindowBar` gives the SwiftUI sheet.
            if windowChrome { header.addSubview(windowBar) }
            for view in [badge, titleView, subtitleView].compactMap({ $0 }) as [NSView] { header.addSubview(view) }
            for action in actions { header.addSubview(action) }
            body.addSubview(content)
            if let footer { foot.addSubview(footer) } else { foot.isHidden = true }
            layer?.addSublayer(topLine); layer?.addSublayer(bottomLine)
            bottomLine.isHidden = footer == nil
        }
        required init?(coder: NSCoder) { fatalError("Not used from a nib") }
        override var isFlipped: Bool { true }

        private var leading: CGFloat { windowChrome ? Self.trafficLightInset : PiSpacing.xl }
        /// The subtitle's lines in `room`, at most two.
        private func subtitleHeight(_ room: CGFloat) -> CGFloat {
            guard let subtitleView, let subtitle else { return 0 }
            return min(subtitleView.height(forWidth: room), Line(subtitle, font: PiKit.Font.caption, color: .black).lineHeight * 2)
        }
        /// The room the title and subtitle have in a header `width` wide: what
        /// the badge and the actions leave, less the `Spacer()` between them
        /// (its least length, 8, and the stack's spacing on its far side).
        private func textRoom(_ width: CGFloat) -> CGFloat {
            var room = width - leading - PiSpacing.xl - (badge == nil ? 0 : 30 + PiSpacing.md)
            for action in actions { room -= action.fittingSize.width + PiSpacing.md }
            return max(0, room - PiSpacing.md - 8)
        }
        private var headerHeight: CGFloat {
            let text = titleView.intrinsicContentSize.height + (subtitleView == nil ? 0 : 3 + subtitleHeight(textRoom(sheetWidth)))
            let tallest = max(text, badge == nil ? 0 : 30, actions.map(\.fittingSize.height).max() ?? 0)
            return tallest + (windowChrome ? PiSpacing.md : PiSpacing.lg) + PiSpacing.lg
        }
        private var footerHeight: CGFloat {
            footer.map { PiKit.height(of: $0, width: max(0, sheetWidth - PiSpacing.xl * 2)) + PiSpacing.md * 2 + 1 } ?? 0
        }
        /// The width it is, or will be once laid out.
        private var sheetWidth: CGFloat {
            bounds.width > 0 ? bounds.width : width ?? max(minWidth ?? 0, content.fittingSize.width)
        }
        override var intrinsicContentSize: NSSize {
            let content = self.content.fittingSize
            return NSSize(width: width ?? max(minWidth ?? 0, content.width),
                          height: height ?? max(minHeight ?? 0, headerHeight + 1 + content.height + footerHeight))
        }

        override func layout() {
            super.layout()
            let headerHeight = self.headerHeight
            header.frame = CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: bounds.width, height: headerHeight)
            windowBar.frame = header.bounds
            let top = windowChrome ? PiSpacing.md : PiSpacing.lg
            let inner = headerHeight - top - PiSpacing.lg
            var x = leading
            if let badge { badge.frame = CGRect(x: x, y: top + (inner - 30) / 2, width: 30, height: 30); x += 30 + PiSpacing.md }
            var right = bounds.width - PiSpacing.xl
            for action in actions.reversed() {
                let size = action.fittingSize
                action.frame = CGRect(x: right - size.width, y: top + PiKit.round((inner - size.height) / 2, piScale), width: size.width, height: size.height)
                right -= size.width + PiSpacing.md
            }
            let room = textRoom(bounds.width)
            let textHeight = titleView.intrinsicContentSize.height + (subtitleView == nil ? 0 : 3 + subtitleHeight(room))
            var y = top + PiKit.round((inner - textHeight) / 2, piScale)
            titleView.frame = CGRect(x: x, y: y, width: min(titleView.intrinsicContentSize.width, right - x), height: titleView.intrinsicContentSize.height)
            y += titleView.frame.height + 3
            if let subtitleView {
                // A field's cell insets its text two points; the field sits that far out.
                subtitleView.frame = CGRect(x: x - PiKit.fieldInset, y: y, width: room + PiKit.fieldInset * 2, height: subtitleHeight(room))
            }
            let footerHeight = self.footerHeight
            body.frame = CGRect(x: 0, y: headerHeight + 1, width: bounds.width, height: max(0, bounds.height - headerHeight - 1 - footerHeight))
            content.frame = body.bounds
            if let footer {
                foot.frame = CGRect(x: 0, y: bounds.height - footerHeight + 1, width: bounds.width, height: footerHeight - 1)
                // A footer narrower than the sheet sits in its middle, as a
                // SwiftUI footer does; a flexible one fills it.
                let room = foot.bounds.insetBy(dx: PiSpacing.xl, dy: PiSpacing.md)
                let natural = footer.intrinsicContentSize.width
                if natural != NSView.noIntrinsicMetric, natural < room.width {
                    footer.frame = CGRect(x: PiKit.round(room.midX - natural / 2, piScale), y: room.minY, width: natural, height: room.height)
                } else {
                    footer.frame = room
                }
            }
            CATransaction.begin(); CATransaction.setDisableActions(true)
            topLine.frame = CGRect(x: 0, y: headerHeight, width: bounds.width, height: 1)
            bottomLine.frame = CGRect(x: 0, y: bounds.height - footerHeight, width: bounds.width, height: 1)
            CATransaction.commit()
            updateLayer()
        }
        override var wantsUpdateLayer: Bool { true }
        override func updateLayer() {
            layer?.backgroundColor = piCGColor(.piWindow)
            header.layer?.backgroundColor = piCGColor(.piWindow)
            foot.layer?.backgroundColor = piCGColor(.piWindow)
            body.layer?.backgroundColor = piCGColor(.piContent)
            topLine.backgroundColor = piCGColor(.piHairline); bottomLine.backgroundColor = piCGColor(.piHairline)
        }
        override func viewDidMoveToWindow() {
            super.viewDidMoveToWindow()
            // The header badge pops in once.
            if window != nil, let badge, !piReducesMotion, let layer = badge.layer {
                let pop = Motion.pop("transform.scale"); pop.fromValue = 0.6; pop.toValue = 1
                pop.beginTime = CACurrentMediaTime() + 0.08; pop.fillMode = .backwards
                let fade = CABasicAnimation(keyPath: "opacity"); fade.fromValue = 0; fade.toValue = 1
                fade.duration = Motion.quick; fade.beginTime = pop.beginTime; fade.fillMode = .backwards
                layer.add(pop, forKey: "pop"); layer.add(fade, forKey: "fade")
            }
        }
